1.1.11 sāndrānandāvabodhātmakamanupamitaṃ kāladeśāvadhibhyāṃ
1.1.12 nirmuktaṃ nityamuktaṃ nigamaśatasahasreṇa nirbhāsyamānam |
1.1.13 aspaṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭamātre punarurupuruṣārthātmakaṃ brahma tattvaṃ
1.1.14 tattāvadbhāti sākṣādgurupavanapure hanta bhāgyaṃ janānām ||
1.1.21 evaṃ durlabhyavastunyapi sulabhatayā hastalabdhe yadanyat
1.1.22 tanvā vācā dhiyā vā bhajati bata janaḥ kṣudrataiva sphuṭeyam |
1.1.23 ete tāvadvayaṃ tu sthirataramanasā viśvapīḍāpahatyai
1.1.24 niśśeṣātmānamenaṃ gurupavanapurādhīśamevāśrayāmaḥ ||
1.1.31 sattvaṃ yattatpurābhyāmaparikalanato mirmalaṃ tena tāvad
1.1.32 bhūtairbhūtenidrayaiste vapuriti bahuśaḥ śrūyate vyāsavākyam |
1.1.33 tatsvacchatvādyadacchāditaparasukhacidjarbhanirbhāsarūpaṃ
1.1.34 tasmin dhanyā ramante śrutimatimadhure sugrahe vigrahe te ||
1.1.41 niṣkampe nityapūrṇe niravadhi paramānandapīyūṣarūpe
1.1.42 nirlīnānekamuktāvalisubhagatame nirmalabrahmasindhau |
1.1.43 kallolollāsatulyaṃ khalu vimalataraṃ sattvamāhustadātmā
1.1.44 kasmānno niṣkalastvaṃ sakala iti vacastvatkalāsveva bhūman! ||
1.1.51 nirvyāpāro'pi niṣkāraṇamaja! bhajase yatkriyāmīkṣaṇākhyāṃ
1.1.52 tenaivodeti līnā prakṛtirasatikalpāpi kalpādikāle |
1.1.53 tasyāḥ saṃśuddhamaṃśaṃ kamapi tamatirodhāyakaṃ sattvarūpaṃ
1.1.54 sa tvaṃ dhṛtvā dadhāsi svamahimavibhavākuṇṭha! vaikuṇṭḥ! rūpam ||
1.1.61 tatte pratyagradhārādharalalitakalāyāvalīkelikāraṃ
1.1.62 lāvaṇyasyaikasāraṃ sukṛtijanadṛśāṃ pūrṇapuṇyāvatāram |
1.1.63 lakṣmīniśśaṅkalīlānilayanamamṛtasyandohamantaḥ
1.1.64 siñcatsañcintakānāṃ vapuranukalaye mārutāgāranātha! ||
1.1.71 kaṣtā te sṛṣṭiceṣṭā bahutarabhavakhedāvahā jīvabhājāmityevaṃ
1.1.72 pūrvamālocitamajita! mayā naivamadyābhijāne |
1.1.73 no cejjīvāḥ kathaṃ vā sadhurataramidaṃ tvadvapuścidrasārdraṃ
1.1.74 netraiḥ śrotraiśca pītvā paramarasasudhāmbhodhipūre rameran ||
1.1.81 namrāṇāṃ sannidhatte satatamapi purastairanabhyārthitānapyarthān
1.1.82 kāmānajasraṃ vitarati paramānandasāndrāṃ gatiṃ ca |
1.1.83 itthaṃ niśśeṣalabhyo niravadhikaphalaḥ pārijāto hare! tvaṃ
1.1.84 kṣudraṃ taṃ śakravāṭīdrumamabhilaṣati vyarthamarthivrajo'yam ||
1.1.91 kāruṇyātkāmamanyaṃ dadati khalu pare svātmadastvaṃ viśeṣā
1.1.92 daiśvaryādīśate'nye jagati parajane svātmano'pīśvarastvam |
1.1.93 tvayyuccairāramanti pratipadamadhure cetanāḥ sphītabhāgyāstvaṃ
1.1.94 cātmārāmevetyatulaguṇagaṇādhāra! śaure! namaste ||
1.1.101 eśvaryaṃ śaṅkarādīśvaraviniyamanaṃ viśvatejoharāṇāṃ
1.1.102 tejassaṃhāri vīryaṃ vimalamapi yaśo nispṛhaiścopagītam |
1.1.103 aṅgāsaṅgā sadā śrīrakhilavidasi na kvāpi te saṅgavārtā
1.1.104 tadvātāgāravāsin! murahara! bhagavacchabdamukhyāśrayo'si ||
1.2.11 sūryaspardhikirīṭamūrdhvatilakaprodbhāsiphālāntaraṃ
1.2.12 kāruṇyākulanetramārdrahasitollāsaṃ sunāsāpuṭam |
1.2.13 gaṇḍodyanmakarābhakuṇḍalayugaṃ kaṇṭhojjvalatkaustubhaṃ
1.2.14 tvadrūpaṃ vanamālyahīrapaṭalaśrīvatsadīpraṃ bhaje ||
1.2.21 keyūrāṅgadakaṅkaṇottamamahāratnāṅgulīyāṅkitaśrīmadbāhucatuṣkasaṅgatagadāśaṅkhāripaṅkeruhām |
1.2.22 kāñcitkāñcinakāñcilācchitalasatpītāmbarālambinīmālambe vimalāmbujadyutipadāṃ mūrtiṃ tavārticchidam ||
1.2.31 yattrailokyamahīyaso'pi mahitaṃ sammohanaṃ mohanāt
1.2.32 kāntaṃ kāntinidhānato'pi madhuraṃ mādhuryadhuryādapi |
1.2.33 saundaryottarato'pi sundarataraṃ tvadrūpamāścaryato'pyāścaryaṃ
1.2.34 bhuvane na kasya kutukaṃ puṣṇāti viṣṇo! vibho! ||
1.2.41 tattādṛṅmadhurātmakaṃ tava vapuḥ samprāpya sampanmayī
1.2.42 sā devī paramotsukā cirataraṃ nāste svabhakteṣvapi |
1.2.43 tenāsyā bata kaṣṭamacyut! vibho! tvadrūpamānojñakapremasthairyamayādacāpalabalāccāpalyavārtodabhūt ||
1.2.51 lakṣmīstāvakarāmaṇīyakahṛtaiveyaṃ pareṣvasthiret
1.2.52 yasminnanyadapi pramāṇamadhunā vakṣyāmi lakṣmīpate! |
1.2.53 ye tvaddhynaguṇānukīrtanarasāsaktā hi bhaktā janās
1.2.54 teṣveṣā vasati sthiraiva dayitaprastāvadattādarā ||
1.2.61 evambhūtamanojñatānavasudhāniṣyandasandohanaṃ
1.2.62 tvadrūpaṃ paracidrasāyanamayaṃ cetoharaṃ śṛṇvatām |
1.2.63 sadyaḥ prerayate matiṃ madayate romāñcayatyṅgakaṃ
1.2.64 vyāsiñcatyapi śītabāṣpavisarairānandamūrcchodbhavaiḥ ||
1.2.71 evambhūtatayā hi bhaktyabhihito yogaḥ sa yogadvayat
1.2.72 karmajñānamayādbhṛśottamataro yogīśvarairgīyate |
1.2.73 saundaryaikarasātmake tvayi khalu premaprakarṣātmikā
1.2.74 bhaktirniḥśramameva viśvapuruṣairlabhyā ramāvallam! ||
1.2.81 niṣkāmaṃ niyatasvadharmacaraṇaṃ yatkarmayogābhidhaṃ
1.2.82 taddūretyaphalaṃ yadaupaniṣadajñānopalabhyaṃ punaḥ |
1.2.83 tattvavyaktatayā sudurgamataraṃ cittasya tasmādvibho!
1.2.84 tvatpremātmakabhaktireva satataṃ svadīyasī śreyasī ||
1.2.91 atyāyāsakarāṇi karmapaṭalānyācarya niryanmalā
1.2.92 bodhe bhaktipathe'thavāpyuvitatāmāyānti kiṃ tāvatā |
1.2.93 kliṣṭvā tarkapathe paraṃ tava vapurbrahmākhyamanye punaś
1.2.94 cittārdratvamṛte vicintya bahubhiḥ sidhyanti janmāntaraiḥ ||
1.2.101 tvadbhaktistu kathārasāmṛtajñarīnirmajjanena svayaṃ
1.2.102 sidhyanti vimalapraṣodhapadavīmakleśatastanvatī |
1.2.103 sadyaḥ siddhikarī jayatyayi vibho! saivāstu me tvatpada
1.2.104 premaprauḍhirasārdratā drutataraṃ vātālayādhīśvara! ||
1.3.11 paṭhanto nāmāni pramadabharasindhau nipatitāḥ
1.3.12 smaranto rūpaṃ te varada! kaṭhayanto guṇakaṭhāḥ |
1.3.13 caranto ye bhaktāstvapi khalu ramante paramamu
1.3.14 nahaṃ dhanyānmanye samadhigatasarvābhilaṣitān ||
1.3.21 gadakliṣṭaṃ kaṣṭaṃ tava caraṇasevārasabhare
1.3.22'pyanāsaktaṃ cittaṃ bhavati bata viṣṇo! kuru dayām |
1.3.23 bhavatpādāmbhojasmaraṇarasiko nāmanivahā
1.3.24 nahaṃ gāyaṃgāyaṃ kuhacana vivatsyāmi vijane ||
1.3.31 kṛpā te jātā cetkimiva na hi labhyaṃ tanubhṛtāṃ
1.3.32 madīyakleśaughapraśamanadaśā nāma kiyatī |
1.3.33 na ke ke loke'sminnaniśamayi śokābhirahitā
1.3.34 bhavadbhaktā muktāḥ sukhagatimasaktā vidadhate ||
1.3.41 muniprauḍhā rūḍhā jagati khalu gūḍhātmagatayo
1.3.42 bhavatpādāmbhojasmaraṇavirujo nāradmukhāḥ |
1.3.43 carantīś! svairaṃ satataparinirbhātaparacit
1.3.44 sadānandādvaitaprasaraparimagnāḥ kimaparam ||
1.3.51 bhavadbhaktiḥ sphītā bhavatu mama saiva praśamaye
1.3.52 daśeṣakleśaughaṃ na khalu hṛdi sandehakaṇikā |
1.3.53 na cedvyāsasyoktistava ca vacanaṃ naigamavaco
1.3.54 bhavenmithyā rathyāpuruṣavacanaprāyamakhilam ||
1.3.61 bhavadbhaktistāvatpramukhamadhurā tvādguṇarasāt
1.3.62 mimapyārūḍhā cedakhilaparitāpapraśamanī |
1.3.63 pūnaścānte svānte vimalapari bodhodayamilan
1.3.64 mahānandādvaitaṃ diśati kimataḥ prārthyamaparam ||
1.3.71 vidhūya kleśānme kuru caraṇayugmaṃ dhṛtarasaṃ
1.3.72 bhavatkṣetraprāptau karamapi ca te pūjanavidhau |
1.3.73 bhavanmūrtyāloke nayanamatha te pādatulasī
1.3.74 parighrāṇe ghrāṇaṃ śravaṇamapi te cārucarite ||
1.3.81 prabhūtādhivyādhiprasabhavalite māmakahṛdi
1.3.82 tvadīyaṃ tadrūpaṃ paramarasacidrūpamudiyāt |
1.3.83 udañcadromāñco galitabahuharṣāśrūnivaho
1.3.84 yathā vismaryāsaṃ durupaśamapīḍāparibhavan ||
1.3.91 marudgehādhīś! tvayi khalu parāñco'pi sukhino
1.3.92 bhavatsnehī so'haṃ subahu paritapye ca kimidam |
1.3.93 akīrtiste mā bhūdvarada! gadabhāraṃ praśamayan
1.3.94 bhavatbhaktottaṃsaṃ jhaṭiti kuru māṃ kaṃsadamana! ||
1.3.101 kimuktairbhūyobhistava hi karuṇā yāvadudiyādahaṃ
1.3.102 tāvaddeva! prahitavividhārtapralapitaḥ |
1.3.103 puraḥ klṛpte pāde varada! tava neṣyāmi divasān
1.3.104 yathāśakti vyaktaṃ natinutiniṣevā viracayan ||

2.4.11 kalyatāṃ mama kuruṣva tāvatīṃ kalyate bhavadupāsanaṃ yayā |
2.4.12 spaṣṭamaṣṭavidhayogacaryayā puṣṭayāśu tava tuṣṭimāpnuyām ||
2.4.21 bhrahmacaryadṛḍhatādibhiryamairāplvādiniyamaiśca pāvitāḥ |
2.4.22 kurmahe dṛḍhamamī sukhāsanaṃ paṅkajādyamapi vā bhavatparāḥ ||
2.4.31 tāramantaranucintya santataṃ prāṇavāyumabhiyamya nirmalāḥ |
2.4.32 indriyāṇi viṣayādathāpahṛtyāsmahe bhavadupāsanonmukhāḥ ||
2.4.41 asphuṭe vapuṣi prayatnato dhārayema dhiṣaṇāṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
2.4.42 tena bhaktirasamantarādratāmudvahema bhavadaṅghricintakāḥ ||
2.4.51 visphuṭāvayavabhedasundaraṃ tvadvapuḥ suciraśīlanāvaśāt |
2.4.52 aśramaṃ manasi cintayāmahe dhyānayoganiratāstvadāśrayāḥ ||
2.4.61 dhyāyatāṃ sakalamūrtimīdṛśīmunmiṣanmadhuratāhṛtātmanām |
2.4.62 sāndramodarasarūpamāntaraṃ brahma rūpamayi! te'vibhāsate ||
2.4.71 tatsamāsvadanarūpiṇīṃ sthitiṃ tvatsamādhimayi viśvanāyaka! |
2.4.72 āśritāḥ punarataḥ paricyutāvārabhemahi ca dhāraṇādikam ||
2.4.81 itthamabhyasananirbharollasattvatparātmasukhakalpitotsavāḥ |
2.4.82 muktabhaktakulamaulitāṃ gatāḥ sañcarema śukanāradādivat ||
2.4.91 tvatsamādhivijaye tu yaḥ punarmaṅkṣu mokṣarasikaḥ krameṇa vā |ṇ
2.4.92 yogavaśyamanilaṃ ṣaḍāśrayairunnayatyaja! suṣumṇayā śanaiḥ ||
2.4.101 liṅgadehamapi santyajannatho līyate tvayi pare nirāgrahaḥ |
2.4.102 ūrdhvalokakutukī tu mūrdhataḥ sārdhameva karaṇairnirīyate ||
2.4.111 agnivāsaravalarkṣapakṣagairuttarāyaṇajuṣā ca daivataiḥ |
2.4.112 prāpito ravipadaṃ bhavatparo modavān dhruvapadāntamīyate ||
2.4.121 āsthito'tha maharālaye yadā śeṣavaktradahanoṣmaṇārdyate |
2.4.122 īyate bhavadupāśrayastadā vedhasaḥ padamataḥ puraiva vā ||
2.4.131 tatra vā tava pade'thavā vasan prākṛtapralaya eti muktatām |
2.4.132 svecchayā khalu purāpi mucyate saṃcibhidya jagadaṇḍamojasā ||
2.4.141 tasya ca kṣitipayomahoniladyomahatprakṛtisaptakāvṛtī |
2.4.142 tattadātmakatayā viśan sukhī yāti te padamanāvṛtaṃ vibho! ||
2.4.151 arcirādigatimīdṛśīṃ vrajan vicyutiṃ na bhajate jagatpate! |
2.4.152 saccidītmaka! bhavadguṇodayānuccarantamanileśa! pāhi mām ||
2.5.11 vyaktāvyaktamidaṃ na kiñcidabhavatprākprākṛtaprakṣaye
2.5.12 māyāyāṃ guṇasāmyaruddhavikṛtau tvayyāgatāyāṃ layam |
2.5.13 no mṛtyuśca tadāmṛtaṃ ca samabhūnnāhno na rātreḥ sthiti
2.5.14 statraikastvamaśiṣyathāḥ kila parānandaprakāśātmanā ||
2.5.21 kālaḥ karma guṇāśca jīvanivahā viśvaṃ ca kāryaṃ vibho!
2.5.22 cillīlāratimeyuṣi tvayi tadā nirlīnatāmāyayuḥ |
2.5.23 teṣāṃ naiva vadantyasattvamayi bhoḥ! śaktyātmanā tiṣṭhatām
2.5.24 no cetkiṃ gaganaprasunasadṛśāṃ bhūyo bhavetsambhavaḥ ||
2.5.31 evañca dviparārdhakālavigatāvīkṣāṃ sisṛkṣātmikāṃ
2.5.32 bibhrāṇe tvayi cukṣume tribhuvanībhāvāya māyā svayam |
2.5.33 māyātaḥ khalu kālaśaktirakhilādṛṣṭaṃ svabhāvo'pi ca
2.5.34 prādurbhūya guṇān vikāsya vidadhustasyāḥ sahāyakriyām ||
2.5.41 māyāsannihito'praviṣṭavapuṣā sākṣīti gīto bhavān
2.5.42 bhedaistāṃ pratibimbato viviśivān jīvo'pi naivāparaḥ |
2.5.43 kālādipratibodhitātha bhavatā sañcoditā ca svayaṃ
2.5.44 māyā sā khalu buddhitattvamasṛjadyo'sau mahānucyate ||
2.5.51 tatrāsau triguṇātmako'pi ca mahān sattvapradhānaḥ svayaṃ
2.5.52 jīve'smin khalu nirvikalpamahamityudbodhaniṣpādakaḥ |
2.5.53 cakre' smin savikalpabodhakamahantattvaṃ mahān khalvasu
2.5.54 saṃpuṣṭaṃ triguṇaistamotibahulaṃ viṣṇo! bhavatpreraṇāt ||
2.5.61 so'haṃ ca triguṇakramāttrividhatāmāsādyavaikāriko
2.5.62 bhūyastaijasatāmasāviti bhavannādyena sattvātmanā |
2.5.63 devānidriyamānino'kṛta diśāvātārkapāśyaśvino
2.5.64 vahnīndrācyutamitrakān vidhuvidhiśrīrudraśārīrakān ||
2.5.71 bhūman! mānasabuddhyahaṅkṛtimilaccittākhyavṛttyanvitaṃ
2.5.72 taccāntaḥ karaṇaṃ vibho! tava balātsattvāṃśevāsṛjat |
2.5.73 jātastaijasato daśendriyagaṇastattāmasāṃśātpuna
2.5.74 stanmātraṃ nabhaso marutpurapate! śabdo'jani tvadbalāt ||
2.5.81 śabdādvyāma tataḥ sasarjitha vibho! sparśaṃ tato mārutaṃ
2.5.82 tasmādrūpamato maho'thaca rasaṃ toyaṃ gandhaṃ mahīm |
2.5.83 evaṃ mādhava! pūrvapūrvakalanādādyādyadharmānvitaṃ
2.5.84 bhūtagrāmamimaṃ tvameva bhagavan! prākāśayastāmasāt ||
2.5.91 ete bhūtagaṇāstathendriyagaṇā devāśca jātāḥ pṛthaṅ
2.5.92 no śekurbhuvanāṇḍanirmitividhādevairamībhistadā |
2.5.93 tvaṃ nānāvidhasūktibhirnutaguṇastattvānyamūnyāviśaṃ
2.5.94 śceṣṭāśaktimudīrya tāni baṭayan hairaṇyamaṇḍaṃ vyadhāḥ ||
2.5.101 aṇḍaṃ tatkhalu pūrvasṛṣṭasalile'tiṣṭhatsahasraṃ samā
2.5.102 nirbindannakṛthāścaturdaśajagadrūpaṃ virāḍāhvayam |
2.5.103 sāhasraiḥ karapādamūrdhanīvahairniśśeṣajīvātmako
2.5.104 nirbhāto'si marutpurādhipa! sa māṃ trāyasva sarvāmayāt ||
2.6.11 evaṃ caturdaśajaganmayatāṃ gatasya
2.6.12 pātālamīśa! tava pādatalaṃ vadanti |
2.6.13 pādordhvadeśamapi deva! rasātalaṃ ta
2.6.14 gulphadvayaṃ khalu mahātalamadbhutātman ||
2.6.21 jaṅghe talātalamatho sutalaṃ ca jān
2.6.22 kiñcorubhāgayugalaṃ vitalātele dve |
2.6.23 kṣoṇītalaṃ jaghanamambaramaṅga! nābhir
2.6.24 vakṣaśca śakranilayastava cakrepāṇe! ||
2.6.31 grīvā mahastava mukhaṃ ca janastapastu
2.6.32 phālaṃ śirastava samastamayasya satyam |
2.6.33 evaṃ jaganmayantayo! jagadāśrītaira
2.6.34 pyanyairnibaddhavapuṣe bhagavan! namaste ||
2.6.41 tvadbrahmarandhrapadamīśvara! viśvakanda
2.6.42 cchandāṃsi keśaṣ! dhanāstava keśapāśāḥ |
2.6.43 ullāsicilliyugalaṃ druhiṇasya gehaṃ
2.6.44 pakṣmāṇi rātridivasau savitā ca netre ||
2.6.51 niśśeṣaviśvaracanā ca kaṭākṣamokṣaḥ
2.6.52 karṇau diśo'śviyugalaṃ tava nāsike dve |
2.6.53 lobhatrape ca bhagavannadharottaroṣṭhau
2.6.54 tārāgaṇāśca radanāḥ śamanaśca daṃṣṭrā ||
2.6.61 māyā vilāsahasitaṃ śvasitaṃ samīro
2.6.62 jihvā jalaṃ vacanamīś! śakuntapaṅktiḥ |
2.6.63 siddhādayaḥ svaragaṇā mukharandhramagnir
2.6.64 devā bhujāḥ stanayugaṃ tava dharmadevaḥ ||
2.6.71 pṛṣṭhaṃ tvadharmaiha deva! manaḥ sudhāṃśu
2.6.72 ravyaktameva hṛdayāmbujamamnujākṣa! |
2.6.73 kukṣiḥ samudranivahā vasanaṃ tu sandhye
2.6.74 śephaḥ prajāpatirasau vṛṣṇau ca mitraḥ ||
2.6.81 śroṇīsthalaṃ mṛgagaṇāḥ padayornakhāste
2.6.82 hastyṣṭrasaindhavamukhā gamanaṃ tu kālaḥ |
2.6.83 viprādivarṇabhavanaṃ vadanābjabāhu
2.6.84 cārūruyugmacaraṇaṃkaruṇāmbudhe! te ||
2.6.91 saṃsāracakramāyi cakradhara! kriyāste
2.6.92 vīryaṃ mahāsuragaṇo'sthikulāni śailāḥ |
2.6.93 nāḍyaḥ saritsamudayāstaravaśca roma
2.6.94 jīyādidaṃ vapuranirvacanīyamīś! ||
2.6.101 īdṛgjaganmayavapustava karmabhājāṃ
2.6.102 karmāvasānasamaye smaraṇīyamāhuḥ |
2.6.103 tasyāntarātmavapuṣe vimalātmane te
2.6.104 vātālayādhipa! namo'stu nirundhi rogān ||
2.6.105 evaṃ deva! caturdaśātmakajagadrūpeṇa jātaḥ punas
2.6.106 tasyodharvaṃ khalu satyalokanikalaye jāto'si dhātā svayam |
2.6.107 yaṃ śaṃsanti hiraṇyagarbhamakhilatrailokyajīvātmakaṃ
2.6.108 yo'bhūtsphītarajovikāravikasannānāsisṛkṣārasaḥ ||
2.7.21 so'yaṃ viśvavisargadattahṛdayaḥ saṃpaśyamānaḥ svayaṃ
2.7.22 bodhaṃ khalvanavāpya viśvaviṣayaṃ hi cintākulastasthivān |
2.7.23 tāvattvaṃ jagatāṃ pate! tapa tapetyevaṃ hi vaihāyasīṃ
2.7.24 vāṇīmenamaśiśravaḥ śrutisukhāṃ kurvaṃstapaḥpreraṇām ||
2.7.31 ko'sau māmavadatpumāniti jalāpūrṇe jaganmaṇḍale
2.7.32 dikṣūdvīkṣya kimapyanīkṣitavatā vākyārthamutpaśyatā |
2.7.33 divyaṃ varṣasahasramāttatapasā tena tvamārādhitas
2.7.34 tasmai darśitavānasi svanilayaṃ vaikuṇṭhamekādbhutam ||
2.7.41 māyā yatra kadāpi no vikurute bhāte jagadbhyate bahiḥ
2.7.42 śokakrodhavimohasādhvasamukhā bhāvāstu dūraṃ gatāḥ |
2.7.43 sāndrānandajharī ca yatra paramajyotiḥ prakāśātmake
2.7.44 tatte dhāma vibhāvitaṃ vijayate vaikuṇṭharūpaṃ vibho! ||
2.7.51 yasminnāma caturbhujā harimaṇiśyāmāvadātatviṣo
2.7.52 nānābhūṣaṇaratnadīpitadiśo rājadvimānālayāḥ |
2.7.53 bhaktiprāptatathāvidhonnatapadā dīvyanti divyo janās
2.7.54 tatte dhāma nirastasarvaśamalaṃ vaikuṇṭharūpaṃ jayet ||
2.7.61 nānādivyavadhūjanairabhivṛtā vidyullatātulyayā
2.7.62 viśvonmādanahṛdyagātralatayā vidyotitāśāntarā |
2.7.63 tvatpādāmbujasauramaikakutukāllakṣmīḥ svayaṃ lakṣyate
2.7.64 yasmin vismayanīyadivyavibhavā tatte oadaṃ dehi me ||
2.7.71 tatraivaṃ pratidarśite nijapade ratnāsanādhyāsitaṃ
2.7.72 bhāsvatkoṭilasatkirīṭakaṭakādyākalpadīprākṛti |
2.7.73 śrīvatsāṅkitamāttakaustubhaṇicchāyāruṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ
2.7.74 viśveṣāṃ tava rūpamaikṣata vidhistatte vibho! bhātu me ||
2.7.81 kālāmbhodakalāyakomalarucī cakreṇa cakraṃ diśā
2.7.82 māvṛṇvānamudāramandahasitasyandaprasannānanam |
2.7.83 rājatkambugadāripaṅkajadharaśrīmadbhujāmaṇḍalaṃ
2.7.84 sraṣṭustuṣṭikaraṃ vapustava vibho! madrogamudvāsayet ||
2.7.91 dṛṣṭvā sambhṛtasambhramaḥ kamalabhūstvatpādapāthoruhe
2.7.92 harṣāveśavaśaṃvado nipatitaḥ prītyā kṛtārthībhavan |
2.7.93 jānāsyeva manīṣitaṃ mama vibho! jñānaṃ tadāpādaya
2.7.94 dvaitādvaitabhavatsvarūpaparamityācaṣṭa taṃ tvāṃ bhaje ||
2.7.101 ātāmre caraṇe vinamramatha taṃ hastena haste spṛśan
2.7.102 bodhaste bhavitā na sargavidhibhirbando'pi sañjāyate |
2.7.103 ityābhāṣya giraṃ pratoṣyta nitarāṃ taccittagūḍhaḥ svayaṃ
2.7.104 sṛṣṭau taṃ samudairayaḥ sa bhagavannullāsayollāghatām ||

3.8.11 evaṃ tāvatprākṛtaprakṣayānte brāhme kalpe hyādime labdhajanmā |
3.8.12 brahmā bhūyastvatta evāpya vedān sṛṣṭiṃ cakre pūrvakalpopamānām ||
3.8.21 so'yaṃ caturyugasahasramitānyahāni
3.8.22 tāvanmitāśca rajanīrbahuśo nināya |
3.8.23 nidrātyasau tvayi nilīya samaṃ svasṛṣṭair
3.8.24 naimittikapralayamāhurato'sya rātrim ||
3.8.31 asmādṛśāṃ punaraharmukhakṛtyatulyāṃ
3.8.32 sṛṣṭiṃ karotyanudinaṃ sa bhavatprasādāt |
3.8.33 prāgbrāhmakalpajanuṣāṃ ca parāyuṣāṃ tu
3.8.34 suptaprabodhanasamāsti tadā visṛṣṭiḥ ||
3.8.41 pañcāśadabdamadhunā svavayordharūpam
3.8.42 ekaṃ parārdhamativṛtya hi vartate'sau |
3.8.43 tatrāntyarātrijanitān kathayāmi bhūman!
3.8.44 paścāddināvataraṇe ca bhavadvilāsān ||
3.8.51 dināvasāne'tha sarojayoniḥ suṣuptikāmastvayi sannililye |
3.8.52 jaganti ca tvajjaṭharaṃ samīyustadedamekārṇavamāsa viśvam ||
3.8.61 tavaiva veṣe phaṇirāji śeṣe jalaikaśeṣe bhuvane sma śeṣe |
3.8.62 ānandasāndrānubhavasvarūpaḥ svayoganidrāparimudritātmā ||
3.8.71 kālākhyaśaktiṃ pralayāvasāne prabodhayetyādiśatā kilādau |
3.8.72 tvayā prasuptaṃ parisuptaśaktivrajena tatrākhilajīvadhāmnā ||
3.8.81 caturyugāṇāṃ ca sahasramevaṃ tvayi prasupte punaradvitīye |
3.8.82 kālākhyaśaktiḥ prathamaprabuddhā prāvodhayattvāṃ kila viśvanātha ||
3.8.91 vibudhya ca tvaṃ jalagarbhaśāyin! vilokya lokānakhilān pralīnān |
3.8.92 teṣveva sūkṣmātmatayā nijāntaḥ sthiteṣu viśveṣu dadātha dṛṣṭim ||
3.8.101 tatastvadīyādayi! nābhirandhrādudañcitaṃ kiñcana divyapadmam |
3.8.102 nilīnaniśśeṣapadārthamālāsaṅkṣeparūpaṃ mukulāyamānam ||
3.8.111 tadetadambhoruhakuḍmalaṃ te kalebarāttoyapathe prarūḍḥm |
3.8.112 bahirnirītaṃ paritaḥ sphuradbhiḥ svadhāmabhirdhvāntamalaṃ nyakṛntat ||
3.8.121 saṃphullapatre nitarāṃ vicitre tasmin bhavadvīryadhṛte saroje |
3.8.122 sa padmajanmā vidhirāvirāsītsvayaṃprabuddhākhilavedarāśiḥ ||
3.8.131 asmin parātman! nanu pādmakalpe tvamitthamutthāpitapadmayoniḥ |
3.8.132 anantabhumā mama rogarāśiṃ nirundhi vātālayavās! viṣṇo! ||
3.9.11 sthitaḥ sa kamalodbhavastava hi nābhipaṅkeruhe
3.9.12 kutaḥ svididamambudhāvuditamityanālokaayan |
3.9.13 tadīkṣaṇakutūhalātpratidiśaṃ vivṛttānanaś
3.9.14 caturvadanatāmagādvikasadaṣṭadṛṣṭyambujām ||
3.9.21 mahārṇavavighūrṇtaṃ kamalameva tatkevalaṃ
3.9.22 vilokya tadupāśrayaṃ tava tanuṃ tu nālokayan |
3.9.23 ka eṣa kamalodare mahati nissahāyo hyahaṃ
3.9.24 kutaḥ svididamambujaṃ samajanīti cintāmagāt ||
3.9.31 amuṣya hi saroruhaḥ kimapi kāraṇaṃ sambhaved
3.9.32 itisma kṛtaniścayaḥ sa khalu nālarandhrādhvanā |
3.9.33 sayogabalavidyayā samavarūḍhavān prauḍhadhīs
3.9.34 tvadīyamatimohanaṃ na tu kalebaraṃ dṛṣṭavān ||
3.9.41 tataḥ sakalanālikāvivaramārgago mārgayan
3.9.42 praysya śatavatsaraṃ kimapi naiva sandṛṣṭavān |
3.9.43 nivṛtya kamalodare sukhaniṣaṇṇa ekāgradhīḥ
3.9.44 samādhibalamādadhe bhavadanugrahaikāgrahī ||
3.9.51 śatena parivatsarairdṛḍhasamādhibandhollasat
3.9.52 prabodhaviśdīkṛtaḥ sa khalu padminīsambhavaḥ |
3.9.53 adṛṣṭacaramadbhutaṃ tava hi rūpamantardṛśā
3.9.54 vyacaṣṭa parituṣṭadhīrbhujagabhogabhāgāśrayam ||
3.9.61 kirīṭamakṛṭollasatkaṭakahārakeyūrayug
3.9.62 maṇisphuritamekhalaṃ suparivītapītāmbaram |
3.9.63 kalāyakusumaprabhaṃ galatalollasatkaustubhaṃ
3.9.64 vapustadayi! bhāvaye kamalajanmane darśitam ||
3.9.71 śrutiprakaradarśitapracuravaibhava! śrīpate!
3.9.72 hare! jaya jaya prabho! padamupaiṣi diṣṭyā dṛśoḥ |
3.9.73 kuruṣva dhiyamāśu me bhuvananirmitau karmaṭhām
3.9.74 iti druhiṇavarṇitasvaguṇabaṃhimā pāhi mām ||
3.9.81 labhasva bhuvanatrayīracanadakṣatāmakṣatāṃ
3.9.82 gṛhāṇa madanugrahaṃ kuru tapaśca bhūyo vidhe! |
3.9.83 bhavatvakhilasādhanī mayi ca bhaktiratyutkaṭe
3.9.84 tyudīrya giramādadhā muditacetasaṃ vedhasam ||
3.9.91 śataṃ kṛtatapāstataḥ sa khalu divyasaṃvatsarā
3.9.92 navāpya ca tapobalaṃ matibalaṃ ca pūrvādhikam |
3.9.93 udīkṣya kila kampitaṃ payasi paṅkajaṃ vāyunā
3.9.94 bhavatbalavijṛmbhitaḥ pavanapāthasī pītavān ||
3.9.101 tavaiva kṛpayā punaḥ sarasijena tenaiva sa
3.9.102 prakalpya bhuvanatrayīṃ pravavṛte prajānirmitau |
3.9.103 tathāvidhakṛpābharo gurumarutpurādhīśvara!
3.9.104 tvamāśu paripāhi māṃ gurudayokṣitairīkṣitaiḥ ||
3.10.11 vaikuṇṭḥ! vardhitabalo'tha bhavatprasādā
3.10.12 dambhojayonirasṛjatkila jīvadehān |
3.10.13 sthāsnūni bhūruhamayāni tathā tiraścāṃ
3.10.14 jātīrmanuṣyanivahānapi devabhedān ||
3.10.21 mithyāgrahāsmimatirāgavikopabhītir
3.10.22 ajñānavṛttimiti pañcavidhāṃ sa sṛṣṭvā |
3.10.23 uddāmatāmasapadārthavidhānadūnas
3.10.24 tene tvadīyacaraṇasmaraṇaṃ viśuddhyai ||
3.10.31 tāvatsasarja manasā sanakaṃ sanandaṃ
3.10.32 bhūyaṃ sanātanamuniṃ ca sanatkumāram |
3.10.33 te sṛṣṭikarmaṇi tu tena niyujyamānās
3.10.34 tvatpādabhaktirasikā jagṛhurna vāṇīm ||
3.10.41 tāvatprakopamuditaṃ pratirundhato'sya
3.10.42 bhrūmadhyato'jani mṛḍo bhavadekadeśaḥ |
3.10.43 nāmāni me kuru padāni ca hā viriñcet
3.10.44 yadau rurodkila tena sa rudranāmā ||
3.10.51 ekādaśāhvayatayā ca vibhinnarūpaṃ
3.10.52 rudraṃ vidhāya dayitā vanitāśca dattvā |
3.10.53 tāvantyadatta ca padāni bhavatpraṇunnaḥ
3.10.54 prāha prajāviracanāya ca sadāraṃ tam ||
3.10.61 rudrābhisṛṣtabhayadākṛtirudrasaṅgha
3.10.62 saṃpūryamāṇābhuvanatrayabhītacetāḥ |
3.10.63 mā mā prajāḥ sṛja tapaścara maṅgalāyet
3.10.64 yācaṣṭa taṃ kamalabhūrbhavadīritātmā ||
3.10.71 tasyātha sargarasikasya marīciratris
3.10.72 tatrāṅgirāḥ kratuminiḥ pulahaḥ pulastyaḥ |
3.10.73 aṅgādajayata bhṛguśca vasiṣṭhadakṣau
3.10.74 śrīnāradaśca bhagavān bhavadaṅghridāsaḥ ||
3.10.81 dharmādikānabhsṛjannatha kardamaṃ ca
3.10.82 vāṇīṃ vidhāya vidhiraṅgajasaṅkulo'bhūt |
3.10.83 tvadbodhitaiḥ sanakadakṣamukhaistanūjair
3.10.84 udbodhitaśca virarāma tamo vimuñcan ||
3.10.91 devān purāṇanivahānapi sarvavidyāḥ
3.10.92 kurvannijānanagaṇāccaturānano'sau |
3.10.93 putreṣu teṣu vinidhāya sa sargavṛddhim
3.10.94 aprāpnuvaṃstava padāmbujamāśrito'bhūt ||
3.10.101 jānannupāyamatha dehamajo vibhajya
3.10.102 strīpuṃsabhāvamabhajanmanutadvadhūbhyām |
3.10.103 tābhyāṃ ca mānuṣakulāni vivardhayaṃstvaṃ
3.10.104 govinda! mārutapurādhipa! rundhi rogān ||
3.11.11 krameṇa sarge parivardhamāne kadāpi divyāḥ sanakādayaste |
3.11.12 bhavadvilokāya vikuṇṭhalokaṃ prapedire mārutamandireśa! ||
3.11.21 manojñanaiḥ śreyasakānanādyairanekavāpamiṇimandiraiśca |
3.11.22 anopamaṃ taṃ bhavato niketaṃ munīśvarāḥ prāpuratītakakṣyāḥ ||
3.11.31 bhavaddidṛkṣūn bhavanaṃ vivikṣūn dvāḥsthau jayastān vijayo'pyarundhām |
3.11.32 teṣāṃ ca citte padamāpa kopaḥ sarvaṃ bhavatpreraṇayaiva bhūman ||
3.11.41 vaikuṇṭhalokānucitapraceṣtau kaṣṭau yuvāṃ daityagatiṃ bhajetam |
3.11.42 iti praśaptau bhavadāśrayau tau harismṛtirno'stviti nematustān ||
3.11.51 tedetadājñāya bhavānavāptaḥ sahaiva lakṣmyā bahirambujākṣa! |
3.11.52 khageśvarāṃsārpitacārubāhurānandayaṃstīnabhirāmamūrtyā ||
3.11.61 prasādya gīrbhiḥ stuvato munīndrānananyanāthāvatha pārṣadau tau |
3.11.62 saṃrambhayogena bhavaistribhirmāmupetamityāttakṛpāṃ nyagādīḥ ||
3.11.71 tvadīyabhṛtyau kila kāśyapāttau surārivīrāvuditau ditau dvau |
3.11.72 sandhyāsamutpādanakaṣṭaceṣtau yamau ca lokasya yamāvivāyau ||
3.11.81 hiraṇyapūrvaḥ kaśipuḥ kilaikaḥ puro hiraṇyākṣa iti pratītaḥ |
3.11.82 ubhau bhavannāthamaśeṣalokaṃ ruṣā nyarundhāṃ nijavāsanāndhau ||
3.11.91 tayorhiraṇyākṣamahāsurendro raṇāya dhāvannanavāptavairī |
3.11.92 bhavatpriyāṃ kṣmāṃ salile nimajjya cacāra garvādvinadan gadāvān ||
3.11.101 tato jaleśātsadṛśaṃ bhavantaṃ niśamya babhrāma gaveṣayaṃstvām |
3.11.102 bhaktaikadṛśyaḥ sa kṛpānidhe! tvaṃ nirundhi rogānmarudālayeśa! ||
3.12.11 svāyambhuvo manuratho janasargaśīlo
3.12.12 dṛṣṭvā mahīmasamaye salile nimaghnām |ṣ
3.12.13 sraṣṭāramāpa śaraṇaṃ bhavadaṅghrisevā
3.12.14 tuṣṭāśayaṃ munijanaiḥ saha satyaloke ||
3.12.21 kaṣṭaṃ prajāḥ sṛjati mayyavanī nimagnā
3.12.22 sthānaṃ sarojabhava! kalpaya tatprajānām |
3.12.23 ityevameṣa kathito manuni svayambhū
3.12.24 rambhoruhākṣa! tava pādayugaṃ vyacintīt ||
3.12.31 hā hā vibho! jalamahaṃ nyapibaṃ purastād
3.12.32 adyāpi majjati mahī kimahaṃ karomi |
3.12.33 itthaṃ tvadaṅghriyugalaṃ śaraṇaṃ yato'sya
3.12.34 nāsāpuṭātsamabhavaḥ śiśukolarūpī ||
3.12.41 aṅguṣṭḥmātravapurutpatitaḥ purastād
3.12.42 bhūyo'tha kumbhisadṛśaḥ samajṛmbhathāstvam |
3.12.43 abhre tathāvidhamudīkṣya bhavantamuccair
3.12.44 vismeratāṃ vidhiragātsaha sūnubhiḥ svaiḥ ||
3.12.51 ko'sāvacintyamahimā kiṭirutthito me
3.12.52 ghoṇāpuṭātkimu bhavedajitasya māyā |
3.12.53 itthaṃ vicintayati dhātariśailamātraḥ
3.12.54 sadyo bhavan kila jagarjitha ghoraghoram ||
3.12.61 taṃ te ninādamupakarṇya janastapaḥsthāḥ
3.12.62 satyasthitāśca munayo nunuvurbhavantam |
3.12.63 tatstotraharṣulamanāḥ pariṇadya bhūyas
3.12.64 toyāśayaṃ vipulamūrtiravātarastvam ||
3.12.71 ūrdhvaprasāriparidhūmrāvidhūtaromā
3.12.72 protkṣiptavāladhiravāṅmukhaghoraghoṇaḥ |
3.12.73 tūrṇapradīrṇajaladaḥ parighūrṇadakṣṇā
3.12.74 stotnmunīcchiśirayannavateritha tvam ||
3.12.81 antarjalaṃ tadanu saṅkulanakracakraṃ
3.12.82 bhrāmyattimiṅgilakulaṃ kaluṣormimālam |
3.12.83 āviśya bhīṣaṇaraveṇa rasātalasthā
3.12.84 nākampayan vasumatīmagaveṣayastvam ||
3.12.91 dṛṣṭvātha daityahatakena rasātalānte
3.12.92 saṃveśitāṃ jhaṭiti kūṭakiṭirvibho! tvam |
3.12.93 āpātukānavigaṇayya surārikheṭān
3.12.94 daṃṣṭrāṅkureṇa vasudhāmadadhāḥ salīlam ||
3.12.101 abhyuddharannatha dharāṃ daśanāgralagna
3.12.102 mustāṅkurāṅkita ivādhikapīvarātmā |
3.12.103 uddhātaghorasalilājjaladherudañcan
3.12.104 ktīḍāvarāhavapurīśvara! pāhi rogāt ||
3.13.11 hiraṇyākṣaṃ tāvadvarada! bhavadanveṣaṇaparaṃ
3.13.12 carantaṃ sāṃvarte payasi nijajaṅghāparimite |
3.13.13 bhavadbhukto gatvā kapaṭapaṭudhīrnāradamuniḥ
3.13.14 śanairūce nandan danujamapi nindaṃstava balam ||
3.13.21 sa māyāvī viṣṇurharati bhavadīyaṃ vasumatīṃ
3.13.22 prabho! kaṣṭaṃ kaṣṭaṃ kimidamiti tenābhigaditaḥ |
3.13.23 nadan kvāsau kvāsāviti sa muninā darśitaptho
3.13.24 bhavantaṃ saṃprāpaddharaṇidharamudyantamudakāt ||
3.13.31 aho āraṇyo'yaṃ mṛga iti hasantaṃ bahutarair
3.13.32 duruktairvidhyantaṃ ditisutamavajñāya bhagavan! |
3.13.33 mahīṃ dṛṣṭvā daṃṣṭrāśirasi cakitāṃ svena mahasā
3.13.34 payodhāvādhāya prasabhamudayuṅkthā mṛdhavidhau ||
3.13.41 gadāpāṇau daitye tvamapi hi gṛhītonnatagado
3.13.42 niyuddhena krīḍan ghaṭaghaṭaravodghuṣṭaviyatā |
3.13.43 raṇālokaitsukyānmilati surasaṅghe drutamamuṃ
3.13.44 nirundhyāḥ sandhyātaḥ prathamamiti dhātrā jagadiṣe ||
3.13.51 gadonmarde tasmiṃstava khalu gadāyāṃ ditibhuvo
3.13.52 gadāghātādbhūmau jhaṭiti patitāyāmahaha bhoḥ! |
3.13.53 mṛdusmerāsyastvaṃ danujakulanirmūlanacaṇaṃ
3.13.54 mahācakraṃ smṛtvā karabhuvi dadhāno ruruciṣe ||
3.13.61 tataḥ śūlaṃ kālapratimaruṣi daitye visṛjati
3.13.62 tvayicchindatyenatkarakalitacakrapraharaṇāt |
3.13.63 samāruṣṭo muṣṭyā sa khalu vitudaṃstvāṃ samatamod
3.13.64 galanmāye māyāstvayi kila jaganmohanakarīḥ ||
3.13.71 bhavaccakrajyotiṣkaṇalavanipātena vidhute
3.13.72 tato māyācakre vitataghanaroṣāndhamanasam |
3.13.73 gariṣṭhābhirmuṣṭiprahṛtibhirabhighnantamasuraṃ
3.13.74 karāgreṇa svena śravaṇapadamūle niravadhīḥ ||
3.13.81 mahākāyaḥ so'yaṃ tava karasarojapramathito
3.13.82 galadrakto vaktrādapatadṛṣibhiḥ ślāghitahatiḥ |
3.13.83 tadā tvāmudāmapramadabharavidyotihṛdayā
3.13.84 munīndrāḥ sāndrābhiḥ stutibhiranuvannadhvaratanam ||
3.13.91 tvaci cchando romasvapi kuśagaṇaścakṣuṣi ghṛtaṃ
3.13.92 caturhotāro'ṅghrau srugapi vadane codara iḍā |
3.13.93 grahā jihvāyāṃ te parapuruṣa! karṇe ca camasā
3.13.94 vibho! somo vīryaṃ varada! galadeśe'pyupasandaḥ ||
3.13.101 munīndrairityādistavanamukharairmoditamanā
3.13.102 mahīyasyā mūrtyā vimalatarakīrtyā ca vilasan |
3.13.103 svadhiṣṇyaṃ saṃprāptaḥ sukharasavihārī madhuripo!
3.13.104 nirundhyā rogaṃ me sakalamapi vātālayapate! ||
3.14.11 samanusmṛtatāvakāṅghriyugmaḥ sa manuḥ paṅkajasambhavāṅgajanmā |
3.14.12 nijamantaramantarāyahīnaṃ caritaṃ te kathayan sukhaṃ nināya ||
3.14.21 samaye khalu tatra kardamākhyo druhiṇacchāyabhavastadīyavācā |
3.14.22 ghṛtasargaraso nisargaramyaṃ bhagavaṃstvāmayutaṃ samāḥ siṣeve ||
3.14.31 garuḍopari kālameghakamraṃ vilasatkelisarojapāṇipadmam |
3.14.32 hasitollasitānanaṃ vibho! tvaṃ vapurāviṣkuruṣe sma kardamāya ||
3.14.41 stuvate pulakāvṛtāya tasmai manuputrīṃ dayitāṃ navāpi putrīḥ |
3.14.42 kapilaṃ ca sutaṃ svameva paścātsvagatiṃ cāpyanugṛhya nirgato'bhūḥ ||
3.14.51 sa manuḥ śatarūpayā mahiṣyā guṇavatyā sutayā ca devahūtyā |
3.14.52 bhavadīritanāradopadiṣṭaḥ samagātkardamamāgatipratīkṣam ||
3.14.61 manunopahṛtāṃ ca devahūtiṃ taruṇīratnamavāpya kardamo'sau |
3.14.62 bhavadarcananirvṛto'pi tasyāṃ dṛḍhaśuśrūṣaṇayā dadhau prasādam ||
3.14.71 sapunastvadupāsanaprabhāvāddayitākāmakṛte kṛte vimāne |
3.14.72 vanitākulasaṅkule navātmā vyaharaddevapatheṣu devahūtyā ||
3.14.81 śatavarṣamatha vyatītya so'yaṃ nava kanyāḥ samavāpya dhanyarūpāḥ |
3.14.82 vanayānasamudyato'pi kāntāhitakṛttvajjananotsuko nyavātsīt ||
3.14.91 nijabhartṛgirā bhavanniṣevāniratāyāmatha deva! devahūtyām |
3.14.92 kapilastvamajāyathā janānāṃ prathayiṣyan paramātmatattvavidyām ||
3.14.101 vanameyuṣi kardame prasanne matasarvasvamupādiśañjananyai |
3.14.102 kapilātmaka! vāyumadireś! tvaritaṃ tvaṃ paripāhi māṃ gadaughāt ||
3.15.11 matiriha guṇasaktā bandhakṛtteṣvasaktā
3.15.12 tvamṛtakṛduparundhe bhaktiyogastu saktim |
3.15.13 mahadanugamalabhyā bhaktirevātra sādhyā
3.15.14 kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ ||
3.15.21 prakṛtimahadahaṅkārāśca mātrāśca bhūtānyapi
3.15.22 hṛdapi daśākṣī pūruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśaḥ |
3.15.23 iti viditavibhāgo mucyate'sau prakṛtyāṃ
3.15.24 kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ ||
3.15.31 prakṛtigataguṇaughairnājyate pūruṣo'yaṃ
3.15.32 yadi tu sajati tasyāṃ tadguṇāstaṃ bhajeran |
3.15.33 madanubhajanatattvālocanaiḥ sāpyapeyāt
3.15.34 kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ ||
3.15.41 vimalamatirupāttairāsanādyairmadaṅgaṃ
3.15.42 guruḍasamadhirūḍhaṃ divyabhūṣāyudhāṅkam |
3.15.43 rucitulitatamālaṃ śīlayetānuvelaṃ
3.15.44 kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ ||
3.15.51 mama guṇaguṇalīlākarṇanaiḥ kīrtinādyair
3.15.52 mayi surasaridoghaprakhyacittānuvṛttiḥ |
3.15.53 bhavati paramabhaktiḥ sā hi mṛtyorvijetrī
3.15.54 kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ ||
3.15.61 ahaha bahulahiṃsāsañcitārthaiḥ kuḍumbaṃ
3.15.62 pratidinamanupuṣṇan strījito bālalālī |
3.15.63 viśati hi gṛhasakto yātanāṃ mayyabhaktaḥ
3.15.64 kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ ||
3.15.71 yuvatijaṭharakhinno jātabodho'pyakāṇḍe
3.15.72 prasavagalitabodhaḥ pīḍayollaṅghya bālyam |
3.15.73 punarapi bata muhyatyeva tāruṇyakāle
3.15.74 kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ ||
3.15.81 pitṛsuragaṇayājī dhārmiko yo gṛhasthaḥ
3.15.82 sa ca nipatati kāle dakṣiṇādhvopagāmī |
3.15.83 mayi nihitamakāṃ karma tūdakpathārthaṃ
3.15.84 kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ ||
3.15.91 iti suviditavedyāṃ deva! he devahūtiṃ
3.15.92 kṛtanutimanugṛhya tvaṃ gato yogisaṅghaiḥ |
3.15.93 vimalamatirathāsau bhaktiyogena muktā
3.15.94 tvamapi janahitīrthaṃ vartase prāgudīcyām ||
3.15.101 parama! kimu bahūktyā tvatpadāmbhojabhaktiṃ
3.15.102 sakalabhayavinetrīṃ sarvakāmopanetrīm |
3.15.103 vadasi khalu dṛḍhaṃ tvaṃ tvadvidhūyāmayānme
3.15.104 gurupavanapureś! tvayyupādhatsva bhaktim ||

4.16.11 daśo viriñcatanayo'tha manostanūjāṃ
4.16.12 labdhvā prasūtimiha ṣoḍaśa cāpa kanyāḥ |
4.16.13 dharme trayodaśa dadau piṛṣu svadhāṃ ca
4.16.14 svāhāṃ havirbhuji satīṃ giriśe tvadaṃśe ||
4.16.21 mūrtirhi dharmagṛhiṇī suṣuve bhavantaṃ
4.16.22 nārāyaṇaṃ narasakhaṃ mahitānubhāvam |
4.16.23 yajjanmani pramuditāḥ kṛtaturyaghoṣāḥ
4.16.24 puṣpotkarān pravavṛṣurnunuvuḥ suraughāḥ ||
4.16.31 daityaṃ sahasrakavacaṃ kavacaiḥ parītaṃ
4.16.32 sāhasravatsaratapassamarābhilavyaiḥ |
4.16.33 paryāyanīrmitatapassamarau bhavantau
4.16.34 śiṣṭaikakaṅkaṭamamuṃ nyahatāṃ salalim ||
4.16.41 anvācarannupadiśannapi mokṣadharmaṃ
4.16.42 tvaṃ bhrātṛmān badarikāśramamadhyavātsīḥ |
4.16.43 śakro'tha te śamatapobalanissahātmā
4.16.44 divyāṅganāparivṛtaṃ prajighāya māram ||
4.16.51 kāmo vasantamalayānilabandhuśālī
4.16.52 kāntākaṭākṣaviśikhairvikasadvilāsaiḥ |
4.16.53 vidhyanmuhurmuhurakampamudīkṣya ca tvāṃ
4.16.54 bhītastvāyātha jagade mṛduhāsabhājā ||
4.16.61 bhītyālamaṅgajavasantasurāṅganā! vo
4.16.62 manmānasaṃ tviha juṣudhvamiti bruvāṇaḥ |
4.16.63 tvaṃ vismayena paritaḥ stuvatāmathaiṣāṃ
4.16.64 pradarśayaḥ svaparicārakakātarākṣīḥ ||
4.16.71 sammohanāya militā madanādayste
4.16.72 tvaddāsikāparimalaiḥ kila mohamāpuḥ |
4.16.73 dattāṃ tvayā ca jagṛhustrapayaiva sarva
4.16.74 svarvāsigarvaśamanīṃ punarurvaśīṃ tām ||
4.16.81 dṛṣṭvorvaśīṃ tvaṃ kathāṃ ca niśamya śakraḥ
4.16.82 paryākulo'jāni bhavanmahimāvamarśāt |
4.16.83 evaṃ praśāntaramaṇīyataro'vatāras
4.16.84 tvatto'dhiko varada! kṛṣṇatanustvameva ||
4.16.91 dakṣastu dhāturatilālanayā rajondho
4.16.92 nātyādṛtastvayi ca kaṣṭamaśāntirāsīt |
4.16.93 yena vyanrundha sa bhavattanumeva śarvaṃ
4.16.94 yajño ca vairapiśune svasutāṃ vyamānīt ||
4.16.101 kruddheśamarditamakhaḥ sa tu kṛttaśīrṣo
4.16.102 devaprasāditaharādatha labdhajīvaḥ |
4.16.103 tvatpūritakratuvaraḥ punarāpa śāntiṃ
4.16.104 sa tvaṃ praśāntikara! pāhi marutpureś! ||
4.17.11 uttānapādanṛpatermanunandanasya
4.17.12 jāyā babhūva surucirnitarāmabhīṣṭā |
4.17.13 anyā sunītiriti bhartunarāddatā sā
4.17.14 tvāmeva nityamagatiḥ śaraṇaṃ gatābhūt ||
4.17.21 aṅke pituḥ suruciputrakamutramaṃ taṃ
4.17.22 dṛṣṭvā dhruvaḥ kila sunītisuto'dhirokṣyan |
4.17.23 ācikṣipe kila śiśuḥ sutarāṃ surucyā
4.17.24 dussantyajā khalu bhavadvimukhairasūyā ||
4.17.31 tvanmohite pitari paśyati dāravaśye
4.17.32 dūraṃ duruktinihataḥ sa gato nijāmbām |
4.17.33 sāpi svakarmagatisantaraṇāya puṃsāṃ
4.17.34 tvatpādameva śaraṇaṃ śiśave śaśaṃsa ||
4.17.41 ākarṇya so'pi bhavadarcaniścitātmā
4.17.42 mānī niretya nagarātkila pañcavarṣaḥ |
4.17.43 sandṛṣṭanāradaniveditamantramārgas
4.17.44 tvāmārarādha tapasā madhukānanānte ||
4.17.51 tāte viṣaṇṇahṛdaye nagarīṃ gatena
4.17.52 śrīnāradena parisāntvitacittavṛttau |
4.17.53 bālastvadarpitamanāḥ kramavardhitena
4.17.54 ninye kaṭhoratapasā kila pañca māsān ||
4.17.61 tāvattapobalanirucchvasite digante
4.17.62 devārthitastvamudayatkaruṇārdracetāḥ |
4.17.63 tvadrūpacidrasanilīnamateḥ purastā
4.17.64 dāvirbabhūvitha vibho! garuḍādhirūḍhaḥ ||
4.17.71 tvaddarśanapramadabhārataraṅgitaṃ taṃ
4.17.72 dṛgbhyāṃ nimagnamiva rūparasāyane te |
4.17.73 tuṣṭūṣamāṇamavagamya kapoladeśe
4.17.74 saṃspṛṣṭavānasi dareṇa tathādareṇa ||
4.17.81 tāvadvibodhavimalaṃ praṇuvantameṇa
4.17.82 mābhāṣathāstvamavagamya tadīyabhāvam |
4.17.83 rājyaṃ ciraṃ samanubhūya bhajasva bhūyaḥ
4.17.84 sarvottaraṃ dhruva! padaṃ vinivṛttihīnam ||
4.17.91 ityūcuṣi tvayi gate nṛpanandano'sā
4.17.92 vānanditākhilajano nagarīmupetaḥ |
4.17.93 reme ciraṃ bhavadanugrahapūrṇakāmas
4.17.94 tāte gate ca vanamādṛtarājyabhāraḥ ||
4.17.101 yakṣeṇa deva! nihate punaruttame'smin
4.17.102 yakṣaiḥ sa yuddhanirato virato manūkyā |
4.17.103 śāntyā prasannahṛdayāddhanadādupetāt
4.17.104 tvadbhaktimeva sudṛḍhāmavṛṇonmahātmā ||
4.17.111 ante bhavatpuruṣanītavimānayāto
4.17.112 mātrā samaṃ dhruvapade mudito'yamāste |
4.17.113 evaṃ svabhṛtyajanapālanaloladhīstvaṃ
4.17.114 vātālayādhipa! nirundhi mamāmayaughān ||
4.18.11 jātasya dhruvakula eva tuṅgakīrte
4.18.12 raṅgasya vyajani sutaḥ sa venanāmā |
4.18.13 yaddoṣavyathitamatiḥ sa rājavarya
4.18.14 stvatpāde vihitamanā vanaṃ gato'bhūt ||
4.18.21 pāpo'pi kṣititalapālanāya venaḥ
4.18.22 paurādyairupanihitaḥ kaṭhiravīryaḥ |
4.18.23 sarvebhyo nijabalameva sampraśaṃsan
4.18.24 bhūcakre tava yajanānyayaṃ nyarautsīt ||
4.18.31 samprāpte hitakathanāya tāpasaudhe
4.18.32 matto'nyo bhavanapatirna kaścaneti |
4.18.33 tvannindāvacanaparo munīśvaraistaiḥ
4.18.34 śāpāgnau śalabhadaśāmanāyi venaḥ ||
4.18.41 tannāśātkhalajanabhīrukairmunīndrai
4.18.42 stanmātrā ciraparirakṣite tadaṅge |
4.18.43 tyaktāghe parimathitādathorudaṇḍād
4.18.44 dordaṇḍe parimathite tvamāvirāsīḥ ||
4.18.51 vikhyātaḥ pṛthuriti tāpasopadiṣṭaiḥ
4.18.52 sūtādyaiḥ pariṇutabhāvibhūrivīryaḥ |
4.18.53 venārtyā kabalitasampadaṃ dharitrī
4.18.54 mākrāntāṃ nijadhanuṣā samāmakārṣī ||
4.18.61 bhūyastāṃ nijakulamukhyavatsayuktair
4.18.62 devādyaiḥ samucitacārubhājanesu |
4.18.63 annādīnyabhilaṣitāni yāni tāni
4.18.64 svacchandaṃ surabhitanūmadūduhastvam ||
4.18.71 ātmānaṃ yahati sakhaistvayi tridhāma
4.18.72 nnārabdhe śatatamavājimedhayāge |
4.18.73 spardhāluḥ śatamakha etya nīcaveṣo
4.18.74 hṛtvāśvaṃ tava tanayātparājito'bhūt ||
4.18.81 devendraṃ muhuriti vājinaṃ harantaṃ
4.18.82 vahnau taṃ munavaramaṇḍale juhūṣau |
4.18.83 rundhāne kamalabhave kratoḥ samāptau
4.18.84 sākṣāttvaṃ madhuripumaikṣathāḥ svayaṃ svam ||
4.18.91 taddataṃ varamupalabhya bhaktimekāṃ
4.18.92 gaṅgānte vihitapadaḥ kadāpi deva! |
4.18.93 satrasthaṃ muninivahaṃ hitāni śaṃsa
4.18.94 nnaikṣiṣṭhāḥ sanakamukhānmunīn purastāt ||
4.18.101 vijñānaṃ sanakamukhoditaṃ dadhānaḥ
4.18.102 svātmānaṃ svayamagamo vanāntasevī |
4.18.103 tattādṛkpṛthuvapurīśa! satvaraṃ me
4.18.104 rogaughaṃ praśamaya vātagehavāsin! ||
4.19.11 pṛthostu naptā pṛthudharmakarmaṭhaḥ prācīnabarhiryuvatau śatadrutau |
4.19.12 pracetaso nāma sucetasaḥ sutānajījanattvatkaruṇāṅkurāniva ||
4.19.21 pituḥ sisṛkṣāniratasya śāsanādbhavattapasyāniratā daśāpi te |
4.19.22 payonidhiṃ paścimametya tattaṭe sarovaraṃ sandadṛśurmanoharam ||
4.19.31 tadā bhavattīrthamidaṃ samāgato bhavo bhavatsevakadarśanādṛtaḥ |
4.19.32 prakāśamāsādya puraḥ pracetasāmupādiśadbhaktatamastava stavam ||
4.19.41 stavaṃ japantastamamī jalāntare bhavantamāseviṣatāyutaṃ samāḥ |
4.19.42 bhavatsukhāsvādarasādamīṣviyān babhūva kalo dhruvavanna śīghratā ||
4.19.51 tapobhireṣāmatimātravardhibhiḥ sa yajñahiṃsānirato'pi pāvitaḥ |
4.19.52 pitāpi teṣāṃ gṛhayātanāradapradarśitātmā bhavadātmatāṃ yayau ||
4.19.61 kṛpābalenaiva tatḥ pracetasāṃ prakāśamāgāḥ patagendravāhanaḥ |
4.19.62 virājicakrādivarāyudhāṃśubhirbhujābhiraṣṭābhirudañcitadyutiḥ ||
4.19.71 pracetasāṃ tāvadayācatāmapi tvameva kāruṇyabharādvārānadāḥ |
4.19.72 bhavadvicintāpi śivāyadehināṃ bhavatvasau rudranutiśca kāmadā ||
4.19.81 avāpya kāntāṃ tanayāṃ mahīruhāṃ tayā ramadhvaṃ daśalakṣavatsarīm |
4.19.82 suto'stu dakṣo nanu tatkṣaṇācca māṃ prayāsyatheti nyagado mudaiva tān ||
4.19.91 tataśca te bhūtalarodhinastarūn krūdhā dahanto druhiṇena vāritāḥ |
4.19.92 drumaiśca dattāṃ tanayāmavāpya tāṃ tvaduktakālaṃ sukhino'bhiremire ||
4.19.101 avāpya dakṣaṃ ca sutaṃ kṛtādhvarāḥ pracetaso nāradalabdhayā dhiyā |
4.19.102 avāpurānandapadaṃ tathāvidhustvamīśa! vātālayanātha! pāhi mām ||

5.20.11 priyavratasya priyaputrabhūtādāgnīdhrarājādudito hi nābhiḥ |
5.20.12 tvāṃ dṛṣṭavāniṣṭadamiṣṭamadhye tavaiva tuṣṭyai kṛtayajñakarmā ||
5.20.21 abhiṣṭutastatra munīśvaraistvaṃ rājñā svatulyaṃ sutamarthyamānaḥ |
5.20.22 svayaṃ janiṣye'hamiti bruvāṇastirodadhā barhiṣi viśvamūrte! ||
5.20.31 nābhipriyāyāmatha merudevyāṃ tvamaṃśato'bhūrṛṣabhābhidhānaḥ |
5.20.32 alokasāmānyaguṇaprabhāvaprabhāvitāśeṣajanapramodaḥ ||
5.20.41 tvayi trilokībhṛti rājybhāraṃ nidhāya nābhiḥ saha merudevyā |
5.20.42 tapovanaṃ prāpya bhavanniṣevī gataḥ kilānandapadaṃ padaṃ te ||
5.20.51 indrastvadutkarṣakṛtādamarṣādvavarṣa nāsminnajanābhavarṣe |
5.20.52 yadā tadā tvaṃ nijayogaśaktyā svavarṣamenadvyadadhāḥ suvarṣam ||
5.20.61 jitendradattāṃ kamanīṃ jayantīmathodvahannātmaratāśayo'pi |
5.20.62 ajījanattatra śataṃ tanūjān yeṣāṃ kṣitīṣo bharato'grajanmā ||
5.20.71 navābhavan yogivarā navānye tvapālayan bhāratavarṣakhaṇḍān |
5.20.72 saikā tvaśītistava śeṣaputrāstapobalādbhūsurabhūyamīyuḥ ||
5.20.81 uktvā sutebhyo'tha munīndramadhye viraktibhaktyanvitamuktimārgam |
5.20.82 svayaṃ gataḥ pāramahaṃsyavṛttimadhā jaḍonmattapiśācacaryām ||
5.20.91 parātmabhūto'pi paropadeśaṃ kurvan bhavan sarvanirasyamānaḥ |
5.20.92 vikārahīno vicacāra kṛtsnāṃ mahīmahīnātmarasābhilīnaḥ ||
5.20.101 śayuvrataṃ gomṛgakākacaryāṃ ciraṃ carannāpya paraṃ svarūpam |
5.20.102 davāhṛtāṅgaḥ kuṭakācale tvaṃ tāpānmamāpākuru vātanātha! ||
5.21.11 madhyodbhavao bhuva ilāvṛtanāmri varṣe
5.21.12 gaurīpradhānavanitājanamātrabhāji |
5.21.13 śarveṇa mantranutibhiḥ sumupāsyamānaṃ
5.21.14 saṅkarṣaṇātmakamadhīśvara! samśraye tvām ||
5.21.21 bhadrāśvanāmaka ilāvṛtapūrvavarṣe
5.21.22 bhadraśravobhirṛṣibhiḥ pariṇūyamānam |
5.21.23 kalpāntagūḍhanigamoddharaṇapravīṇaṃ
5.21.24 dhyāyāmi deva! hayaśīrṣatanuṃ bhavantam ||
5.21.31 dhyāyāmi dakṣiṇagate harivarṣavarṣe
5.21.32 prāhlādamukhyapuruṣaiḥ pariṣevyamāṇam |
5.21.33 uttuṅgaśāntadhavalākṛtimekaśuddha
5.21.34 jñānapradaṃ narahariṃ bhagavan! bhavantam ||
5.21.41 varṣe pratīci lalitātmani ketumāle
5.21.42 līlāviśeṣalalitasmitaśobhanāṅgam |
5.21.43 lakṣmyā prajāpatisutaiśca niṣevyamāṇaṃ
5.21.44 tasyāḥ priyāya dhṛtakāmatanuṃ bhaje tvām ||
5.21.51 ramye'pyudīci khalu ramyakanāmri varṣe
5.21.52 tadvarṣanāthamanuvarysaparyamāṇam |
5.21.53 bhaktaikavatsalamamatsarahṛtsu bhāntaṃ
5.21.54 matsyākṛtiṃ bhuvananātha! bhaje bhavantam ||
5.21.61 varṣaṃ hiraṇmayasamāhvayamauttarāha
5.21.62 māsīnamadridhṛtikarmaṭhakāmaṭhāṅgam |
5.21.63 saṃsevate pitṛgaṇapravaro'ryamāyaṃ
5.21.64 taṃ tvāṃ bhajāmi bhagavan! paracinmayātman! ||
5.21.71 kiñcottareṣu kuruṣu priyayā dharaṇyā
5.21.72 saṃsevito mahitamantranutiprabhedaiḥ |
5.21.73 daṃṣṭrāgraghṛṣṭaghanapṛṣṭhagariṣṭhavarṣmā
5.21.74 tvaṃ pāhi vijñanutayajñavarāhamūrte! ||
5.21.81 yāmyāṃ diśaṃ bhajati kimpuruṣākhyavarṣe
5.21.82 saṃsevito hanumatā dṛḍhabhaktibhājā |
5.21.83 sītābhirāmaparamādbhutarūpaśālī
5.21.84 rāmātmakaḥ parilasan paripāhi viṣṇo! ||
5.21.91 śrīnāradena saha bhāratakhaṇḍamukhyais
5.21.92 tvaṃ sāṅkhyayoganutibhiḥ samupāsyamānaḥ |
5.21.93 ākalpakālamiha sādhujanābhiraksī
5.21.94 nārāyaṇo narasakhaḥ paripāhi bhūman! ||
5.21.101 plākṣe'rkarūpamayi śālmala indurūpaṃ
5.21.102 dvīye bhajanti kuśanāmani vahnirūpam |
5.21.103 krauñce'mburūpamatha vāyumayaṃ ca śāke
5.21.104 tvāṃ brahmarūpamayi puṣkaranāmri lokāḥ ||
5.21.111 sarvairdhruvīdibhiruḍuprakarairgrahaiśca
5.21.112 pucchādikeṣvavayaveṣvabhikalpyamānaiḥ |
5.21.113 tvaṃ śiṃśumāravapuṣā mahatāmupāsyaḥ
5.21.114 sandhyāsu rundhi narakaṃ mama sindhuśāyain! ||
5.21.121 pātālamūlabhuvi śeṣatanuṃ bhavantaṃ
5.21.122 lolaikakuṇḍalavirājisahasraśīrṣam |
5.21.123 nīlāmbaraṃ dhṛtahalaṃ bhujagāṅganābhir
5.21.124 juṣṭaṃ bhaje hara gadān gurugehanātha! ||

6.22.11 ajāmilo nāma mahīsuraḥ purā caran vibho! dharmapathān gṛhāśramī |
6.22.12 gurorgirā kānanametya dṛṣṭavān sughṛṣṭaśīlāṃ kulaṭāṃ madākulām ||
6.22.21 svataḥ praśānto'pi tadāhṛtāśayaḥ svadharmamutsṛja tayā samāraman |
6.22.22 adharmakārī daśamī bhavan punardadhau bhavannāmayute sute ratim ||
6.22.31 sa mṛtyukāle yamarājakiṅgarān bhayaṅkarāmstrīnabhilakṣayan bhiyā |
6.22.32 purā manāktvatsmṛtivāsanābalājjuhāva nārāyaṇanāmakaṃ sutam ||
6.22.41 durāśayasyāpi tadātvanirgatatvadīyanāmākṣaramātravaibhavāt |
6.22.42 puro'bhipeturbhavadīyapārṣadāścaturbhujāḥ pītapaṭā manoharāḥ ||
6.22.51 amuṃ ca sampāśya vikarṣato bhaṭān vimuñcatetyārurudhurbalādamī |
6.22.52 nivāritāste ca bhavajjanaistadā tadīyapāpaṃ nikhilaṃ nyavedayan ||
6.22.61 bhavantu pāpāni kathaṃ tu niṣkṛte kṛte'pi bho daṇḍanamasti paṇḍitāḥ! |
6.22.62 na niṣkṛtiḥ kiīṃ viditā bhavādṛśāmiti prabho! tvatpuruṣā babhāṣire ||
6.22.71 śrutismṛtibhyāṃ vihitā vratādayaḥ punanti pāpaṃ na lunanti vāsanām |
6.22.72 anantasevā tu nikṛntati dvayīmiti prabho! tvatpuruṣā babhāṣire ||
6.22.81 anena bho! janmasahasrakoṭibhiḥ kṛteṣu pāpeṣvapi niṣkṛtiḥ kṛtā |
6.22.82 tadagrahīnnāma bhayākulo hareriti prabho! tvatpuruṣā babhāṣire ||
6.22.91 nṛṇāmabuddhyāpi mukundakīrtanaṃ dahatyaghaughānmahimāsya tādṛśaḥ |
6.22.92 yathāgniredhāṃsi yathauṣedhaṃ gadāniti prabho! tvatpuruṣā babhāṣire ||
6.22.101 itīritairyāmyabhaṭairapāsṛte bhavadbhaṭānāṃ ca gaṇe tirohite |
6.22.102 bhavatsmṛtiṃ kañcana kālamācaran bhavatpadaṃ prāpi bhavadbhaṭairasau ||
6.22.111 svakiṅgarāvedanaśaṅkito yamastvadaṅghribhakteṣu na gamyatāmiti |
6.22.112 svakīyabhṛtyānaśiśikṣaduccakaiḥ sa deva! vātālaynātha! pāhi mām ||
6.23.11 pracetastu bhagavannaparo'pi dakṣas
6.23.12 tvatsevanaṃ vyadhita sargavivṛddhikāmaḥ |
6.23.13 āvirbabhūvitha tadā lasadaṣṭabāhus
6.23.14 tasmai varaṃ daditha tāṃ ca vadhūmasiknīm ||
6.23.21 tasyātmajāstvayutamīśa! punaḥ sahasraṃ
6.23.22 śrīnāradasya vacasā tava mārgamāpuḥ |
6.23.23 naikatravāsamṛṣaye mumuce sa śāpaṃ
6.23.24 bhaktottamastvṛṣiranugnahameva mene ||
6.23.31 ṣaṣṭyā tato duhitṛbhiḥ sṛjataḥ kulaughān
6.23.32 dauhitrasūnuratha tasya sa viśvarūpaḥ |
6.23.33 tvatstotravarmitamajāpayadindramājau
6.23.34 deva! tvadīyamahimā khalu sarvajaitraḥ ||
6.23.41 prākśūrasenaviṣaye kila citraketuḥ
6.23.42 putrāgrahī nṛpatiraṅgirasaḥ prabhāvāt |
6.23.43 labdhvaikaputramatha tatra hate sapatnī
6.23.44 saṅghairamuhyadavaśastava māyayāsau ||
6.23.51 taṃ nāradastu samamaṅgirasā dayāluḥ
6.23.52 samprāpya tāvadupadarśya sutasya jīvam |
6.23.53 kasyāsmi putra iti tasya girā vimohaṃ
6.23.54 tyakatvā tvadarcanavidhau nṛpatiṃ nyayuṅkta ||
6.23.61 stotraṃ ca mantramapi nāradato'tha labdhvā
6.23.62 toṣāya śeṣavapuṣo nanu te tapasyan |
6.23.63 vidyādharādhipatitāṃ sa hi saptarātre
6.23.64 labdhvātyukuṇṭḥmatiranvabhajadbhavantam ||
6.23.71 tasmai mṛṇāladhavalena sahasraśīrṣṇā
6.23.72 rūpeṇa baddhanutisiddhagaṇāvṛteṇa |
6.23.73 prādurbhavannacirato nutibhiḥ prasanno
6.23.74 dattvātmatattvamanugṛhya tirodadhātha ||
6.23.81 tvadbhaktamauliratha so'pi ca lakṣalakṣaṃ
6.23.82 varṣāṇi harṣulamanā bhuvaneṣu kāmam |
6.23.83 sanṅgāpayan guṇagaṇaṃ tava sundarībhiḥ
6.23.84 saṅgatirekarahito lalitaṃ cacāra ||
6.23.91 atyantasaṅgavilayāya bhavatpraṇunno
6.23.92 nūnaṃ sa rūpyagirimāpya mahatsamāje |
6.23.93 niśśaṅkamaṅkakṛtavallabhamaṅgajāriṃ
6.23.94 taṃ śaṅkaraṃ parihasannumayābhiśepe ||
6.23.101 nissambhramastvayamayācitaśāpamokṣo
6.23.102 vṛtrāsuratvamupagamya surendrayodhī |
6.23.103 bhaktyātmatattvakathanaiḥ samare vicitraṃ
6.23.104 śatrorapi bhramamapāsya gataḥ padaṃ te ||
6.23.111 tvatsevanena ditirindravadhodyatāpi
6.23.112 tān prtyutendrasuhṛdo maruto'bhilebhe |
6.23.113 duṣṭāśaye'pi śubhadaiva bhavanniṣevā
6.23.114 tattādṛśastvamava māṃ pavanālayeśa! ||

7.24.11 hiraṇyākṣe potripravaravapuṣā deva! bhavatā
7.24.12 hate śolakrodhaglapitaghṛtiretasya sahajaḥ |
7.24.13 hiraṇyaprārambhaḥ kaśipuramarārātisadasi
7.24.14 pratijñāmātene tava kila vadhārthaṃ muraripo! ||
7.24.21 vidhātāraṃ ghoraṃ sa khalu tapasitvā nacirataḥ
7.24.22 puraḥ sākṣātkurvan suranaramṛgādyairanidhanam |
7.24.23 varaṃ labdhvā dṛpto jagadiha bhavannāyakamidaṃ
7.24.24 parikṣundannindrādaharata divaṃ tvāmagaṇayan ||
7.24.31 nihantuṃ tvāṃ bhūyastava padamavāptasya ca ripor
7.24.32 bahirdṛṣṭerantardadhitha hṛdaye sūkṣmavapuṣā |
7.24.33 nadannuccaistatrāpyakhilabhuvanānte ca mṛgayan
7.24.34 bhiyā yātaṃ matvā sa khalu jitakāśī nivavṛte ||
7.24.41 tato'sya prahlādaḥ samajani suto garbhavasatau
7.24.42 munervīṇāpāṇeradhigatabhabadbhaktimahimā |
7.24.43 sa vai jātyā daityaḥ śiśurapi sametya tvayi ratiṃ
7.24.44 gatastvadbhaktānāṃ varada! paramodāharaṇatām ||
7.24.51 surārīṇāṃ hāsyaṃ tava caraṇadāsyaṃ nijasute
7.24.52 sa dṛṣṭvā diṣṭātmā gurubhiraśiśikṣacciramamum |
7.24.53 guruproktaṃ cāsāvidamidamabhadrāya dṛḍhami
7.24.54 tyapākurvan sarvaṃ tava caraṇabhaktyaiva vavṛdhe ||
7.24.61 adhīteṣu śreṣṭhaṃ kimiti paripṛṣṭe'tha tanaye
7.24.62 bhavadbhaktiṃ varyāmabhigadati paryākuladhṛtiḥ |
7.24.63 gurubhyo roṣitvā sahajamatirasyotyabhividan
7.24.64 vadhipāyānasmin vyatatutbhavatpādaśaraṇe ||
7.24.71 sa śūlairāviddhaḥ subahu mathito diggajagaṇair
7.24.72 mahāsarpairdaṣṭo'pyanaśanagarāhāravidhutaḥ |
7.24.73 girindrāvakṣipto'pyahaha paramātmannayi vibho!
7.24.74 tvayi nyastātmatvātkimapi na nipīḍāmabhajata ||
7.24.81 tataḥ śaṅkāviṣṭaḥ sa punaratiduṣṭo'sya janako
7.24.82 gurūktyā tadgeha kila varuṇapāśaistamaruṇat |
7.24.83 guroścāsānnidhye sa punaranugān daityatanayān
7.24.84 bhavadbhaktestattvaṃ paramapi vijñānamaśiṣat ||
7.24.91 pitā śṛṇvan bālaprakaramakhilaṃ tvatstutiparaṃ
7.24.92 ruṣāndhaḥ prāhainaṃ kulahataka! kaste balamiti |
7.24.93 balaṃ me vaikuṇṭhastava ca jagatāṃ cāpi sa balaṃ
7.24.94 sa eva trailokyaṃ sakalamiti dhīro'yamagadīt ||
7.24.101 are! kvāsau kvāsau sakalajagadātmā haririti
7.24.102 prabhinte sma stambhaṃ calitakaravālo ditisutaḥ |
7.24.103 ataḥ paścādviṣṇo! na hi vaditumīśo'smi sahasā
7.24.104 kṛpātman! viśvātman! pavanapuravāsin! mṛḍaya mām ||
7.25.11 stambhe ghaṭṭayato hiraṇyakaśipoḥ karṇau samācūrṇaya
7.25.12 nnādhūrṇajjagadaṇdakuṇḍakuharo ghorastavābhūdravaḥ |
7.25.13 śrutvā yaṃ kila daityarājahṛdaye pūrvaṃ kadāpyaśrutaṃ
7.25.14 kampaḥ kaścana sampapātcalito'pyambhojabhūrviṣṭapāt ||
7.25.21 daitye dikṣu visṛṣṭacakṣuṣi mahāsaṃrāmbhiṇī stambhataḥ
7.25.22 sambhūtaṃ na mṛgātmakaṃ na manujākāraṃ vapuste vibho! |
7.25.23 kiṃ kiṃ bhīṣaṇametadadbhutamiti vyudbhrāntacitte'sure
7.25.24 visphurjaddhavalograromavikasadvarṣmā samājṛmbhathāḥ ||
7.25.31 taptasvarṇasavarṇaghūrṇadatirūkṣākṣaṃ saṭākesara
7.25.32 protkampapranikumbitāmbaramaho jīyāttavedaṃ vapuḥ |
7.25.33 vyāttavyāptamahādarīsakhamukhaṃ khaḍgogravalganmahā
7.25.34 jihvānirgamadṛśyamānasumahādaṃṣṭrāyugoḍḍāmaram ||
7.25.41 utsarpadvalibhaṅgabhīṣuṇahanuṃ hvasvasthavīyastara
7.25.42 grīvaṃ pīvaradośśatodgatanakhakrūrāṃśudūrolbaṇam |
7.25.43 vyomollaṅghighanāghanopamaghanapradhvānanirdhāvita
7.25.44 spardhāluprakaraṃ namāmi bhavatastannārasiṃhaṃ vapuḥ ||
7.25.51 nūnaḥ vuṣṇurayaṃ nihanmyamumiti bhrāmyadgadābhīṣaṇaṃ
7.25.52 daityendraṃ samupādravantamadhṛthā dorbhyāṃ pṛthubhyāmammum |
7.25.53 vīro nirgalito'tha khaḍgaphalake gṛhṇan vicitraśramān
7.25.54 vyāvṛṇvan punarāpapāta bhuvanagrāsodyataṃ tvāmaho ||
7.25.61 bhrāmyantaṃ ditihādhamaṃ punarapi prodgṛhya dorbhyāṃ javād
7.25.62 dvāre'thoruyuge nipātya nakharān vyutnkhāya vakṣobhuvi |
7.25.63 nirbhindannadhigarbhanirbharagaladraktāmbu baddhotsavaṃ
7.25.64 pāyaṃ pāyamudairayo bahujagatsaṃhārisiṃhāravān ||
7.25.71 tyaktvā taṃ hatamāśu raktalaharīsiktonnamadvarṣmaṇi
7.25.72 pratyutpatya samastadaityapaṭalīṃ cākhādyamāne tvayi |
7.25.73 bhrāmyadbhūmi vikampitāmbudhikulaṃ vyālolaśailotkaraṃ
7.25.74 protsarpatkhacaraṃ carācaramaho duḥsthāmavasthāṃ dadhau ||
7.25.81 tāvanmāṃsavapākarālavapuṣaṃ ghorāntramālādharaṃ
7.25.82 tvāṃ madhyesabhamiddharoṣamuṣitaṃ durvāragurvāravam |
7.25.83 abhyetuṃ na śaśaka ko'pi bhuvane dūre sthitā bhīravaḥ
7.25.84 sarve śarvaviriñcavāsavamukhāḥ pratyekamastoṣata ||
7.25.91 bhūyo'pyakṣataroṣadhāmni bhavati brahmājñayā bālake
7.25.92 prahlāde padayornamatyapabhaye kāruṇyabhārākulaḥ |
7.25.93 śāntastvaṃ karamasya mūrdhni samadhāḥ stotrairathodnāyata
7.25.94 stasyākāmadhiyo'pi tenitha varaṃ lokāya cānugraham ||
7.25.101 evaṃ nāṭitaraudraceṣṭita! vibho! śrītāpanīyābhidha
7.25.102 śrutyantasphuṭagītasarvamahimannatyantaśuddhākṛte! |
7.25.103 tattādṛṅnikhilottaraṃ punaraho kastvāṃ paro laṅghayet
7.25.104 prahlādapriya! he marutpurapate! sarvāmayātpāhi mām ||

8.26.11 indradyūmnaḥ pāṇḍyakhaṇḍādhirājastvadbhaktātmī candanādrau kadīcit |
8.26.12 tvatsevāyāṃ magnadhīrāluloke naivāgastyaṃ prāptamātithyakāmam ||
8.26.21 kumbhodbhūtaḥ saṃbhṛtakrodhabhāraḥ stabdhātmā tvaṃ hastibhūyaṃ bhajeti |
8.26.22 śaptvāthainaṃ pratyagātso'pi lebhe hastīndratvaṃ tvatsmṛtivyaktidhanyam ||
8.26.31 dugdhāmbhodhermadhyabhāji trikūṭe kroḍañchaile yūthapo'yaṃ vaśābhiḥ |
8.26.32 sarvān jantūnatyavartiṣṭa śaktyā tvadbhaktānāṃ kutra notkarṣalābhaḥ ||
8.26.41 stena sthemnā divyadehatvaśaktyā so'yaṃ khedānaprajānan kadācit |
8.26.42 śailaprānte gharmatāntaḥ sarasyāṃ yūthaiḥ sārdhaṃ tvatpraṇunno'bhireme ||
8.26.51 hūhūstāvaddevalasyāpi śāpadgrāhībhūtastajjale vartamānaḥ |
8.26.52 jagrāhainaṃ hastinaṃ pādadeśe śāntyarthaṃ hi śrāntido'si svakānām ||
8.26.61 tvatsevāyā vaibhavāddurnirodhaṃ yudhyantaṃ taṃ vatsarāṇāṃ sahasram |
8.26.62 prāpte kāle tvatpadaikāgryasiddhyai nakrākrāntaṃ hastivīraṃ vyadhāstvam ||
8.26.71 ārtivyaktaprāktanajñānabhaktiḥ śuṇḍotkṣiptaiḥ samarcan |
8.26.72 pūrvābhyastaṃ nirviśeṣātmaniṣṭhaṃ stotraśreṣṭhaṃ so'ndagādītparātman! ||
8.26.81 śrutvā stotraṃ nirguṇasthaṃ samastaṃ brahmeśādyairnāhamityaprayāte |
8.26.82 sarvātmā tvaṃ bhūrikāruṇyavegāttārkṣyārūḍhaḥ prekṣito'bhūḥ purastāt ||
8.26.91 hastīndraṃ taṃ hastapadmena dhṛtvā cakreṇa tvaṃ nakravaryaṃ vyadārīḥ |
8.26.92 gandharve'sminmuktaśāpe sa hastī tvatsārūpyaṃ prāpya dedīpyate sma ||
8.26.101 etadvṛttaṃ tvāṃ ca māṃ ca prage yo gāyetso'yaṃ bhūyase śreyase syāt |
8.26.102 ityuktvainaṃ tena sārdhaṃ gatastvaṃ dhiṣṇyaṃ viṣṇo! pāhi vātālayeśa! ||
8.27.11 durvāsāḥ suravanitāptadivyamālyaṃ śakrāya svayamupadāya tatra bhūjaḥ |
8.27.12 nāgendrapratimṛdite śaśāya śakraṃ kā kṣāntistvaditaradevatāṃśajānām ||
8.27.21 śāpena prathitajare'tha nirjarendre deveṣvapyasurajiteṣu niṣprabheṣu |
8.27.22 śarvādyāḥ kamalajametya sarvadevā nirvāṇaprabhava! samaṃ bhavantamāpuḥ ||
8.27.31 brahmādyairnutamahimā ciraṃ tadānīṃ prāduḥṣan varada! puraḥ pareṇa dhāmnā |
8.27.32 he devā! ditijakulairvidhāya sandhiṃ pīyūṣaṃ parimathateti paryaśāstvam ||
8.27.41 sandhānaṃ kṛtavati dānavaiḥ suraudhe manthānaṃ nayati madena mandarādrim |
8.27.42 bhraṣṭe'smin badaramivodvahan khagendre sadystvaṃ vinihitavān payaḥ payodhau ||
8.27.51 ādhāya drutamatha vāsukiṃ varatrāṃ pāthodhau vinihitasarvabījajāle |
8.27.52 prārabdhe mathanavidhau surāsuraistairvyājāttvaṃ bhujagamukhe'karoḥ surārīn ||
8.27.61 kṣubdhādrau kṣubhitajalodare tadānīṃ dugdhābdhau gurutarabhārato nimagne |
8.27.62 deveṣu vyathitatameṣu tatpriyaiṣī prāṇaiṣīḥ kamaṭhatanuṃ kaṭhorapṛṣṭhām ||
8.27.71 vajrātisthiratarakarpareṇa viṣṇo! vistārātparigatalakṣayojanena |
8.27.72 ambhodheḥ kuharagatena varṣmaṇā tvaṃ nirmagnaṃ kṣitidharanāthamunninetha ||
8.27.81 unmagne jhaṭiti tadā dharādharendre nirmethurdṛḍhamiha sammadena sarve |
8.27.82 āviśya dvitayagaṇe'pi sarparāje vaivaśyaṃ pariśamayannavīvṛdhastān ||
8.27.91 uddāmabhramaṇajavonnamadgirīndranyastaikasthiratarahastapaṅkajaṃ tvām |
8.27.92 abhrānte vidhigiriśādayaḥ pramodādudbhrāntā nunuvurupāttapuṣpavarṣāḥ ||
8.27.101 daityaudhe bhujagamukhānilena tapte tenaiva tridaśakule'pi kiñcidārte |
8.27.102 kāruṇyāttava kila deva! vārivāhāḥ prāvarṣannamaragaṇānna daityasaṅghān ||
8.28.11 garalaṃ taralānalaṃ purastājjaladherudvijagāla kālakūṭam |
8.28.12 amarastutivādamodanighno niriśastannipapau bhavatpriyārtham ||
8.28.21 vimathatsu surāsureṣu jātā surabhistāmṛṣiṣu nyadhāstridhāman! |
8.28.22 hayaratnamabhūdathebharatnaṃ dyūtaruścāpsarasaḥ sureṣu tāni ||
8.28.31 jagadīśa! bhavatparā tadānīṃ kamanīyā kamalā babhūva devī |
8.28.32 amalāmavalokya yāṃ vilokaḥ sakalo'pi spṛhayāmbabhūva lokaḥ ||
8.28.41 tvayi dattahṛdde tadaiva devyai tridaśendro maṇipīṭhikāṃ vyatārīt |
8.28.42 sakalopahṛtābhiṣecanīyairṛṣayastāṃ śrutigīrbhirabhyaṣiñcan ||
8.28.51 abhiṣekajalānupātimugdhatvadapāṅgairavabhūṣitāṅgavallīm |
8.28.52 maṇikuṇḍalapītacelahārapramukhaistāmamarādayo'ndabhūṣan ||
8.28.61 varaṇasrajamāttabhṛṅganādāṃ dadhatī sā kucakumbhamandayānā |
8.28.62 padaśiñjitamañjunpurā tvāṃ kalitavrīlavilāsamāsasāda ||
8.28.71 giriśadruhiṇādisarvadevān guṇabhājo'pyavimuktadoṣaleśān |
8.28.72 avamṛśya sadaiva sarvaramye nihitā tvayyanayāpi divyamālā ||
8.28.81 urasā tarasā mamānithaināṃ bhuvanānāṃ jananīmananyabhāvām |
8.28.82 tvadurovilasattadīkṣaṇaśrīparivṛṣṭyā paripuṣṭamāsa viśvam ||
8.28.91 atimohanavibhramā tadānīṃ madayantī khalu vāruṇī nirāgāt |
8.28.92 tamasaḥ padavīmadāstvamenāmatisammānanayā mahāsurebhyaḥ ||
8.28.101 taruṇāmbudasundarastadā tvaṃ nanu dhanvantarirutthito'mburāśeḥ |
8.28.102 amṛtaṃ kalaśe vahan karābhyāmakhilārtiṃ hara mārutālayeśa! ||
8.29.11 udgacchatastava karādamṛtaṃ haratsu
8.29.12 daityeṣu tānaśaraṇānanunīya devān |
8.29.13 sadhastirodadhitha deva! bhavatprabhāvād
8.29.14 udyatsayūthyakalahā ditijā babhūvuḥ ||
8.29.21 śyāmāṃ rucāpi vayasāpi tanuṃ tadānīṃ
8.29.22 prāpto'si tuṅgakucamaṇḍalabhaṅgurāṃ tvam |
8.29.23 pīyuṣakumbhakalahaṃ parimucya sarve
8.29.24 tṛṣṇākulāḥ pratiyayustvadurojakumbhe ||
8.29.31 kā tvaṃ mṛgākṣi! vibhajasva sudhāmimāmi
8.29.32 tyārūḍharāgavivaśānabhiyācato'mūn |
8.29.33 viśvasyate mayi kathaṃ kulaṭāsmi daityā!
8.29.34 ityālapannapi suviśvasitānatānīḥ ||
8.29.41 modātsudhākalaśameṣu dadatsu sā tvaṃ
8.29.42 duśceṣṭitaṃ mama sahadhvamiti bruvāṇā |
8.29.43 paṅktiprabhedaviniveśitadevadaityā
8.29.44 līlāvilāsagatibhiḥ samadāḥ sudhāṃ tām ||
8.29.51 asmāsviyaṃ praṇayinītyusureṣu teṣu
8.29.52 joṣaṃ sthiteṣvatha samāpya sudhāṃ sureṣu |
8.29.53 tvaṃ bhaktalokavaśago mijarūpametya
8.29.54 svarbhānumardhaparipītasudhaṃ vyalāvīḥ ||
8.29.61 tvattaṃ sudhāharaṇayogyaphalaṃ pareṣu
8.29.62 dattvā gate tvayi suraiḥ khalu te vyagṛhṇan |
8.29.63 ghore'tha mūrchati raṇe balidaityamāyā
8.29.64 vyāmohite suragaṇe tvamihāvirāsīḥ ||
8.29.71 tvaṃ kālanemimatha mālisukhāñjaghantha
8.29.72 śakro jaghāna balijambhavalān sapākān |
8.29.73 śuṣkārdraduṣkaravadhe namucau ca lūne
8.29.74 phenena nāradagirā nyaruṇo raṇaṃ tam ||
8.29.81 yoṣāvapurdanujamohanamāhitaṃ te
8.29.82 śrutvaṃ vilokanakutūhalavānmaheśaḥ |
8.29.83 bhūtaiḥ samaṃ girijayā ca gataḥ padaṃ te
8.29.84 stutvābravīdabhimataṃ tvamatho tirodhāḥ ||
8.29.91 ārāmasīmani ca kandukaghātalīlā
8.29.92 lolāyamānanayanāṃ kamanīṃ manojñām |
8.29.93 tvāmeṣa vīkṣya vigaladvasanāṃ manobhū
8.29.94 vegādanaṅgaripuraṅga! samāliliṅga ||
8.29.101 bhūyo'pi vidrutavatīmupadhāvya devo
8.29.102 vīryapramokṣavikasatparamārthabodhaḥ |
8.29.103 tvanmānitastava mahattvamuvāca devyai
8.29.104 tattādṛśastvamava vātaniketanātha! ||
8.30.11 śakreṇa saṃyati hato'pi balirmahātmā
8.30.12 śukreṇa jīvitatanuḥ kratuvardhitoṣmā |
8.30.13 vikrāntimān bhayanilīnasurāṃ trilokīṃ
8.30.14 cakre vaśe sa tava cakramukhādabhītaḥ ||
8.30.21 putrārtidarśanavaśādaditirviṣaṇṇā
8.30.22 taṃ kāśyapaṃ nijapatiṃ śaraṇaṃ prapannā |
8.30.23 tvatpūjanaṃ taduditaṃ hi payovratākhyaṃ
8.30.24 sā dvādaśāhamacarattvayi bhaktipūrṇā ||
8.30.31 tasyāvadhau tvayi nilīnamateramuṣyāḥ
8.30.32 śyāmaścaturbhujavapuḥ svayamāvirāsīḥ |
8.30.33 namrāṃ ca tāmiha bhavattanayo bhaveyaṃ
8.30.34 gopyaṃ madīkṣaṇamiti pralapannayāsīḥ ||
8.30.41 tvaṃ kāśyape tapasi sannidadhattadānīṃ
8.30.42 prāpto'si garbhamaditeḥ praṇuto vidhātrā |
8.30.43 prāsūta ca prakaṭavaiṣṇavadivyarūpaṃ
8.30.44 sā dvādaśīśravaṇapuṇyadine bhavantam ||
8.30.51 puṇyāśramaṃ tamabhivarṣati puṣpavarṣair
8.30.52 harṣākule surakule kṛtatūryaghoṣe |
8.30.53 baddhvāñjaliṃ jaya jayeti tanuḥ pitṛbhyāṃ
8.30.54 tvaṃ tatkṣaṇe paṭutamaṃ vaṭurūpamādhāḥ ||
8.30.61 tāvatprajāpatimukhairupanīya mauñjī
8.30.62 daṇḍājinākṣavalayādibhirarcyamānaḥ |
8.30.63 dedīpyamānavapurīśa! kṛtāgnikāryas
8.30.64 tvaṃ prāsthithā baligṛhaṃ prakṛtāśvamedham ||
8.30.71 gātreṇa bhāvimahimocitagauravaṃ prāg
8.30.72 vyāvṛṇvateva dharaṇīṃ calayannayāsīḥ |
8.30.73 chatraṃ paroṣmatiraṇārthamivādadhāno
8.30.74 daṇḍaṃ ca dānavajaneṣvivaṃ sannidhātum ||
8.30.81 tāṃ narmadittarataṭe hayamedhaśālā
8.30.82 māseduṣi tvayi rucā tava ruddhanetraiḥ |
8.30.83 bhāsvān kimeṣa dahano nu sanatkumāro
8.30.84 yogī nu ko'yamiti śukramukhaiḥ śaśaṅke ||
8.30.91 ānītamāśu bhṛgubhirmahasābhibhūtais
8.30.92 tvāṃ ramyarūpamasuraḥ pulakāvṛtāṅgaḥ |
8.30.93 bhaktyā sametya sukṛtī pariṣicya pādau
8.30.94 tattoyamanvadhṛta mūrdhati tīrthatīrtham ||
8.30.101 prahlādavaṃśajatayā kratubhirdvijeṣu
8.30.102 viśvāsato nu tadidaṃ ditijo'pi lebhe |
8.30.103 yatte padāmbu giriśasya śirobhilālyaṃ
8.30.104 sa tvaṃ vibho! gurupurālaya! pālayethāḥ ||
8.31.11 prītyā daityastava tanumahaḥprekṣaṇītsarvathāpi
8.31.12 tvāmārādhyannajita! racayannañjaliṃ sañjagāda |
8.31.13 mattaḥ kiṃ te samabhilaṣitaṃ viprasūno! vada tvaṃ
8.31.14 vittaṃ bhaktaṃ bhavanamavanīṃ vāpi sarvaṃ pradāsye ||
8.31.21 tāmakṣīṇāṃ baligiramupākarṇya kāruṇyapūrṇo
8.31.22'pyasyotsekaṃ śamayitumanā daityavaṃśaṃ praśaṃsan |
8.31.23 bhūmiṃ pādatrayaparimitāṃ prārthayāmāsitha tvaṃ
8.31.24 sarvaṃ dehīti tu nigadite kasya hāsyaṃ na vā syāt ||
8.31.31 viśveśaṃ māṃ tripadamiha kiṃ yācase bāliśastvaṃ
8.31.32 sarvāṃ bhūmiṃ vṛṇu kimamunetyālapattvāṃ sa dṛpyan |
8.31.33 yasmāddarpāttripadaparipūrtyakṣamaḥ kṣepavādān
8.31.34 bandhaṃ cāsāvagamadatadarho'pi gāḍhopaśāntyai ||
8.31.41 pādatrayyā yadi na mudito viṣṭapairnāpi tuṣye
8.31.42 dityukte'smin varada! bhavate dātukāme'tha toyam |
8.31.43 daityācāryastava khalu parīkṣārthinaḥ preraṇāttaṃ
8.31.44 mā mā deyaṃ harirayamiti vyaktamevābabhāṣe ||
8.31.51 yācatyevaṃ yadi sa bhagavān pūrṇakāmo'smi so'haṃ
8.31.52 dāsyāmyeva sthiramiti vadan kāvyaśapto'pi daityaḥ |
8.31.53 vindhyāvalyā nijadayitayā dattapādyāya tubhyaṃ
8.31.54 citraṃ citraṃ sakalamapi sa prārpayattoyapūrvam ||
8.31.61 nissandehaṃ ditikulapatau tvayyaśeṣārpaṇaṃ tad
8.31.62 vyātanvāne mumucurṛṣayaḥ sāmarāḥ puṣpavarṣam |
8.31.63 divyaṃ rūpaṃ tava ca tadidaṃ paśyatāṃ viśvabhājām
8.31.64 uccairuccairavṛdhadavadhīkṛtya viśvāṇḍabhāṇḍam ||
8.31.71 tvatpādāgraṃ nijapadagataṃ puṇḍarīkodbhavo'sau
8.31.72 kuṇḍītoyairasicadapunādyajjalaṃ viśvalokān |
8.31.73 harṣotkarṣātsubahu khecarairutsave'smin
8.31.74 bherīṃ nighnan bhuvanamacarajjāmbavān bhaktiśālī ||
8.31.81 tāvaddaityāstvanumatimṛte bharturārabdhatuddhā
8.31.82 devopetairbhavadanucaraiḥ saṅgatā bhaṅgamāpan |
8.31.83 kālātmāyaṃ vasati purato yadvaśātprāgjitāḥ smaḥ
8.31.84 kiṃ vo yuddhairiti baligirā te'tha pātālamāpuḥ ||
8.31.91 pāśairbaddhaṃ patagapatinā daityamuccairavādī
8.31.92 stārtīyīkaṃ diśa mama padaṃ kiṃ na viśveśvaro'si |
8.31.93 pādaṃ mūrdhni praṇaya bhagavannityakampaṃ vadantaṃ
8.31.94 prahlādastaṃ svayamupagato mānayannastavīttvām ||
8.31.101 darpocchittyai vihitamakhilaṃ daitya! siddho'si puṇyair
8.31.102 lokaste'stu tridivavijayī vāsavatvaṃ ca paścāt |
8.31.103 matsāyujyaṃ bhaja ca punarityanvagṛhṇā baliṃ taṃ
8.31.104 vipraiḥ santānitamakhavaraḥ pāhi vātālayeśa! ||
8.32.11 purā hayagrīvamahāsureṇa ṣaṣṭhāntarāntodyadakāṇḍakalpe |
8.32.12 nidronmukhabrahmamukhāddhṛteṣu vedeṣvadhitsaḥ kila matsyarūpam ||
8.32.21 satyavratasya dramilādhibharturnadījale tarpayatastadānīm |
8.32.22 karāñjalau sa jvalitākṛtistvamadṛśyathāḥ kaścana bālamīnaḥ ||
8.32.31 kṣiptaṃ jale tvāṃ cakitaṃ vilokya ninye'nbupātraṇa muniḥ svageham |
8.32.32 svalpairahobhiḥ kalaśīṃ ca kūpaṃ vāpīṃ saraścānaśiṣe vibho! tvam ||
8.32.41 yogaprabhāvādbhavadājñayaiva nītastatastvaṃ muninā payodhim |
8.32.42 pṛṣṭo'munā kalpadidṛkṣumenaṃ saptāhamāssveti vadannayāsīḥ ||
8.32.51 prāpte tvadukte'hani vāridhārāpariplute bhūmitale munīndraḥ |
8.32.52 saptarṣibhiḥ sārdhamapāravāriṇyudghūrṇamānaḥ śaraṇaṃ yayau tvām ||
8.32.61 dharāṃ tvadādeśakarīmavāptāṃ naurūpiṇīmāruruhustadā te |
8.32.62 tatkampakampreṣu ca teṣu bhūyastvamambudherāvirabhūrmahīyān ||
8.32.71 jhaṣākṛtiṃ yojanalakṣadīrghāṃ dadhānamuccaistaratejasaṃ tvām |
8.32.72 nirīkṣya tuṣṭā munayastvaduktyā tvattuṅgaśṛṅge taraṇiṃ babandhuḥ ||
8.32.81 ākṛṣṭanauko munimaṇḍalāya pradarśayan viśvajagadvibhāgān |
8.32.82 saṃstūyamāno nṛvareṇa tena jṅānaṃ paraṃ copadiśannacārīḥ ||
8.32.91 kalpāvadhau sapta munīn purovatprastāpya satyavratabhūmipaṃ tam |
8.32.92 vaivasvatākhyaṃ manumādadhānaḥ krodhāddhayagrīvamabhidruto'bhūḥ ||
8.32.101 svatuṅgaśṛṅgakṣatavakṣasaṃ taṃ nipātya daityaṃ nigamān gṛhītvā |
8.32.102 viriñcaye prītahṛde dadānaḥ prabhañjanāgārapate! prapāyāḥ ||

9.33.11 vaivasvatākhyamanuputranabhāgajāta
9.33.12 nābhāganāmakanarendrasuto'mbarīṣuḥ |
9.33.13 saptārṇavāvṛtamahīdayito'pi reme
9.33.14 tvatsaṅgiṣu tvayi ca magnamanāḥ sadaiva ||
9.33.21 tvatprītayesakalameva vitanvato'sya
9.33.22 bhaktyaiva deva! nacirādabhṛthāḥ prasādam |
9.33.23 yenāsya yācanamṛte'pyabhirakṣaṇārthaṃ
9.33.24 cakraṃ bhavān pravitatāra sahasradhāram ||
9.33.31 sa dvādaśīvratamatho bhvadarcanārthaṃ
9.33.32 varṣaṃ dadhau madhuvane yamunopakaṇṭhe |
9.33.33 patnyā samaṃ sumanasā mahatīṃ vitandan
9.33.34 pūjāṃ dvijeṣu visṛjan paśuṣaṣṭikoṭim ||
9.33.41 tatrātha pāraṇadine bhavadarcanānte
9.33.42 durvāsasāsya muninā bhavanaṃ prapede |
9.33.43 bhoktuṃ vṛtaśca sa nṛpeṇa parārtiśīlo
9.33.44 mandaṃ jagāma yamunāṃ niyamān vidhāsyan ||
9.33.51 rājñātha pāraṇamuhṅrtasamāptikhedād
9.33.52 vāraiva pāraṇamakāri bhavatpareṇa |
9.33.53 prāpto munistadatha divyadṛśā vijānan
9.33.54 kṣipyan krudhoddhṛtajaṭo vitatāna kṛtyām ||
9.33.61 kṛtyāṃ ca tāmasidharāṃ bhuvanaṃ dahantī
9.33.62 magre'bhivīkṣya nṛpatirna padāccakampe |
9.33.63 tvadbhaktabādhamabhivīkṣya sudarśanaṃ te
9.33.64 kṛtyānalaṃ śalabhayanmunimanvadhāvīt ||
9.33.71 dhāvannaśeṣabhuvaneṣu bhiyā sa paśyan
9.33.72 viśvatra cakramapi te gatavān viriñcam |
9.33.73 kaḥ kālacakramatilaṅghayatītyapāstaḥ
9.33.74 śarvaṃ yayau sa ca bhavantamavandataiva ||
9.33.81 bhūyo bhavannilayametya muniṃ namantaṃ
9.33.82 proce bhavānahamṛṣe! nanu bhaktadāsaḥ |
9.33.83 jñānaṃ tapaśca vinayānvitameva mānyaṃ
9.33.84 yāhyambarīṣapadameva bhajeti bhūman! ||
9.33.91 tāvatsametya muninā sa gṛhītapādo
9.33.92 rājāpasṛtya bhavadastramasāva (nauṣī?nāvī) te |
9.33.93 cakre gate muniradādakhilāśiṣo'smai
9.33.94 tvadbhaktimāgasi kṛte'pi kṛpāṃ ca śaṃsan ||
9.33.101 rājā pratīkṣya munimekasamāmanāśvān
9.33.102 sambhojya sādhu tamṛṣiṃ visṛjan prasannam |
9.33.103 bhuktvā svayaṃ tvayi tato'pi dṛḍhaṃ rato'bhūt
9.33.104 sāyujyamāpa ca sa māṃ pavaneśa! pāyāḥ ||
9.34.11 gīrvāṇairarthyamāno daśamukhanidhanaṃ kosaleṣvṛśyaśṛṅge
9.34.12 putrīyāmiṣṭimiṣṭvā daduṣi daśarathakṣmābhṛte pāyasāgryam |
9.34.13 tadbhuktyā tatpurandhrīṣvapi tisṛṣu samaṃ jātagarbhāsu jāto
9.34.14 rāmastvaṃ lakṣmaṇena svayamatha bharatenāpi śatrughnanāmnā ||
9.34.21 kodaṇḍī kauśikasya kratuvaramavituṃ lakṣmaṇenānuyāto
9.34.22 yāto'bhūstātavācā munikathitamanudvandvaśāntādhvakhedaḥ |
9.34.23 n ṇāṃ trāṇāya bāṇairmunivacanabalāttāṭakāṃ pāṭayitvā
9.34.24 labdhvāsmādastrajālaṃ munivanamagamo deva! siddhāśramākhyam ||
9.34.31 mārīcaṃ drāvayitvā makhaśirasi śarairanyarakṣāṃsi nighnan
9.34.32 kalyāṃ kurvannahalyāṃ pathi padarajasā prāpya vaidehageham |
9.34.33 bhindānaścāndracūḍaṃ dhanuravanisutāmindirāmeva labdhvā
9.34.34 rāyaṃ prātiṣṭhathāstvaṃ tribhirapi ca samaṃ bhrātṛvīraiḥ sadāraiḥ ||
9.34.41 ārundhāne ruṣāndhe bhṛgukulatilake saṃkramayya svatejo
9.34.42 yāte yāto'syayodhyāṃ sukhamiha nivasan kāntayā kāntamūrte! |
9.34.43 śatrughnenaikadātho gatavati bharate mātulasyādhivāsaṃ
9.34.44 tātātrabdho'bhiṣekastava kila vihataḥ kekayādhīśaputryā ||
9.34.51 tātokyā yātukāmo vanamanujavadhūsaṃyutaścāpadhāraḥ
9.34.52 paurānārūdhya mārge guhanilayagatastvaṃ jaṭācīradhārī |
9.34.53 nāvā santīrya gaṅgāmadhipadavi punastaṃ bharadvājamārā
9.34.54 nnatvā tadvākyahetoratisukhamavasaścitrakūṭe girīndre ||
9.34.61 śrutvā putrārtikhinnaṃ khalu bharatamukhātsvargayātaṃ svatātaṃ
9.34.62 tapto dattvāmbu tasmai nidadhitha bharate pādukāṃ medinīṃ ca |
9.34.63 atriṃ natvātha gatvā vanamativipulāṃ daṇḍakāṃ caṇḍakāyaṃ
9.34.64 hatvā daityaṃ virādhaṃ sugatimakalayaścāru bhoḥ! śārabhaṅgīm ||
9.34.71 natvāgastyaṃ samastāśaranikarasapatrākṛtiṃ tāpasebhyaḥ
9.34.72 pratyaśrauṣīḥ priyaiṣī tadanu ca muninā vaiṣṇave divyacāpe |
9.34.73 brahmāstre cāpi datte pathi pitṛsuhṛdaṃ dīkṣya jaṭāyuṃ
9.34.74 modādgodātaṭānte pariramasi purā pañcavatyāṃ vadhūṭyā ||
9.34.81 prāptāyāḥ śūrpaṇakhyā madanacaladhṛterarthanairnissahātmā
9.34.82 tāṃ saumitrau visṛjya prabalatamaruṣā tena nirlunanāsām |
9.34.83 dṛṣṭvaināṃ ruṣṭacittaṃ kharamabhipatitaṃ duṣaṇaṃ ca trimūrdhaṃ
9.34.84 vyāhiṃsīrāśarānapyayutasamadhikāṃstatkṣaṇādakṣatoṣmā ||
9.34.91 sodaryāproktavārtāvivaśadaśamukhādiṣṭamārīcamāyā
9.34.92 sāraṅgaṃ sārasākṣyā spṛhitamanugataḥ prāvadhīrbāṇaghātam |
9.34.93 tanmāyākrandaniryāpitabhavadanujāṃ rāvaṇastāmahārṣīt
9.34.94 tenārto'pi tvamantaḥ kimapi mudamadhāstadvadhopāyāyalābhāt ||
9.34.101 bhūyastanvīṃ vicinvannahṛta daśamukhastvadvadhūṃ madvadhene
9.34.102 tyuktvā yāte jaṭāyau divamatha suhṛdaḥ prātanoḥ pretakāryam |
9.34.103 gṛhṇānaṃ taṃ kabandhaṃ jaghanitha śabarīṃ prekṣya pampātaṭe tvaṃ
9.34.104 samprāpto vātasūnuṃ bhṛśamuditamanāḥ pāhi vātālayeśa! ||
9.35.11 nītaḥ sugrīvamaitrīṃ tadanu dundubheḥ kāyamuccaiḥ
9.35.12 kṣiptvāṅguṣṭhena bhūyo lulavitha yugapatpatriṇā sapta sālān |
9.35.13 hatvā sugrīvaghātodyatamatulabalaṃ vālinaṃ vyājavṛttyā
9.35.14 varṣāvelāmanaiṣīrvirahataralaitastvaṃ mataṅgāśramānte ||
9.35.21 sugrīveṇānujoktyā sabhayamabhiyatā vyūhitāṃ vāhinīṃ tā
9.35.22 mṛkṣāṇāṃ vīkṣya dikṣu drutamatha dayitāmārgaṇāyāvanamrām |
9.35.23 sandeśaṃ cāngulīyaṃ pavanasutakare prādiśo modaśālī
9.35.24 mārge mārge mamārge kapibhirapi tadī tvatpriyā saprayāsaḥ ||
9.35.31 tvadvārtākarṇanodyadgarudurujavasampātisampātivākya
9.35.32 prottīrṇārṇodhirantarnagari janakajāṃ vīkṣya dattvāṅgulīyam |
9.35.33 prakṣudyodyānamakṣakṣapaṇacaṇaraṇaḥ soḍhabandho daśāsyaṃ
9.35.34 dṛṣṭvā pluṣṭvā ca laṅkāṃ jhaṭiti sa hanumānmauliratnaṃ dadau te ||
9.35.41 tvaṃ sugrīvāṅgadādiprabalakapicamūcakravikrāntabhūmi
9.35.42 cakro'bhikramya pārejaldhi niśicarendrānujāśrīyamāṇaḥ |
9.35.43 tatproktāṃ śatruvārtāṃ rahasi niśamayan prārthanāpārthyaroṣa
9.35.44 prāstāgneyāstratejastramadudadhigirā labdhavānmadhyamārgam ||
9.35.51 kīśairāśāntaropāhṛtagirinikaraiḥ setumādhāpya yāto
9.35.52 yātūnyāmardya daṃṣṭrānakhaśikhariśilāsālaśastraiḥ svasainyaiḥ |
9.35.53 vyākurvan sanujastvaṃ samarabhuvi paraṃ vikramaṃ śakrajetrā
9.35.54 vegānnāgāstrabaddhaḥ patagapatigarunmārutairmocito'bhūḥ ||
9.35.61 saumitristvatra śaktiprahṛtigaladasurvātajānītaśaila
9.35.62 ghrāṇātpraṇānupeto vyakṛṇuta kusṛtiślāghinaṃ meghanādam |
9.35.63 māyākṣobheṣu vaibhīṣaṇavacanahṛtastambhanaḥ kumbhakarṇaṃ
9.35.64 samprāptaṃ kampitorvītalamakhilacamūbhakṣiṇaṃ vyakṣiṇostvam ||
9.35.71 gṛhṇan jambhārisampreṣitarathakavacau rāvaṇenābhiyudhyan
9.35.72 brahmāstreṇāsya bhindan galatatimabalāmagniśuddhāṃ pragṛhṇan |
9.35.73 deva! śreṇīvarojjīvitasamaramṛtairakṣatairṛkṣasaṅghair
9.35.74 laṅkābhartrā ca sākaṃ nijanagaramagāḥ sapriyaḥ puṣpakeṇa ||
9.35.81 prīto divyābhiṣekairayutasamadhikān vatsarān paryaraṃsīr
9.35.82 maithilyāṃ pāpavācā śiva śiva kila tāṃ garbhiṇīmabhyahāsīḥ |
9.35.83 śatrughnenārdayitvā lavaṇaniśicaraṃ prārdayaḥ śūdrapāśaṃ
9.35.84 tāvadvālmīkigehe kṛtavasatirupāsūta sīta sutau te ||
9.35.91 vālmīkestvatsutodgāpitamadhurakṛterājñayā yajñavāṭe
9.35.92 sītāṃ tvayyāsukāme kṣitimaviśadasau tvaṃ ca kālārthito'bhūḥ |
9.35.93 hetoḥ saumitrighātī svayamatha sarayūmagnaniśśeṣabhṛtyaiḥ
9.35.94 sākaṃ nākaṃ prayāto nijapadamagamo deva! vaikuṇṭhamādyam ||
9.35.101 so'yaṃ martyāvatārastava khalu niyataṃ martyaśikṣārthamevaṃ
9.35.102 viśleṣārtirnirāgastyajanamapi bhavetkāmadharmātisaktyā |
9.35.103 no cetsvātmānubhūteḥ kvanu tava manaso vikriyā cakrapāṇe!
9.35.104 sa tvaṃ sattvaikamūrte! pavanapurapate! vyādhunu vyādhitāpān ||
9.36.11 atreḥ putratayā purā tvamanasūyāyāṃ hi dattābhidho
9.36.12 jātaḥ śiṣyāniṣandhatandritamanāḥ svasthaścaran kāntayā |
9.36.13 dṛṣto bhaktatamena hehayamahīpālena tasmai varā
9.36.14 naṣṭaiśvaryamukhān pradāya daditha svenaiva cānte vadham ||
9.36.21 satyaṃ kartumathārjunasya ca varaṃ tacchaktimātrānataṃ
9.36.22 brahmadveṣi tadākhilaṃ nṛpakulaṃ hantuṃ ca bhūmerbharam |
9.36.23 sañjāto jamadagnito bhṛgukule tvaṃ reṇukāyāṃ hare!
9.36.24 rāmo nāma tadātmajeṣvavarajaḥ pitroradhāḥ sammadam ||
9.36.31 labdhāmnāyagaṇaścaturdaśavayā gandharvarāje manā
9.36.32 gāsatāṃ kila mātaraṃ prati pituḥ krodhākulasyājñayā |
9.36.33 tātājñātigasodaraiḥ samamimāṃ chitvātha śāntātpitus
9.36.34 teṣāṃ jīvanayogamāpitha varaṃ mātā ca te'dādvaram ||
9.36.41 pitrā mātṛmude stavāhṛtaviyaddhenornijādāśramāt
9.36.42 prasthāyātha bhṛgorgirā himagirāvārādhya gaurīpatim |
9.36.43 labdhvā tatparaśuṃ taduktadanujacchedī mahāstrādikaṃ
9.36.44 prāpto mitramathākṛtavṛaṇamuniṃ prāpyāgamaḥ svāśramam ||
9.36.51 ākheṭepagato'rjunaḥ suragavīsamprāptasampadgaṇais
9.36.52 tvatpitrā paripūjitaḥ puragato durmantrivācā punaḥ |
9.36.53 gāṃ kretuṃ sacivaṃ nyayuṅkta kudhiyā tenāpi rundhanmuni
9.36.54 prāṇakṣepasaroṣagohatacamūcakreṇa vatso hṛtaḥ ||
9.36.61 śukrojjīvitatātavākyacalitakrodho'tha sakhyā samaṃ
9.36.62 bibhruddhyātamahodaropanihitaṃ cāpaṃ kuṭhāraṃ śaran |
9.36.63 ārūḍhaḥ sahavāhayantṛkarathaṃ māhiṣmatīmāviśan
9.36.64 vāgbhirvatsamadāśuṣi kṣitipatau samprāstuthāḥ saṅgaram ||
9.36.71 putrāṇāmayutenasaptadaśabhiścākṣauhiṇībhirmahā
9.36.72 senānībhiranekamitranivahirvyājṛmbhitīyodhanaḥ |
9.36.73 sadyastvatkakuṭhārabāṇavidalanniśśeṣasainyotkaro
9.36.74 bhītipradrutanaṣṭaśiṣṭanayastvāmāpataddhehayaḥ ||
9.36.81 līlāvāritanarmadājalavalallaṅkeśagarvāpaha
9.36.82 śrīmadbāhusahasramuktabahuśastrāstraṃ nirundhannamum |
9.36.83 cakre tvayyatha vaiṣṇave'pi vikale buddhvā hariṃ tvāṃ mudā
9.36.84 dhyāyantaṃ chitasrvadoṣamavadhīḥ so'gātparaṃ te padam ||
9.36.91 bhūyo'marṣitahehayātmajagaṇaistāte hate reṇukā
9.36.92 māghnānāṃ hṛdayaṃ nirīkṣya bahuśo ghorāṃ pratijñāṃ vahan |
9.36.93 dhyānānītarathāyudhastvamakṛthā vipradruhaḥ kṣatriyān
9.36.94 dikcakreṣu kuṭhārayan viśikhayanniḥkṣātriyāṃ medinīm ||
9.36.101 tātojjīvanakṛnnṛpālakakulaṃ triḥsaptakṛtvo jayan
9.36.102 santarpyātha samantapañcakamahāraktahṛdaudhe pitn |
9.36.103 yajñe kṣmāmapi kāśyapādiṣu diśan sālvena yudhyan punaḥ
9.36.104 kṛṣṇo'muṃ nihaniṣyatīti śamito yuddhātkumārairbhavān ||
9.36.111 nyasyāstrāṇi mahendrabhūbhṛti tapastanvan punarmajjitāṃ
9.36.112 gokarṇāvadhi sāgareṇa dharaṇīṃ dṛṣṭvārthitastāpasaiḥ |
9.36.113 dhyāteṣvāsaghṛtānalāstracakitaṃ sindhuṃ sruvakṣepaṇā
9.36.114 dutsāryoddhṛtakeralo bhṛgupate! vāteśa! saṃrakṣa mām ||
9.36.115 sāndrānanandatano! hare! nanu purā daivāsure saṅgare
9.36.116 tvatkṛttā api karmaśeṣavaśato ye te na yātā gatim |
9.36.117 teṣāṃ bhūtalajanmanāṃ ditibhuvāṃ bhāreṇa durārditā
9.36.118 bhūmiḥ prāpa viriñcamāśritapadaṃ devaiḥ puraivāgataiḥ ||

10.37.21 hā hā durjanabhūribhāramathitāṃ pāthonidhau pātukām
10.37.22 etāṃ pālaya hanta me vivaśatāṃ saṃpṛccha devānimān |
10.37.23 ityādipracurapralāpavivaśāmālokya dhātā mahīṃ
10.37.24 devānāṃ vadanāni vīkṣya parito dadhyau bhavantaṃ hare! ||
10.37.31 ūce cāmbujabhūramūnayi surāḥ! satyaṃ dharitryā vaco
10.37.32 nanvasyā bhavatāṃ ca rakṣaṇavidhau dakṣo hi lakṣmīpatiḥ |
10.37.33 sarve śarvapurassarā vayamito gatvā payovāridhiṃ
10.37.34 natvā taṃ stumahe javāditi yuyaḥ sākaṃ tavāketanam ||
10.37.41 te mugdhānilaśālidugdhajaladhestīraṃ gatāḥ saṅgatā
10.37.42 yāvattvatpadacintanaikamanasastāvatsa pāthojabhūh |
10.37.43 tvadvācaṃ hṛdaye niśamya sakalānānandayannacivā
10.37.44 nākhyātaḥ paramātmanā svayamahaṃ vākyaṃ tadākarṇyatām ||
10.37.51 jāne dīnadaśāmahaṃ diviṣadāṃ bhūmeśca bhīmairnṛpais
10.37.52 tatkṣepāya bhavāmi yādavakule so'haṃ samagrātmanā |
10.37.53 devā vṛṣṇikule bhavantu kalayā devāṅganāścāvanau
10.37.54 matsevārthamiti tvadīyavacanaṃ pāthojabhūrūcivān ||
10.37.61 śrutvā karṇarasāyanaṃ tava vacaḥ sarveṣu nirvāpita
10.37.62 svānteṣvīśa! gateṣui tāvakakṛpāpīyūṣatṛptātmasu |
10.37.63 vikhyāte mathurāpure kila bhavatsānnidhyapuṇyottare
10.37.64 dhanyāṃ devakanandanāmudavahadrājā sa śūrātmajaḥ ||
10.37.71 udvāhāvasitau tadīyasahajaḥ kaṃso'tha sammānaya
10.37.72 nnetau sūtatayā gataḥ pathi rathe vyomotthayā tvadgirā |
10.37.73 asyāstvāmatiduṣṭamaṣṭamasuto hanteti hanteritaḥ
10.37.74 sattrāsātsa tu hantumantikagatāḥ tanvīṃ kṛpāṇīmadhāt ||
10.37.81 gṛhṇānaścikureṣu tāṃ khalamatiḥ śaureściraṃ sāntvanair
10.37.82 no muñcan punarātmajārpaṇagirā prīto'tha yāto gṛhān |
10.37.83 ādyaṃ tvatsahajaṃ tathārpitamapi snehena nāhannasau
10.37.84 duṣṭānāmapi deva! puṣṭakaruṇā dṛṣṭā hi dhīrekadā ||
10.37.91 tāvattvanmanasaiva nāradamuniḥ proce sa bhojeśvaraṃ
10.37.92 yūyaṃ nanvasurāḥ surāśca yadavo jānāsi kiṃ na prabho! |
10.37.93 māyāvī sa harirbhavadvadhakṛte bhāvī suraprārthanā
10.37.94 dityākarṇya yadūnadūdhunadasau śaureśca sūnūnahan ||
10.37.101 prāpte saptamagarbhatāmahipatau tvatpreraṇānmāyayā
10.37.102 nīte mādhava! rohiṇīṃ tvamapi bhoḥ! saccitsukhaikātmakaḥ |
10.37.103 devakyā jaṭharaṃ viveśitha vibho! saṃstūyamānaḥ suraiḥ
10.37.104 sa tvaṃ kṛṣṇa! vidhūya rogapaṭalīṃ bhaktiṃ parāṃ dehi me ||
10.38.11 ānandarūpa! bhagavannayi! te'vatāre
10.38.12 prāpte pradīptabhavadaṅga nirīyamāṇaiḥ |
10.38.13 kāntivrajairiva ghanāghanamaṇḍalairdyā
10.38.14 māvṛṇvatī viruruce kila varṣavelā ||
10.38.21 āśāsu śītalatarāsu payodatoyai
10.38.22 rāśāsitāptivivaśeṣu ca sajjaneṣu |
10.38.23 naiśākarodayavidhau niśi madhyamāyāṃ
10.38.24 kleśāpahastrijagatāṃ tvamihāvirāsīḥ ||
10.38.31 bālyasṛśāpi vapuṣā dadhuṣā vubhūtī
10.38.32 rudyatkirīṭakaṭakāṅgadahārabhāsā |
10.38.33 śaṅkhārivārijagadāparibhāsitena
10.38.34 meghāsitena parilesitha sūtigehe ||
10.38.41 vakṣaḥsthalīsukhanilānavilāsilakṣmī
10.38.42 mandākṣalakṣitakaṭākṣavimokṣabhedaiḥ |
10.38.43 tanmandirasya khalakaṃsakṛtāmalakṣmī
10.38.44 munmārjayanniva virejitha vāsudeva! ||
10.38.51 śauristu dhīramunimaṇḍalacetaso'pi
10.38.52 dūrasthitaṃ vapurudīkṣya nijekṣaṇābhyām |
10.38.53 ānandabaṣpapulakodgamagadgadārdra
10.38.54 stuṣṭāva dṛṣtimakarandarasaṃ bhavantam ||
10.38.61 deva! prasīda parapūruṣa! tāpavallī
10.38.62 nirlūnidātra! samanetra! kalāvilāsin! |
10.38.63 khedānapākuru kṛpāgurubhiḥ kaṭākṣair
10.38.64 ityādi tena muditena ciraṃ nuto'bhūḥ ||
10.38.71 mātrā ca netrasalilāstṛtagātravallyā
10.38.72 stotrairabhiṣṭutaguṇaḥ karuṇālayastvam |
10.38.73 prācīnajanmayugalaṃ pratibodhya tābhyāṃ
10.38.74 māturgirā dadhitha mānuṣabālaveṣam ||
10.38.81 tvatpreristatadanu nandatanūjayā te
10.38.82 vyatyāsamāracayituṃ sa hi śūrasūnuḥ |
10.38.83 tvāṃ hastayoradhita cittāvidhāryamāryai
10.38.84 rambhoruhasthakalahaṃsakiśoraramyam ||
10.38.91 jātā tadā puśupasadmani yoganidrā
10.38.92 nidrāvimudritamathākṛta pauralokam |
10.38.93 tvatpreraṇātkimiha citramacetanairyad
10.38.94 dvāraiḥ svayaṃ vyaghaṭi saṅgaṭitaiḥ sugāḍham ||
10.38.101 śeṣeṇa bhūriphaṇavāritavāriṇātha
10.38.102 svairaṃ pradarśitapatho maṇidīpitena |
10.38.103 tvāṃ dhārayan sa khalu dhanyatamaḥ pratasthe
10.38.104 so'yaṃ tvamīśa! mama nāśaya rogavegān ||
10.39.11 bhavantamayamudvahan yadukulodvaho nissaran
10.39.12 dadarśa gaganoccalajjalabharāṃ kalindātmajām |
10.39.13 ahi salilasañcayaḥ sa punaraindrajālodito
10.39.14 jalaugha iva tatkṣaṇātprapadameyatāmāyayau ||
10.39.21 prasuptapaśupālikāṃ nibhṛtamārudadbālikā
10.39.22 mapāvṛtakavāṭikāṃ paśupavāṭikāmāviśan |
10.39.23 bhavantamayamarpayan prasavatalpake tatpadād
10.39.24 vahan kapaṭakanyakāṃ svapuramāgato vegataḥ ||
10.39.31 tatastvadanujāravakṣapitanidravegadravad
10.39.32 bhaṭotkaraniveditaprasavavārtayaivārtimān |
10.39.33 vimuktacikurotkarastvaritamāpatan bhojarā
10.39.34 ḍatuṣṭa iva dṛṣṭavān bhaginikākare kanyakām ||
10.39.41 dhruvaṃ kapaṭaśālino madhuharasya māyā bhave
10.39.42 dasāviti kiśorikāṃ bhaginikākarāliṅgitām |
10.39.43 dvipo nalinikāntarādiva mṛṇālikāmākṣipa
10.39.44 nnayaṃ tvadanujāmajāmupalapaṭṭake piṣṭavān ||
10.39.51 tato bhavadupāsako jhaṭiti mṛtyupāśādiva
10.39.52 pramucya tarasaiva sā samadhirūḍharūpāntarā |
10.39.53 adhastalamajagmuṣī vikasadaṣṭabāhusphuran
10.39.54 mahāyudhamaho gatā kila vihāyasā didyute ||
10.39.61 nṛśaṃsatara! kaṃsa! te kimu mayā viniṣpiṣṭayā
10.39.62 babhūva bhavadantakaḥ kvacana cintyatāṃ te hitam |
10.39.63 iti tvadanujā vibho! khalamudīrya taṃ jagmuṣī
10.39.64 marudgaṇapaṇāyitā bhuvi ca mandirāṇyeyuṣī ||
10.39.71 prage punaragātmajāvacanamīriti bhūbhujā
10.39.72 pralambabakapūtanāpramukhadānavā māninaḥ |
10.39.73 bhavannidhanakāmyayā jagati babhramurnirbhayāḥ
10.39.74 kumārakavimārakāḥ kimiva duṣkaraṃ niṣkṛpaiḥ ||
10.39.81 tataḥ paśupamandire tvayi mukunda! nandapriyā
10.39.82 prasūtiśayaneśaye ruvati kiñcidañcatpade |
10.39.83 vibudhya vanitājanaistanayasambhave ghoṣite
10.39.84 mudā kimu vadāmyaho sakalamākulaṃ gokulam ||
10.39.91 aho khalu yaśodayā navakalāyacetoharaṃ
10.39.92 bhavantamalamantike prathamamāpibantyā dṛśā |
10.39.93 punaḥ stanabharaṃ nijaṃ sapadi pāyayantyā mudā
10.39.94 manoharatanuspṛśā jagati puṇyavanto jitāḥ ||
10.39.101 bhavatkuśalakāmyayā sa khalu nandagopastadā
10.39.102 pramodabharasaṃkulo dvijakulāya kiṃ nādadāt |
10.39.103 tathaiva paśupālakāḥ kimu na maṅgalaṃ tenire
10.39.104 jagatritayamaṅgala! tvamiha pāhi māmāmayāt ||
10.40.11 tadanu nandamamandaśubhāspadaṃ nṛpapurīṃ karadānakṛte gatam |
10.40.12 samavalokya jagādbhavatpitā viditakaṃsasahāyajanodyamaḥ ||
10.40.21 ayi sakhe! tava bālakajanma māṃ sukhayate'dya nijātmajajanmavat |
10.40.22 iti bhavatpitṛtāṃ vrajanāyake samadhiropya śaśaṃsa tamādarāt ||
10.40.31 iha ca santyanimittaśatāni te kaṭakasīmne tato laghu gamyatām |
10.40.32 iti ca tadvacasā vrajanāyako bhavadapāyabhiyā drutamāyayau ||
10.40.41 avasare khalu tatra ca kācana vrajapade madhurākṛtiraṅganā |
10.40.42 taralaṣaṭpadalālitakuntalā kapaṭapotaka! te nikaṭaṃ gatā ||
10.40.51 sapasi sā hṛtabālakacetanā niśicarānvayajā kila pūtanā |
10.40.52 vrajavadhūṣviha keyamiti kṣaṇaṃ vimṛśatīṣu bhavantamupādade ||
10.40.61 lalitabhāvavilāsahṛtātmabhiryuvatibhiḥ pratiroddhumapāritā |
10.40.62 stanamasau bhavanāntaniṣeduṣī pradaduṣī bhavate kapaṭātmane ||
10.40.71 samadhiruhya tadaṅkamaśaṅkitastvamatha bālakalopanaroṣitaḥ |
10.40.72 mahadivāmraphalaṃ kucamaṇḍalaṃ praticucūṣitha durviṣadūṣitam ||
10.40.81 asubhireva samaṃ dhayati tvayi stanamasau stanitopamanisvanā |
10.40.82 nirapatadbhayadāyi nijaṃ vapuḥ pratigatā pravisārya bhujāvubhau ||
10.40.91 bhayadaghoṣaṇabhīṣaṇavigrahaśravaṇadarśanamohitavallave |
10.40.92 vrajapade taduraḥsthalakhelanaṃ nanu bhavantamagṛhṇata gopikāḥ ||
10.40.101 bhuvanamaṅkala!nāmabhireva te yuvatibhirbahudhā kṛtarakṣaṇaḥ |
10.40.102 tvamayi vātaniketananātha! māmagadayan kuru tāvakasevakam ||
10.41.11 vrajeśvaraḥ śaurivaco niśamya samāvrajannadhvani bhītacetāḥ |
10.41.12 niṣpiṣṭaniśśeṣataruṃ nirīkṣya kañcitpadārthaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatastvām ||
10.41.21 niśamya gopīvacanādudantaṃ sarve'pi gopā bhayavismayāndhāḥ |
10.41.22 tvatpātitaṃ ghorapiśācadehaṃ dehurvidūre'tha kuṭhārakṛttam ||
10.41.31 tvatpītapūtastanataccharīrātsamuccalannuccataro hi dhūmaḥ |
10.41.32 śaṅkāmadhādāgaravaḥ kimeṣu kiṃ cāndano gauggulavo'thaveti ||
