10.41.41 madaṅgasaṅgasya phalaṃ na dūraṃ kṣaṇena tāvadbhavatāmapi syāt |
10.41.42 utyullapan vallavatallajebhyastvaṃ pūtanāmātanuthāḥ sugandhim ||
10.41.51 citraṃ piśācyā na hataḥ kumāraścitraṃ puraivākathi śauriṇedam |
10.41.52 iti praśaṃsan kila gopaloko bhavanmukhālokarase nyamāṅkṣīt ||
10.41.61 dine dine'tha prativṛddhalakṣmīrakṣīṇamaṅgalyaśato vrajo'yam |
10.41.62 bhavannivāsādayi vāsudeva! pramodasāndraḥ parito vireje ||
10.41.71 gṛheṣu te komalarūpahāsamithaḥkathāsaṅkulitāḥ kamanyaḥ |
10.41.72 vṛtteṣu kṛtyeṣu bhavannirīkṣāsamāgatāḥ pratyahamatyanandan ||
10.41.81 aho kumāro mayi dattadṛṣṭiḥ smitaḥ kṛtaṃ māṃ prati vatsakena |
10.41.82 ehyehi māmityupasārya pāṇiṃ tvayāśa! kiṃ kiṃ na kṛtaṃ vadhūbhiḥ ||
10.41.91 bhavadvapuḥsparśanakautukena karātkaraṃ gopavadhūjanena |
10.41.92 nītastvamātāmrasarojamālāvyālambilolambatulāmalāsīḥ ||
10.41.101 nipāyayantī stanamaṅkagaṃ tvāṃ vilokayantī vadanaṃ hasantī |
10.41.102 daśāṃ yaśodā katamāṃ na bheje sa tādṛśaḥ pāhi hare! gadānmām ||
10.42.11 kadāpi janmarkṣadine tava prabho! nimantritajñātivadhūmahīsurā |
10.42.12 mahānasastvāṃ savidhe nidhāya sā mahānasādau vavṛte vrajeśvarī ||
10.42.21 tato bhavattrāṇaniyuktabālakaprabhītisaṅkrandanasaṅkulāravaiḥ |
10.42.22 vimiśramaśrāvi bhavatsamīpataḥ parisphuṭaddārucaṭaccaṭāravaḥ ||
10.42.31 tatastadākarṇanasaṃbhramaśramaprakampivakṣojabharā vrajāṅganāḥ |
10.42.32 bhavantamantardadṛśuḥ samantato viniṣpataddāruṇadārumadhyagam ||
10.42.41 śiśoraho kiṃ kimabhūditi drutaṃ pradhāvya nandaḥ paśupāśca bhūsurāḥ |
10.42.42 bhavantamālokya yaśodayā dhṛtaṃ samāśvasannaśrujalārdralocanāḥ ||
10.42.51 kasko nu kautaskuta eṣa vismayo viśaṅkaṭaṃ yacchakaṭaṃ vipāṭitam |
10.42.52 na kāraṇaṃ kiñcidiheti te sthitāḥ svanāsikādattakarāstvadīkṣakāḥ ||
10.42.61 kumārakasyāsya payodharārthinaḥ prarodane lolapadāmbujāhatam |
10.42.62 mayā mayā dṛṣṭamano viparyagāditīśa! te pālakabālakā jaguḥ ||
10.42.71 bhiyā tadā kiñcidajānatāmidaṃ kumārakāṇāmatidurghaṭaṃ vacaḥ |
10.42.72 bhavatprabhāvāvidurairitīritaṃ manāgivāśaṅkyāta dṛṣṭapūtanaiḥ ||
10.42.81 pravālatāmraṃ kimidaṃ padaṃ kṣataṃ sarojaramyau nu karau virojitau |
10.42.82 iti prasarpatkaruṇātaraṅgitāstvadaṅgamāpaspṛśuraṅganājanāḥ ||
10.42.91 aye sutaṃ dehi jagatpateḥ kṛpātaraṅgapātātparipātamadya me |
10.42.92 iti sma saṅgṛhya pitā tvadaṅgukaṃ guhurmuhuḥ śliṣyati jītakaṇṭakaḥ ||
10.42.101 anonilīnaḥ kila hantumāgataḥ surārirevaṃ bhavatā vihiṃsitaḥ |
10.42.102 rajo'pi nodṛṣṭamamuṣya tatkathaṃ sa śuddhasattve tvayi līnavān dhruvam ||
10.42.111 prapūjitaistatra tato dvijātibhirviśeṣato lambhitamaṅgalāśiṣaḥ |
10.42.112 vrajaṃ nijairbālyarasairvimohayanmarutpurādhīśa! rujāṃ jahīhi me ||
10.43.11 tvamekadā gurumarutpuranātha! voḍhuṃ
10.43.12 gāḍhādhirūḍḥgarimāṇamapārayantī |
10.43.13 mātā nodhāya śayane kimidaṃ bateti
10.43.14 dhyāyantyaceṣṭata gṛheṣu niviṣṭaśaṅkā ||
10.43.21 tāvadvidūramupakarṇitaghoraghoṣa
10.43.22 vyājṛmbhipāṃsupaṭalīparipūritāśaḥ |
10.43.23 vātyāvapuḥ sa kila daityavarastṛṇāva
10.43.24 rtākhyo jahāra janamānasahāriṇaṃ tvām ||
10.43.31 uddāmapāṃsutimirāhatadṛṣtipāte
10.43.32 draṣṭuṃ kimapyakuśale paśupālaloke |
10.43.33 hā bālaksya kimiti tvadupāntamāptā
10.43.34 mātā bhavantamavilokya bhṛśaṃ rurod ||
10.43.41 tāvatsa dānavavaro'pi ca dīnamūrtir
10.43.42 bhāvatkabhāraparidhāraṇalūnavegaḥ |
10.43.43 saṅkocamāpa tadanu kṣatapāṃsughoṣe
10.43.44 ghoṣe vyatāyata bhavajjananīninādaḥ ||
10.43.51 rodopakarṇanavaśādupagamya gehaṃ
10.43.52 krandatsu nandamukhagopakuleṣu dīnaḥ |
10.43.53 tvāṃ dānavastvakhilamuktikaraṃ mumukṣus
10.43.54 tvayyapramuñcati papātviyatpradeśāt ||
10.43.61 rodākulāstadanu gopagaṇā bahiṣṭha
10.43.62 pāṣāṇapṛṣṭhabhuvi dehamatisthaviṣṭham |
10.43.63 praikṣanta hanta nipantamamuṣya vakṣa
10.43.64 syakṣīṇameva ca bhavantamalaṃ hasantam ||
10.43.71 grāvaprapātaparipiṣṭagariṣṭhadeha
10.43.72 bhraṣṭāsuduṣṭadanujopari dhṛṣṭahāsam |
10.43.73 āghnānamambujakareṇa bhavantametya
10.43.74 gopa dadhurgirivarādiva nīlaratnam ||
10.43.81 ekaikamāśu parigṛhya nikāmananda
10.43.82 nnandādigopaparirabdhavicumbatāṅgam |
10.43.83 ādātukāmapariśaṅkitagopanārī
10.43.84 hastāmbujaprapatitaṃ praṇumo bhavantam ||
10.43.91 bhūyo'pi kinnu kṛṇumaḥ praṇatārtihārī
10.43.92 govinda eva paripālayatātsutaṃ naḥ |
10.43.93 ityādi mātarapitṛprasukhaistadānīṃ
10.43.94 samprārthitastvadavanāya vibho! tvameva ||
10.43.101 vātātmakaṃ danujamevamayi pradhūnvan
10.43.102 vātodbhavānmama gadān kimu no dhunoṣi |
10.43.103 kiṃ vā karomi puranapyanilālayeśa!
10.43.104 niśśeṣarogaśamanaṃ muhurarthaye tvām ||
10.44.11 gūḍhaṃ vasudevagirā kartuṃ te niṣkriyasya saṃskārān |
10.44.12 hṛdgatahorātattvo gargamunistvadgṛhān vibho! gatavān ||
10.44.21 nando'tha nanditātmā bṛndiṣṭhaṃ mānayannamuṃ yaminām |
10.44.22 mandasmitārdramūce tvatsaṃskārān vidhātumutsukadhīḥ ||
10.44.31 yaduvaṃśācāryatvātsunibhṛtamidamārya! kāryamiti kathayan |
10.44.32 gargo nirgatapulakaścakre tava sāgrajasya nāmāni ||
10.44.41 kathamasya nāma kurve sahasranāmno hyanantanāmno vā |
10.44.42 iti nūnaṃ gargamuścakre tava nāma nāma rahasi vibho! ||
10.44.51 kṛṣidhītuṇakārābhyāṃ sattānandātmatāṃ kilābhilapat |
10.44.52 jagadaghakarṣitvaṃ vā kathayadṛṣiḥ kṛṣṇanāma te vyatanot ||
10.44.61 anyāṃśca nāmabhedānnyīkurvannagraje ca rāmādīn |
10.44.62 atimānuṣānubhāvaṃ nyagadattvāmaprakāśayan pitre ||
10.44.71 snihyati yatsava putre suhyati sa na māyikaiḥ punaḥ śokaiḥ |
10.44.72 druhyati yaḥ sa tu naśyedityavadatte mahattvamṛṣivaryaḥ ||
10.44.81 jeṣyati bahutaradaityānneṣyati nijabandhulokamamalapadam |
10.44.82 śroṣyasi suvimalakīrtīrasyeti bhavadvibhūtimṛṣirūce ||
10.44.91 amunaiva sarvadurgaṃ taritāstha kṛtāsthamantra tiṣṭhadhvam |
10.44.92 harirevetyanabhilapannityādi tvāmavarṇayatsa muniḥ ||
10.44.101 garge'tha nirgate'sminnanditanandādinandyamānastvam |
10.44.102 madgadamudgatakaruṇo nirgamaya śrīmarutpurādhīśa! ||
10.45.11 ayi sabala! murāre! pāṇijānupracāraiḥ
10.45.12 kimapi bhavanabhāgān bhūṣayantau bhavantau |
10.45.13 calitacaraṇakañje mañjumañjīraśiñjā
10.45.14 śravaṇakutukabhājau ceratuścāru vegāt ||
10.45.21 mṛdu mṛdu vihasantāvunmiṣaddantavantau
10.45.22 vadanapatitakeśau dṛśyapādābjadeśau |
10.45.23 bhujagalitakarāntavyālagatkaṅkaṇāṅkau
10.45.24 matimaharatamuccaiḥ paśyatāṃ viśvan ṇām ||
10.45.31 anusarati janaughe kautukavyākulākṣe
10.45.32 kimapi kṛtaninādaṃ vyāhasantau dravantau |
10.45.33 balitavadanapadmaṃ pṛṣṭhato dattadṛṣṭī
10.45.34 kimiva na vidadhāthe kautukaṃ vāsudeva! ||
10.45.41 dutagatiṣu patantāvutthitau liptapaṅkau
10.45.42 divi munibhirapaṅkaiḥ sasmitaṃ vandyamānau |
10.45.43 drutamatha jananībhyāṃ sānukampaṃ gṛhītau
10.45.44 muhurapi parirabdhau drāgyuvāṃ cumbitau ca ||
10.45.51 snutakucabharamaṅke dhārayantī bhavantaṃ
10.45.52 taralamati yaśodā stanyadā dhanyadhanyā |
10.45.53 kapaṭapaśupa! madhye mugdhahāsāṅkuraṃ te
10.45.54 daśanamukulahṛdyaṃ vīkṣyaṃ vaktraṃ jaharṣa ||
10.45.61 tadanu caraṇacārī dārakaiḥ sākamārā
10.45.62 nnilayatatiṣu khelan bālacāpalyaśālī |
10.45.63 bhavanaśukabiḍālān vatsakāṃścānudhāvan
10.45.64 kathamapi kṛtahāsairgopakairvārito'bhūḥ ||
10.45.71 haladharasahitastvaṃ yatra yatropayāto
10.45.72 vivaśapatitanetrāstatra tatraiva gopyaḥ |
10.45.73 vigalitagṛhakṛtyā vismṛtāpatyabhṛtyā
10.45.74 murahara! muhuratyantākulā nityamāsan ||
10.45.81 pratinavanavanītaṃ gopikādattamicchan
10.45.82 kalapadamupagāyan komalaṃ kvāpi nṛtyan |
10.45.83 sadayayuvatilokairarpitaṃ sarpiraśnan
10.45.84 kvacana navavipakvaṃ dugdhamatyāpibastvam ||
10.45.91 mama khalu baligehe yācanaṃ jātamāstām
10.45.92 iha punarabalānāmagrato naiva kurve |
10.45.93 iti vihitamatiḥ kiṃ deva! santyajya yācñāṃ
10.45.94 dadhighṛtamaharastvaṃ cāruṇā coraṇena ||
10.45.101 tava dadhighṛtamoṣe ghoṣayoṣājanānā
10.45.102 mabhajata hṛdi roṣo nāvakāśaṃ na śokaḥ |
10.45.103 hṛdayamapi muṣitvā harṣasindhau nyadhāstvaṃ
10.45.104 sa mama śamaya rogān vātagehādhinātha! ||
10.45.111 śākhāgre'tha vidhuṃ vilokya phalamityambāṃ ca tātaṃ muhuḥ
10.45.112 samprārthyātha tadā tadīyavacasā protkṣiptabāhau tvayi |
10.45.113 citraṃ deva! śaśī sa te karamagātkiṃ brūmahe sampata
10.45.114 jjyotirmaṇḍalapūritākhilavapuḥ prāgā virāḍrūpatām ||
10.45.121 kiṃ kiṃ batedamiti saṃbhramabhājamenaṃ
10.45.122 brahmārṇave kṣaṇamamuṃ parimajjya tātam |
10.45.123 māyāṃ punastanayamohamayīṃ vitanva
10.45.124 nnānandacinmaya! jaganmaya! pāhi rogāt ||
10.46.11 ayi deva! purā kila tvayi svayamuttānaśaye stanandhaye |
10.46.12 parijṛmbhaṇato vyapāvṛte vadane viśvamacaṣṭa vallavī ||
10.46.21 punarapyatha bālakaiḥ samaṃ tvayi līlānirate jagatpate! |
10.46.22 phalasañcayavañcanakrudhā tava mṛdbhojanamūcurarbhakāḥ ||
10.46.31 ayi te pralayāvadhau vibho! kṣititoyādisamastabhakṣiṇaḥ |
10.46.32 mṛdupāśanato rujā bhavediti bhītā jananī cukopa sā |||
10.46.41 ayi durvinayātmaka! tvayā kimu mṛtsā bata vatsa! bhakṣitā |
10.46.42 iti mātṛgiraṃ ciraṃ vibho! vitathāṃ tvaṃ pratijajñiṣe hasan ||
10.46.51 ayi te sakalairviniścite vimatiścedvadanaṃ vidāryatām |
10.46.52 iti mātṛvibhartsito mukhaṃ vikasatpadmanibhaṃ vyadārayaḥ ||
10.46.61 ayi mṛllavadarśanotsukāṃ jananīṃ tāṃ bahu tarpayanniva |
10.46.62 pṛthivīṃ nikhilāṃ na kevalaṃ bhuvanānyapyakhilānyadīdṛśaḥ ||
10.46.71 kuhacidvanamambudhiḥ kvacitkvacidabhraṃ kuhacidrasātalam |
10.46.72 manujā danujāḥ kvacitsurā dadṛśe kiṃ na tadā tvadānane ||
10.46.81 kalaśāmbudhiśāyinaṃ punaḥ paravaikuṇṭhapadādhivāsinam |
10.46.82 svapuraśca nijārbhakātmakaṃ katidhā tvāṃ na dadarśa sā mukhe ||
10.46.91 vikasadbhuvane mukhodare nanu bhūyo'pi tathāvidhānanaḥ |
10.46.92 anayā sphuṭamīkṣito bhavānanavasthāṃ jagatāṃ batātanot ||
10.46.101 dhṛtatattvadhiyaṃ tadā kṣaṇaṃ jananīṃ tāṃ praṇayena mohayan |
10.46.102 stanamamba! diśetyupāsajan bhagavannadbhutabāla! pāhi mām ||
10.47.11 ekadā dadhivimāthakāriṇīṃ mātaraṃ samupasedivān bhavān |
10.47.12 stanyalolupatayā nivārayannaṅkametya papivān payodharau ||
10.47.21 ardhapītakucakuḍmale tvayi snigdhahāsamadhurānanāmbuje |
10.47.22 dugdhamīśa! dahane parisnutaṃ dhartumāśu jananī jagāma te ||
10.47.31 sāmipītarasabhaṅgasaṅgatakrodhabhāraparibhūtacetasā |
10.47.32 manthadaṇḍamupagṛhya pāṭitaṃ hanta deva! dadhibhājanaṃ tvayā ||
10.47.41 uccala dhvanitamuccakaistadā saṃniśamya jananī samādrutā |
10.47.42 tvadyaśovisaravaddadarśa sā sadya eva dadhi visṛtaṃ kṣitau ||
10.47.51 vedamārgaparimārgitaṃ ruṣā tvāmavīkṣya parimārgayantyasau |
10.47.52 sandadarśa sukṛtinyulūkhale dīyamānanavanītamotave ||
10.47.61 tvāṃ pragṛhya bata bhītibhāvanābhāsurānanasarojamāśu sā |
10.47.62 roṣarūṣitamukhī sakhīpuro bandhanāya raśanāmupādade ||
10.47.71 bandhumicchati yameva sajjanastaṃ bhavantamayi bandhumicchatī |
10.47.72 sā niyujya raśanāguṇān bahūn vdyaṅgulonamakhilaṃ kilaikṣata ||
10.47.81 vismitotsmitasakhījanekṣitāṃ svinnasannavapuṣaṃ nirīkṣya tām |
10.47.82 nityamuktavapurapyaho hare! bandhameva kṛpayānvamayathāḥ ||
10.47.91 sthīyatāṃ ciramulūkhale khaletyāgatā bhavanameva sā yadā |
10.47.92 prāgulūkhalabilāntare tadā sarpirarpitamadannavāsthithāḥ ||
10.47.101 yadyapāśasugamo bhavān vibho! saṃyataḥ kimu sapāśayānayā |
10.47.102 evamādi divijairabhiṣṭuto vātanātha! paripāhi māṃ gadāt ||
10.48.11 mudā suraudhaistvamudārasammadairudīrya dāmodara ityabhiṣṭutaḥ |
10.48.12 mṛdūdaraḥ svairamulūkhale lagannadūrato dvau kakubhāvudaikṣathāḥ ||
10.48.21 kuberasūnurnalakūbarābhidhaḥ paro maṇigrīva iti prathāṃ gataḥ |
10.48.22 maheśasevādhigataśriyonmadau ciraṃ kila tvadvimukhāvakhelatām ||
10.48.31 surāpagāyāṃ kila tau madotkaṭau surāpagāyadbahuyauvatāvṛtau |
10.48.32 vivāsasau keliparau sa nārado bhavatpadaikapravaṇo niraikṣata ||
10.48.41 bhiyā priyālokamupāttavāsasaṃ puro nirīkṣyāpi madāndhacetasau |
10.48.42 imau bhavadbhaktyupaśāntisiddhaye munirjagau śāntimṛte kutaḥ sukham ||
10.48.51 yuvāmavāptau kakubhātmatāṃ ciraṃ hariṃ nirīkṣyātha padaṃ svamāpnutam |
10.48.52 itīritau tau bhavadīkṣaṇaspṛhāṃ gatau vrajānte kakubhau babhūvatuḥ ||
10.48.61 atandramindradruyugaṃ tathāvidhaṃ sameyuṣā mantharagāminā tvayā |
10.48.62 tirāyitolūkhalarodhanirdhutau cirāya jīrṇau paripātitau tarū ||
10.48.71 abhāji śākhidvitayaṃ yadā tvayā tadaiva tadgarbhatalānnireyuṣā |
10.48.72 mahātviṣā yakṣayugena tatkṣaṇādabhāji govinda! bhavānapi stavaiḥ ||
10.48.81 ihānyabhakto'pi sameṣyati kramādbhavantametau khalu rudrasevakau |
10.48.82 muniprasādādbhavadaṅghrimāgatau gatau vṛṇānau khalu bhaktimuttamām ||
10.48.91 tatastarūddāraṇadāruṇāravaprakampisampātini gopamaṇḍale |
10.48.92 vilajjitatvajjananīmukhekṣiṇā vyamokṣi nandane bhavān vimokṣadaḥ ||
10.48.101 mahīruhormadhyagato batārbhako hareḥ prabhāvādaparikṣato'dhunā |
10.48.102 iti bravāṇairgamito gṛhaṃ bhavānmarutpurādhīśvara! pāhi māṃ gadāt ||
10.49.11 bhavatprabhāvāvidurā hi gopāstaruprapātādikamatra goṣṭḥe |
10.49.12 shetumutpātagaṇaṃ viśaṅkya prayātumanyatra mano vitenuḥ ||
10.49.21 tatropanandābhidhagopavaryo jagau bhavatpreraṇayaiva nūnam |
10.49.22 itiḥ pratīcyāṃ vipinaṃ manojñaṃ bṛndāvanaṃ nāma virājatīti ||
10.49.31 bṛhadvanaṃ tatkhalu nandamukhyā vidhāya gauṣṭhīnamatha kṣaṇena |
10.49.32 tvadanvitatvajjananīniviṣṭagariṣtayānānugatā viceluḥ ||
10.49.41 anomanojñadhvanidhenupālīkhurapraṇādāntarato vadhūbhiḥ |
10.49.42 bhavadvinodālapitīkṣarāṇi prapīya nājñāyata mārgadairghyam ||
10.49.51 nirīkṣya bṛndāvanamīśa! nandatprasūnakundapramukhadrumaugham |
10.49.52 amodathāḥ śādvalasāndralakṣmyā harinmaṇīkuṭṭimapuṣṭaśobham ||
10.49.61 navākanirvyuḍhanivāsabhedeṣvaśeṣagopeṣu sukhāsiteṣu |
10.49.62 vanaśriyaṃ gopakiśorapālīvimiśritaḥ paryagalokathāstvam ||
10.49.71 arālamārgāgatanirmalāpāṃ marālakujākṛtanarmalāpām |
10.49.72 nirantarasmerasarojavaktrāṃ kalindakanyāṃ samalokayastvam ||
10.49.81 mayūrakekāśatalobhanīyaṃ myūkhamīlaśabalaṃ maṇīnām |
10.49.82 viriñcalokaspṛśamuccaśṛṅgairgiriṃ ca govardhanamaikṣathāstvam ||
10.49.91 samaṃ tato gopakumārakaistvaṃ samantato yatra vanāntamāgāḥ |
10.49.92 tatastatastāṃ kṛṭilāmapaśyaḥ kalindajāṃ rāgavatīmivaikām ||
10.49.101 tathāvidhe'smin vipine paśavye samutsuko vatsagaṇapracāre |
10.49.102 caran sarāmo'tha kumārakaistvaṃ samīragehādhipa! pāhi rogāt ||
10.50.11 taralamadhukṛdbṛnde bṛndāvane'tha manohare
10.50.12 paśupaśiśubhiḥ sākaṃ vatsānupālanalolupaḥ |
10.50.13 haladharasakho deva! śrīman! viceritha dhārayan
10.50.14 gavalamuralīvetraṃ netrābhirāmatanudyutiḥ ||
10.50.21 vihitajagatīrakṣaṃ lakṣmīkarāmbujalīlitaṃ
10.50.22 dadati caraṇadvandvaṃ bṛndāvane tvayi pāvane |
10.50.23 kimiva na babhau sampatsampūritaṃ taruvallarī
10.50.24 saliladharaṇīgotrakṣetrādikaṃ kamalāpate! ||
10.50.31 vilasadulape kāntārānte samīraṇaśītale
10.50.32 vipulayamunātīre govardhanācalamūrdhasu |
10.50.33 lalitamuralīnādaḥ sañcārayan khalu vātsakaṃ
10.50.34 kvacana divase daityaṃ vatsākṛtiṃ tvamudaikṣathāḥ ||
10.50.41 rabhasavilasatpucchaṃ vicchāyato'sya vilokayan
10.50.42 kimapi valitaskandhaṃ randhrapratīkṣamudīkṣitam |
10.50.43 tamatha caraṇe bibhradvibhrāmayanmuhuruccakaiḥ
10.50.44 kuhacana mahāvṛkṣe cikṣepitha kṣatajīvitam ||
10.50.51 nipatati mahādaitye jātyā durātmani tatkṣaṇaṃ
10.50.52 nipatanajavakṣuṇṇakṣoṇīruhakṣatakānane |
10.50.53 divi paramiladbṛndā bṛndārakāḥ kusumotkaraiḥ
10.50.54 śirasi bhavato harṣādvarṣanti nāma tadā hare! ||
10.50.61 surabhilatamā mūrdhanyūrdhvaṃ kutaḥ kusumāvalī
10.50.62 nipatati tavetyukto bālaiḥ sahelamudairayaḥ |
10.50.63 jhaṭiti danujakṣepeṇordhvaṃ gatastarumaṇḍalāt
10.50.64 kusumanikaraḥ so'yaṃ nūnaṃ sameti śanairiti ||
10.50.71 kvacana divase bhūyo bhūyastareparuṣātape
10.50.72 tapanatanayāpāthaḥ pātuṃ gatā bhavadādayaḥ |
10.50.73 calitagarutaṃ prekṣāmāsurbakaṃ khalu vismṛtaṃ
10.50.74 kṣitidharagarucchede kailāsaśailamivāparam ||
10.50.81 pibati salilaṃ gopavrāte bhavanatamabhidrutaḥ
10.50.82 sa kila niginalannagniprakhyaṃ punardrutamudvaman |
10.50.83 dalayitumagāttroṭyāḥ koṭyā tadā yu bhavān vibho!
10.50.84 khalajanabhidācuñcuścañcū pragṛhya dadāra tam ||
10.50.91 sapadi sahajāṃ sandraṣṭuṃ vā mṛtāṃ khalu pūtanā
10.50.92 manujamaghamapyagre gatvā pratīkṣitumeva vā |
10.50.93 śamananilayaṃ yāte tasmin bake sumanogaṇe
10.50.94 kirati sumanobṛndaṃ bṛndāvanādgṛhamaiyathāḥ ||
10.50.101 lalitamuralīnādaṃ dūrānniśamya vadhūjanais
10.50.102 tvaritamupagamyārādārūḍhamodamudīkṣitaḥ |
10.50.103 janitajananīnandānandaḥ samīraṇamandira
10.50.104 prathitavasate! śaure! dūrīkuruṣva mamāmayān ||
10.51.11 kadācana vrajaśiśubhiḥ samaṃ bhavān
10.51.12 vanāśane vihitamatiḥ pragetarām |
10.51.13 samāvṛto bahutaravatsamaṇḍalaiḥ
10.51.14 satemanairniragamadīśa! jemanaiḥ ||
10.51.21 viniryatastava caraṇāmbujadvayā
10.51.22 dudāñcitaṃ tribhuvanapāvanaṃ rajaḥ |
10.51.23 maharṣayaḥ pulakadharaiḥ kalebarai
10.51.24 rudūhire dhṛtabhavadīkṣaṇotsavāḥ ||
10.51.31 pracārayatyaviralaśādvale tale
10.51.32 paśūn vibho! bhavati samaṃ kumārakaiḥ |
10.51.33 aghāsuro nyaruṇadaghāya vartanīṃ
10.51.34 bhayānakaḥ sapadi śayānakākṛtiḥ ||
10.51.41 mahācalapratimatanorguhānibha
10.51.42 prasāritaprathitamukhasya kānane |
10.51.43 mukhodaraṃ viharaṇakautukādgatāḥ
10.51.44 kumārakāḥ kimapi vidūrage tvayi ||
10.51.51 pramādataḥ praviśati pannagodaraṃ
10.51.52 kvathattanau paśupakule savātsake |
10.51.53 vidannidaṃ tvamapi viveśitha prabho!
10.51.54 suhṛjjanaṃ viśaraṇāmāśu rakṣitum ||
10.51.61 galedare vipulitavarṣmaṇā tvayā
10.51.62 mahorage luṭhati niruddhamārute |
10.51.63 drutaṃ bhavān vidalitakaṇṭhamaṇḍalo
10.51.64 vimocayan paśupaśūn viniryayau ||
10.51.71 kṣaṇaṃ divi tvadupagamārthamāsthitaṃ
10.51.72 mahāsuraprabhavamaho maho mahat |
10.51.73 vinirgate tvayi tu nilīnamañjasā
10.51.74 nabhaḥshtale nanṛturatho jaguḥ surāḥ ||
10.51.81 savismayaiḥ kamalabhavādibhiḥ surair
10.51.82 anudrutastadanu gataḥ kumārakaiḥ |
10.51.83 dine punastaruṇadaśāmupeyuṣi
10.51.84 svakairbhavānatanuta bhojanotsavam ||
10.51.91 viṣāṇikāmapi muralīṃ nitambake
10.51.92 niveśayan kabaladharaḥ karāmbuje |
10.51.93 prahāsayan kalavacanaiḥ kumārakān
10.51.94 bubhojitha tridaśagaṇairmudā nutaḥ ||
10.51.101 sukhāśanaṃ tviha tava gopamaṇḍale
10.51.102 makhāśanātpriyamiva devamaṇḍale |
10.51.103 iti stutastridaśavarairjagatprabho!
10.51.104 marutpurīnilaya! gadātprapāhi mām ||
10.52.11 anyāvatāranikareṣvanirīkṣitaṃ te
10.52.12 bhūmātirekamabhivīkṣya tadāghamokṣe |
10.52.13 brahmā parīkṣitumanāḥ sa parokṣabhāvaṃ
10.52.14 ninye'tha vatsakagaṇān pravitatya māyām ||
10.52.21 vatsānavīkṣya vivaśe paśupotkare tā
10.52.22 nānetukāma iva dhātṛmatānuvartī |
10.52.23 tvaṃ sāmibhuktakabalo gatavāṃstadānīṃ
10.52.24 bhuktāṃstirodhita sarojabhavaḥ kumārān ||
10.52.31 vatsāyitastadanu gopagaṇāyitastvaṃ
10.52.32 śikyādibhāṇḍamuralīgavalādirūpaḥ |
10.52.33 prāgvadvihṛtya vipineṣu cirāya sāyaṃ
10.52.34 tvaṃ māyayātha bahudhā vrajamāyayātha ||
10.52.41 tvāmeva śikyāgavalādimayaṃ dadhāno
10.52.42 bhūyastvameva paśuvatsakabālarūpaḥ |
10.52.43 gorūpiṇībhirapi gopavadhūmayībhi
10.52.44 rāsādito'si jananībhiratipraharṣāt ||
10.52.51 jīvaṃ hi kañcidabhimānavaśātsvakīyaṃ
10.52.52 matvā tanūja iti rāgabharaṃ vahantyaḥ |
10.52.53 ātmānameva tu bhavantamavāpya sūnuṃ
10.52.54 prītiṃ yayurna kiyatīṃ vanitāśca gāvaḥ ||
10.52.61 evaṃ pratikṣaṇavijṛmbhitaharṣabhāra
10.52.62 niśśeṣagopagaṇalālitabhūrimūrtim |
10.52.63 tvāmagrajo'pi bubudhe kila vatsarānte
10.52.64 brahmātmanorapi mahān yuvayorviśeṣaḥ ||
10.52.71 varṣāvadhau navapurātanavatsapālān
10.52.72 dṛṣṭvā vivekamasṛṇe druhiṇe vimūḍhe |
10.52.73 prādīdṛśaḥ pratinavānmakuṭāngadādi
10.52.74 bhūśāṃścaturbhujayujaḥ sajalāmbudābhān ||
10.52.81 pratyekameva kamalāparilālitāṅgān
10.52.82 bhogīndrabhogaśayanānnayanābhirāmān |
10.52.83 līlānimīlitadṛśaḥ sanakādiyogi
10.52.84 vyāsevitān kamalabhūrbhavato dadarśa ||
10.52.91 nārāyaṇākṛtimasaṅkhyatamāṃ nirīkṣya
10.52.92 sarvatra sevakamapi svamavekṣya dhātā |
10.52.93 māyānimagnahṛdayo vimumoha yāvta
10.52.94 deko babhūvitha tadā kabalārdhapāṇiḥ ||
10.52.101 naśyanmade tadanu viśvapatiṃ muhustvāṃ
10.52.102 natvā ca nūtavati dhātari dhāma yāte |
10.52.103 potaiḥ samaṃ pramuditaiḥ praviśanniketaṃ
10.52.104 vātālayādhipa! vibho! paripāhi rogāt ||
10.53.11 atītya bālyaṃ jagatāṃ pate! tvamupetya paugaṇḍavayo manojñam |
10.53.12 upekṣya vatsāvanamutsavena prāvartathā gogaṇapālanāyām ||
10.53.21 upakramasyānuguṇaiva seyaṃ marutpurādhīṣa! tava pravṛttiḥ |
10.53.22 gotrāparitrāṇakṛte'vatīnastadeva devārabhathāstadā yat ||ṣ
10.53.31 kadāpi rāmeṇa samaṃ vanānte vanaśriyaṃ vīkṣya caran sukhena |
10.53.32 śrīdāmanāmnaḥ svasakhasya vācā modādagā dhenukakānanaṃ tvam ||
10.53.41 uttālatālīnivahe tvaduktyā balena dhūte'tha balena dorbhyām |
10.53.42 mṛduḥ kharaścābhyapatatpurastātphalotkaro dhenukadānavo'pi ||
10.53.51 samudyato dhainukapālane'haṃ vadhaṃ kathaṃ dhainukamadya kurve |
10.53.52 itīva matvā dhruvamagrajena suraugharoddhāramajīghatastvam ||
10.53.61 tadīyabhṛtyānapi jambukatvenopāgatānagrajasaṃyutastvam |
10.53.62 jambūphalānīva tadā nirāsthastāleṣu khelan bhagavan! nirāsthaḥ ||
10.53.71 vinighnati tvayyatha jambukaughaṃ sanāmakatvādvaruṇastadānīm |
10.53.72 bhayākulo jambukanāmadheyaṃ śrutiprasiddhaṃ vyadhiteti manye ||
10.53.81 tavāvatārasya phalaṃ murāre! sañjātamadyeti surairnutastvam |
10.53.82 satyaṃ phalaṃ jātamiheti hāsī bālaiḥ samaṃ tālaphalānyabhuṅkthāḥ ||
10.53.91 madhudravasrunti bṛhanti tāni phalāni medobharabhṛnti bhuktvā |
10.53.92 tṛptaiśca dṛptairbhavanaṃ phalaughaṃ vahadbhirāgāḥ khalu bālakaistvam ||
10.53.101 hato hato dhenuka ityupetya phalānyadadbhirmadhurāṇi lokaiḥ |
10.53.102 jayeti jīveti nuto vibho! tvaṃ marutpurādhīśvara! pāhi rogāt ||
10.54.11 tvatsevotkaḥ saubhārirnāma pūrvaṃ kālindyantardvādaśābdaṃ tapasyan |
10.54.12 mīnavrāte snehavān bhogalole tārkṣyaṃ sākṣādaikṣatāgre kadācit ||
10.54.21 tvadvāhaṃ taṃ sakṣudhaṃ tṛkṣasūnuṃ mīnaṃ kañcijjakṣataṃ lakṣayan saḥ |
10.54.22 taptaścitte śaptavānatra cettvaṃ jantūn bhoktā jīvitaṃ cāpi bhoktā ||
10.54.31 tasmin kāle kāliyaḥ kṣveladarpātsarpārāte kalpitaṃ bhāgamśnan |
10.54.32 tena krodhāttvatpadāmbhojabhājā pakṣakṣiptastaddurāpaṃ payo'gāt ||
10.54.41 ghore tasmin sūrajānīravāse tīre vṛkṣā vikṣatāḥ kṣvelavegāt |
10.54.42 pakṣivrātāḥ peturabhre patantaḥ kāruṇyārdraṃ tvanmanastena jātam ||
10.54.51 kāle tasminnekadā sīrapāṇiṃ muktvā yāte yāmunaṃ kānanāntam |
10.54.52 tvayyuddāmagrīṣmabhīṣmoṣmataptā gogopālā vyāpiban kṣvelatoyam ||
10.54.61 naśyajjīvān vicyutān kṣmātale tān viśvān paśyannacyuta! tvaṃ dayārdraḥ |
10.54.62 prāpyopāntaṃ jīvayāmāsitha drākpīyūṣāmbhovarṣibhiḥ śrīkaṭākṣaiḥ ||
10.54.71 kiṃ kiṃ jāto harṣavarṣātirekaḥ sarvāṅgeṣvityutthitā gopasaṅghāḥ |
10.54.72 dṛṣṭvāgre tvāṃ tvatkṛtaṃ tadvidantasrvāmāliṅgan dṛṣṭanānāprabhāvāḥ ||
10.54.81 gāvaścaivaṃ labdhajīvāḥ kṣaṇena sphītānandāstvāṃ ca dṛṣṭvā purastāt |
10.54.82 drāgāvavruḥ sarvato harṣabāṣpaṃ vyāmuñcantyo mandamudyanninādāḥ ||
10.54.91 romāñco'yaṃ sarvato naḥ śarīre bhūyasyantaḥ kācidānandamūrchā |
10.54.92 āścaryo'yaṃ kṣvelavego mukundetyukto gopairnandito vandito'bhūḥ ||
10.54.101 evaṃ bhaktānmuktajīvānapi tvaṃ mugdhāpāṅkairastarogāṃstanoṣi |
10.54.102 tādṛgbhūtasphītakāruṇyabhūmā rogītpāyā vāyugehādhivāsa! ||
10.55.11 atha vāriṇi ghorataraṃ phaṇinaṃ prativārayituṃ kṛtadhīrbhagavan! |
10.55.12 drutamāritha tīraganīpataruṃ viṣamīrutaśoṣitaparṇacayam ||
10.55.21 adhiruhya padāmburuheṇa ca taṃ navapallavatulyamojñarucā |
10.55.22 hadavāriṇi dūrataraṃ nyapataḥ parighūrṇitaghorataraṅgagaṇe ||
10.55.31 bhuvanatrayabhārabhṛto bhavato gurubhāravikrampivijṛmbhijalā |
10.55.32 parimajjayati sma dhanuḥśataṃ taṭinī jhaṭiti sphuṭaghoṣavatī ||
10.55.41 atha dikṣu vidikṣu parikṣubhitabhramitodaravārininādabharaiḥ |
10.55.42 udakādudagāduragādhipatistvadupāntamaśāntaruṣāndhamanāḥ ||
10.55.51 phaṇaśṛṅgasahasraviniḥsṛmarajvaladagnikaṇograviṣāmbudharam |
10.55.52 purataḥ phaṇinaṃ samalokayathā bahuśṛṅgiṇamañjanaśailamiva ||
10.55.61 jvaladakṣiparikṣaradugraviṣaśvasaniṣmabharaḥ sa mahānbhujagaḥ |
10.55.62 paridaśya bhavantamanantabalaṃ samaveṣṭayadasphuṭaceṣṭamaho ||
10.55.71 avilokya bhavantamathākulite taṭagāmini bālakadhenugaṇe |
10.55.72 vrajagehatale'pyanimittaśataṃ samudīkṣya gatā yamunāṃ paśupāḥ ||
10.55.81 akhileṣu vibho! bhavadīyadaśāmavalokya jihāsuṣu jīvabharam |
10.55.82 phaṇibandhanamāśu vimucya javādudagamyata hāsajuṣā bhavatā ||
10.55.91 adhiruhya tataḥ phaṇirājaphaṇānnanṛte bhavatā mṛdupādarucā |
10.55.92 kalaśiñcitanūpuramañcumilatkarakaṅkaṇasaṃkulasaṃkvaṇitam ||
10.55.101 jahṛṣuḥ paśupāstutuṣurmunayo vavṛṣuḥ kusumāni surendragaṇāḥ |
10.55.102 tvayi nṛtyati mārutagehapate! paripāhi sa māṃ tvamadāntagadāt ||
10.56.11 racirakampitakuṇḍalamaṇḍalaḥ suciramīśa! nanartitha pannage |
10.56.12 amaratāḍitadundubhisundaraṃ viyati gāyati daivatayauvate ||
10.56.21 namati yadyadamuṣya śiro hare! parivihāya tadunnatamunnatam |
10.56.22 parimathan padapaṅkaruhā ciraṃ vyaharathāḥ karatālamanoharam ||
10.56.31 tvadavabhagnavibhugnaphaṇāgaṇe galitaśoṇitaśoṇitapāthasi |
10.56.32 phaṇipatāvavasīdati sannatāstadabalāstava mādhava! pādayoḥ ||
10.56.41 ayi puraiva cirāya pariśrutatvadanubhāvavilīnahṛdo hi tāḥ |
10.56.42 munibhirapyanavāpyapathaiḥ stavairnunuvurīśa! bhavantamayantritam ||
10.56.51 phaṇivadhūgaṇabhaktivilokanapravikasatkaruṇākulacetasā |
10.56.52 phaṇipatirbhavatācyuta! jīvitastvaji samarpitamūrtiravānamat ||
10.56.61 ramaṇakaṃ vraje cāridhimadhyagaṃ phaṇiripurna karoti virodhitām |
10.56.62 iti bhavadvacanānyatimānayan phaṇipatirniragāduragaiḥ samam ||
10.56.71 phaṇivadhūjanadattamaṇivrajajvalitahāradukūlavibhūṣitaḥ |
10.56.72 taṭagataiḥ pramadāśruvimiśritaiḥ samagathāḥ svajanairdivasāvadhau ||
10.56.81 niśi punastamasā vrajamandiraṃ vrajitumakṣama eva janotkare |
10.56.82 svapati tatra bhavaccaraṇāśraye davakṛśānurarundha samantataḥ ||
10.56.91 prabudhitānatha pālaya pālayetyudayadārtaravān paśupālakān |
10.56.92 avitumāśu papātha mahānalaṃ kimiha citramayaṃ khalu te mukham ||
10.56.101 śikhina varṇata eva hi pītatā parilasatyudhanā kriyayāpyasau |
10.56.102 iti nutaḥ paśupairmuditairvibho! hara hare! duritaiḥ saha me gadān ||
10.57.11 rāmasakhaḥ kvāpi dine kāmada! bhagavan! gato bhavān vipinam |
10.57.12 sūnubhirapi gopānāṃ dhenubhirabhisaṃvṛto lasadveṣu ||
10.57.21 sandarśayan balāya svairaṃ bṛndāvanaśriyaṃ vimalām |
10.57.22 kāṇḍīraiḥ saha bālairbhāṇḍīrakamāgamo vaṭaṃ krīḍan ||
10.57.31 tāvattāvakanidhanaspṛhayālurgopamūrtiradayāluḥ |
10.57.32 daityaḥ pralambanāmā pralambabāhuṃ bhavantamāpede ||
10.57.41 jānannapyavijānanniva tena samaṃ niṣaddhasauhārdaḥ |
10.57.42 vaṭanikaṭe paṭupaśupavyānaddhaṃ dvandvayuddhamārabdhāḥ ||
10.57.51 gopān vibhajya tanvan saṅghaṃ balabhadrakaṃ bhavatkamapi |
10.57.52 tvadbalabhīruṃ daityaṃ tvadbalagatamanvamanyathā bhagavan! ||
10.57.61 kalpitavijetṛvahane samare parauūthagaṃ svadayitataram |
10.57.62 śrīdāmānamadhatthāḥ parājito bhaktadāsatāṃ prathayan ||
10.57.71 evaṃ bahuṣu vibhūman! bāleṣu vahatsu vāhyamāneṣu |
10.57.72 rāmavijitaḥ pralambo jahāra taṃ dūrato bhavadbhītyā ||
10.57.81 tvaddūraṃ gamayantaṃ taṃ dṛṛṣṭvā halini vihitagarimabhare |
10.57.82 daityaḥ svarūpamāgādyadrūpātsa hi balo'pi cakito'bhūt ||
10.57.91 uccatayā daityatanostvanmukhamālokya dūrato rāmaḥ |
10.57.92 vigatabhayo dṛḍhamuṣṭyā bhṛśaduṣṭaṃ sapadi piṣṭavānenam ||
10.57.101 hatvā dānavavīraṃ prāptaṃ balamāliliṅgitha premṇā |
10.57.102 tāvanmilatoryvayoḥ śirasi kṛtā puṣpavṛṣṭiramaragaṇaiḥ ||
10.57.111 ālambo bhuvanānāṃ prālambaṃ nidhanamevamāracayan |
10.57.112 kālaṃ vihāya sadyo lolambaruce! hare! hareḥ kleśān ||
10.58.11 tvayi viharaṇalole bālajālaiḥ pralamba
10.58.12 pramathanasavilambe dhenavaḥ svairacārāḥ |
10.58.13 tṛṇakutukaniviṣṭā dūradūraṃ carantyaḥ
10.58.14 kimapi vipinamaiṣīkākhyamīṣāṃbabhūvuḥ ||
10.58.21 anadhigatanidāghakrauryabṛndāvanāntād
10.58.22 bahiridamupayātāḥ kānanaṃ dhenavastāḥ |
10.58.23 tava virahaviṣaṇṇā ūṣmalagrīṣmatāpa
10.58.24 prasaravisaradambhasyākulāḥ stambhamāpuḥ ||
10.58.31 tadanu saha sahāyairdūramanviṣya śaure!
10.58.32 galitasaraṇimuñjāraṇyasañjātakhedam |
10.58.33 paśukulamabhivīkṣya kṣipramānetumārāt
10.58.34 tvayi gatavati hī hī sarvato'gnirjajṛmbhe ||
10.58.41 sakalahariti dīpte ghorabhāṅkārabhīme
10.58.42 śikhini vihatamārgā ardhadagdhā ivārtāḥ |
10.58.43 ahaha bhuvanabandho! pāhi pāhīti sarve
10.58.44 śaraṇamupagatāstvāṃ tāpahartāramekam ||
10.58.51 alamalamatibhītya sarvato mīlayadhvaṃ
10.58.52 dṛśamiti tava vācā mīlitākṣeṣu teṣu |
10.58.53 kvanu davadahano'sau kutra muñjāṭavī sā
10.58.54 sapadi vavṛtire te hanta bhaṇḍīradeśe ||
10.58.61 jaya jaya tava māyā keyamīśeti teṣāṃ
10.58.62 nutibhiruditahāso baddhanānāvilāsaḥ |
10.58.63 punarapi vipinānte prācaraḥ pāṭalādi
10.58.64 prasavanikaramātragrāhyagharmānubhāve ||
10.58.71 tvayi vimukhamivoccaistāpabhāraṃ vahantaṃ
10.58.72 tava bhajanavadantaḥ paṅkamucchoṣayantam |
10.58.73 tava bhujavadudañcadbhūritejaḥpravāhaṃ
10.58.74 tapasamayamanaiṣīryāmuneṣu sthaleṣu ||
10.58.81 tadanu jaladajālaistvadvapustulyabhābhir
10.58.82 vikasadamalavidyutpītavāsovilāsaiḥ |
10.58.83 sakalabhuvanabhājāṃ harṣadāṃ varṣavelāṃ
10.58.84 kṣitidharakuhareṣu svairavāsī vyanaiṣīḥ ||
10.58.91 kuharatalaniviṣṭaṃ tvāṃ gariṣṭhaṃ girīndraḥ
10.58.92 śikhikulanavakekākākubhiḥ stotrakārī |
10.58.93 sphuṭakuṭajakadambastomapuṣpāñjaliṃ ca
10.58.94 pravidadhadanubheje deva! govardhano'sau ||
10.58.101 atha śaradamupetāṃ tāṃ bhavadbhaktaceto
10.58.102 vimalasalilapūrāṃ mānayan kānaneṣu |
10.58.103 tṛṇāmamalavanānte cāru sañcārayan gāḥ
10.58.104 pavanapurapate! tvaṃ dehi me dehasaukhyam ||
10.59.11 tvadvapurnavakalāyakomalaṃ premadohanamaśeṣamohanam |
10.59.12 brahmā tattvaparacinmudātmakaṃ vīkṣya sammumuhuranvahaṃ striyaḥ ||
10.59.21 manmathonmathitamānasāḥ kramāttvadvilokanaratāstatastataḥ |
10.59.22 gopikāstava na sehire hare! kānanopagatimapyaharmukhe ||
10.59.23 nirgate bhavati dattadṛṣṭayastvadgatena manasā mṛgekṣaṇāḥ |
10.59.24 veṇunādamupakarṇya dūratastvadvilāsakathayābhiremire ||
10.59.41 kānanāntamitavān bhavānapi snigdhapādapatale manorame |
10.59.42 vyatyayākalitapādamāsthitaḥ pratyapūrayata veṇunālikām ||
10.59.51 mārabāṇadhutakhecarīkulaṃ nirvikārapaśupakṣimaṇḍalam |
10.59.52 drāvaṇaṃ ca dṛṣadāmapi prabho! tāvakaṃ vyajani veṇukūjitam ||
10.59.61 veṇurandhrataralāṅgulīdalaṃ tālasañcalitapādapallavam |
10.59.62 tatsthitaṃ tava parokṣamapyaho saṃvicintyā mumuhurvrajāṅganāḥ ||
10.59.71 nirviśaṅkabhavadaṅgadarśinīḥ khecarīḥ khagamṛgān paśūnapi |
10.59.72 tvatpadapraṇayi kānanaṃ ca tā dhanyadhanyamiti nanvamānaya ||
10.59.81 āpiṣeyamadharāmṛtaṃ kadā veṇubhuktarasaśeṣamekadā |
10.59.82 dūrato bata kṛtaṃ durāśayetyākulā muhurimāḥ samāmuhan ||
10.59.91 pratyahaṃ ca punaritthamaṅganāścittayonijanitādanugrahāt |
10.59.92 baddharāgavivaśāstvayi prabho! nityamāpuriha kṛtyamūḍhatām ||
10.59.101 rāgastāvajjāyate hi svabhāvānmokṣopāye yatnataḥ syānna vā syāt |
10.59.102 tāsāṃ tvekaṃ taddvayaṃ labdhamāsīdbhāgyaṃ bhāgyaṃ pāhi māṃ māryteśa! ||
10.60.11 madanāturacetaso'nvahaṃ bhavadaṅghridvayadāsyakāmpayā |
10.60.12 yamunātaṭasīmni saikatīṃ taralākṣyo girijāṃ samārcican ||
10.60.21 tava nāmakathāratāḥ samaṃ sudṛśaḥ prātarupāgatā nadīm |
10.60.22 upahāraśatairapūjayan dayito nandasuto bhavediti ||
10.60.31 iti māsamupāhitavratāstaralākṣīrabhivīkṣya tā bhavān |
10.60.32 karuṇāmṛdulo nadītaṭaṃ samayāsīttadanugrahecchayā ||
10.60.41 niyamāvasitau nijāmbaraṃ taṭasīmanyavamucya tāstadā |
10.60.42 yamunājalakhelanākulāḥ puratastvāmavalokya lajjitāḥ ||
10.60.51 trapayā namitānanāsvatho vanitāsvambarajālamantike |
10.60.52 nihitaṃ parigṛhya bhūruho viṭapaṃ taṃ tarasādhirūḍhavān ||
10.60.61 iha tāvadupetya nīyatāṃ vasanaṃ vaḥ sudṛśo! yathāyatham |
10.60.62 iti narmamṛdusmite tvayi vruvati vyāmumuhe vadhūjanaiḥ ||
10.60.71 ayi jīva ciraṃ kiśora! nastava dāsīravaśīkaroṣi kim |
10.60.72 pradiśāmbaramambujekṣaṇetyuditastvaṃ smitameva vattavān ||
10.60.81 adhiruhya taṭaṃ kṛtāñjalīḥ pariśuddhāḥ svagatīrnirīkṣya tāḥ |
10.60.82 vasanānyakhilānyanugrahaṃ punarevaṃ giramapyadā mudā ||
10.60.91 viditaṃ nanu vo manīṣitaṃ vaditārastviha yogyamuttaram |
10.60.92 yamunāpulīne sacandrikāḥ kṣaṇadā ityabalāstvamūcivān ||
10.60.101 upakarṇya bhavanmukhacyutaṃ madhuniṣyandi vaco mṛgīdṛśaḥ |
10.60.102 praṇayādayi vīkṣya vīkṣya te vadanābjaṃ śanakairgṛhaṃ gatāḥ ||
10.60.111 iti nanvanugṛhya ballavīrtipinānteṣu pureva sañcaran |
10.60.112 karuṇāśiśiro hare! hara tvarayā me sakalāmayāvalim ||
10.61.11 tataśca bṛndāvanato'tidūrato vanaṃ gatastvaṃ khalu gopagokulaiḥ |
10.61.12 hṛdantare bhaktataradvijāṅginākadambakānugrahaṇāgrahaṃ vahan ||
10.61.21 tato nirīkṣyāśaraṇe vanāntare kiśoralokaṃ kṣudhitaṃ tṛṣākulam |
10.61.22 udūrato yajñaparān dvijān prati vyasarjayo dīdiviyācanāya tān ||
10.61.31 gateṣvatho teṣvabhidhāya te'bhidhāṃ kumārakeṣvodanayāciṣu prabho! |
10.61.32 śrutisthirā apyabhininyuraśrutiṃ na kiñcidūcuśca mahīsurottamāḥ ||
10.61.41 anādarātkhinnadhiyo hi bālakāḥ samāyayuryuktamidaṃ hi yajvasu |
10.61.42 cirādabhaktāḥ khalu te mahīsurāḥ kathaṃ hi bhaktaṃ tvayi taiḥ samarpyate ||
10.61.51 nivedayadhvaṃ gṛhiṇījanāya māṃ diśeyurannaṃ karuṇākulā imāḥ |
10.61.52 iti smitārdraṃ bhavateritā gatāste dārakā dārajanaṃ yayācire ||
10.61.61 gṛhītanāmni tvayi sambhramākulāścaturvidhaṃ bhojyarasaṃ pragṛhya tāḥ |
10.61.62 ciraṃ dhṛtatvatpravilokanāgrahāḥ svakairniruddhā api tūrṇamāyayuḥ ||
10.61.71 vilolapiñchaṃ cikure kapolayoḥ samullasatkuṇḍalamārdramīkṣite |
10.61.72 nidhāya bāhuṃ suhṛdaṃsasīmani sthitaṃ bhavantaṃ samalokayanta tāḥ ||
10.61.81 tadā ca kācittvadupāgamodyatā gṛhītahastā dayitena yajvanā |
10.61.82 tadaiva sañcintya bhavantamañjasā viveśa kaivalpamaho kṛtinyasau ||
10.61.91 ādāya bhojyānyanugṛhya tāḥ punastvadaṅgasaṅgaspṛhayojjhatīrgṛham |
10.61.92 vilokya yajñāya visarjayannimāścakartha bhartnapi tāsvagarhaṇān ||
10.61.101 nirūpya doṣaṃ nijamaṅganājane vilokya bhaktiṃ ca punarvicāribhiḥ |
10.61.102 prabuddhatattvaistvamabhiṣṭuto dvijairmarutpurādhīśa! nirundhi me gadān ||
10.62.11 kadācidgopālān vihitamakhasambhāravibhavān
10.62.12 nirīkṣya tvaṃ śaure! maghavamadamuddhvaṃsitumanāḥ |
10.62.13 vijānannapyetān vinayamṛdu nandādipaśupā
10.62.14 napṛcchaḥ ko vāyaṃ janaka! bhavatāmudyama iti ||
10.62.21 babhāṣe nandastvāṃ suta! nanu vidheyo maghavato
10.62.22 makho varṣe varṣe sukhayati sa varṣeṇa pṛthivīm |
10.62.23 nṛṇāṃ varṣāyattaṃ nikhilamupajīvyaṃ mahitale
10.62.24 viśeṣādasmākaṃ tṛṇasalilajīvā hi paśavaḥ ||
10.62.31 iti śrutvā vācaṃ piturayi bhavānāha sarasaṃ
10.62.32 dhigetanno satyaṃ maghavajanitā vṛṣṭiriti yat |
10.62.33 adṛṣṭaṃ jīvānāṃ sṛjati khalu vṛṣṭiṃ samucitāṃ
10.62.34 mahāraṇye vṛkṣāḥ kimiva balimindrāya dadate ||
10.62.41 idaṃ tāvatsatyaṃ yadiha paśapo naḥ kuladhanaṃ
10.62.42 tadājīvyāyāsau baliracalabhartre samucitaḥ |
10.62.43 surebhyo'pyutkṛṣṭā nanu dharaṇidevāḥ kṣititale
10.62.44 tataste'pyārādhyā iti jagaditha tvaṃ nijajanām ||
10.62.51 bhavadvācaṃ śrutvā bahumatiyutāste'pi paśupā
10.62.52 dvijendrānarcanto balimadaduruccaiḥ kṣitibhṛte |
10.62.53 vyadhuḥ prādakṣiṇyaṃ subhṛśamanamannādarayutās
10.62.54 tvamādaḥ śailātmā balimakhilamābhīrapurataḥ ||
10.62.61 avocaścaivaṃ tān kimiha vitathaṃ me nigaditaṃ
10.62.62 girīndro nanveṣu svabalimupabhūṅkte svavapuṣā |
10.62.63 ayaṃ gotro gotradviṣi ca kupite rakṣitumalaṃ
10.62.64 samastānityuktā jahṛṣurakhilā gokulajuṣaḥ ||
10.62.71 pariprītā yātāḥ khalu bhavadupetā vrajajuṣo
10.62.72 vrajaṃ yāvattāvannijamakhavibhaṅgaṃ niśamayan |
10.62.73 bhavantaṃ jānannapyadhikarajasākrāntahṛdayo
10.62.74 na sehe devendrastvaduparacitātmonnatirapi ||
10.62.81 manuṣyatvaṃ yāto madhubhidapi deveṣvavinayaṃ
10.62.82 vidhatte cennaṣṭastridaśasadasāṃ ko'pi mahimā |
10.62.83 tataśca dhvaṃsiṣye paśupahatakasya śriyamiti
10.62.84 pravṛttastvāṃ jetuṃ sa kila maghavā durmadanidhiḥ ||
10.62.91 tvadāvāsaṃ hantuṃ pralayajaladānambarabhuvi
10.62.92 prahiṇvan bibhrāṇaḥ kuliśamayamabhrebhagamanaḥ |
10.62.93 pratasthe'nyairantardahanamarudādyairvihasito
10.62.94 bhavanmāyā naiva tribhūvanapate! mohayati kam ||
10.62.101 surendraḥ kuddhaśceddvijakaruṇayā śailakṛpayā
10.62.102 pyanātaṅko'smākaṃ niyata iti viśvāsya paśupān |
10.62.103 aho kiṃ nāyāto giribhiditi sañcintya nivasan
10.62.104 marudgehādhīśa! praṇuda muravairin! mama gadān ||
10.63.11 dadṛśire kila tatkṣaṇamakṣatastanitajṛmbhitakampitadiktaṭāḥ |
10.63.12 suṣamayā bhavadaṅgatulāṃ gatā vrajapadopari vāridharāstvayā ||
10.63.21 vipulakarakamiścaistoyadhārānipātair
10.63.22 diśi diśi paśupānāṃ maṇḍale daṇḍyamāne |
10.63.23 kupitaharikṛtānnaḥ pāhi pāhīti teṣāṃ
10.63.24 vacanamajita! śṛṇvanmā bibhītetyabhāṇīḥ ||
10.63.31 kula iha khalu gotro daivataṃ gotraśatror
10.63.32 vihatimiha sa rundhyatko nuḥ vaḥ saṃśaayo'smin |
10.63.33 iti sahasitavādī deva! govardhanādriṃ
10.63.34 tvaritamudamumūlo mūlato bāla! dorbhyām ||
10.63.41 tadanu girivarasya proddhṛtasyāsya tāvat
10.63.42 sikatilamṛdudeśe dūrato vāritāpe |
10.63.43 parikaraparimiśrān dhenugopānadhastā
10.63.44 dupanidadhadadhatthā hastapadmena śailam ||
10.63.51 bhavati vidhṛtaśaile bālikābhirvayasyair
10.63.52 api vihitavilāsaṃ kelilāpādilole |
10.63.53 savidhamilitadhenūrekahastena kaṇdū
10.63.54 yati sati paśupālāstoṣamaiṣanta sarve ||
10.63.61 atimahān girireṣu tu vāmake karasaroruhi taṃ dharate ciram |
10.63.62 kimidamadbhutamadribalaṃ nviti tvadavalokibhirākathi gopakaiḥ ||
10.63.71 ahaha dhārṣṭyamamuṣya vaṭorgiriṃ vyathitabāhurasāvavaropayet |
10.63.72 iti haristvayi baddhavigarhaṇo divasasaḥtakamugramavarṣayat ||
10.63.81 acalati tvayi deva! padātpadaṃ galitasarvajale ca ghanotkare |
10.63.82 apahṛte marutā marutāṃ patistvadabhiśaṅkitadhīḥ samupādravat ||
10.63.91 śamamupeyuṣi varṣabhare tadā paśupadhenukule ca vinirgate |
10.63.92 bhuvi vibho! samupāhitabhūdharaḥ pramuditaiḥ paśupaiḥ parirebhiṣe ||
10.63.101 dharaṇimeva purā dhṛtavānasi kṣitidharoddharaṇe tava kaḥ śramaḥ |
10.63.102 iti nutastridaśaiḥ kumalāpate! gurupurālaya! pālaya māṃ gadāt ||
10.64.11 ālokya śailoddharaṇādirūpaṃ prabhāvamuccaistava gopalokāḥ |
10.64.12 vīśveśvaraṃ tvāmabhimatya viśve nandaḥ bhavajjātakamanvapṛcchan ||
10.64.21 gargodito nirgadito nijāya vargāya tātena tava prabhāvaḥ |
10.64.22 pūrvādhikstvayyanurāga eṣāmaidhiṣṭa tāvadbahumānabhāraḥ ||
10.64.31 tato'vamānoditatattvabodhaḥ surādhirājaḥ saha divyagavyā |
10.64.32 upetya tuṣtāva sa naṣṭagarvaḥ spṛṣṭvā padābjaṃ maṇimaulinā te ||
10.64.41 snehastunaistvāṃ surabhiḥ payobhirgovindanāmāṅkitamabhyaṣiñcat |
10.64.42 erāvatopāhṛtadivyagaṅgāpāthobhirindro'pi ca jātaharṣaḥ ||
10.64.51 jagattrayeśe tvayi gokuleśatayābhiṣikte sati gopavāṭaḥ |
10.64.52 noke'pi vaikuṇṭhapade'pyalabhyāṃ śriyaṃ prapede bhavataḥ prabhāvāt ||
10.64.61 kadācidantaryamunaṃ prabhāte snāyan pitā vāruṇapūruṣeṇa |
10.64.62 nītastamānetumagāḥ purīṃ tvaṃ tāṃ vāruṇīṃ kāraṇamartyarūpaḥ ||
10.64.71 sasambhramaṃ tena jalādhipena prapūjitastvaṃ pratigṛhya tātam |
10.64.72 upāgatastatkṣaṇamātmagehaṃ pitāvadattaccaritaṃ nijebhyaḥ ||
10.64.81 hariṃ viniścitya bhavantametān bhavatpadālokanabaddhatṛṣṇān |
10.64.82 nirīkṣya viṣṇo! paramaṃ padaṃ taddurāpamanyaistvamadīdṛśastān ||
10.64.91 sphuratparānandarasapravāhaprapūrṇakaivalyamahāpayodhau |
10.64.92 ciraṃ nimagnāḥ khalu gopasaṅghāstvayaiva bhūman! punaruddhṛtāste ||
10.64.101 karabadaravadevaṃ deva! kutrāvatāre
10.64.102 nijapadamanavāpyaṃ darśitaṃ bhaktibhājām |
10.64.103 tadiha paśuparūpī tvaṃ hi sākṣātparātman!
10.64.104 pavanapuranivāsin! pāhi māmāmayebhyaḥ ||
10.65.11 gopījanāya kathitaṃ niyamāvasāne
10.65.12 marotsavaṃ tvamatha sādhayituṃ pravṛttaḥ |
10.65.13 sāndreṇa cāndramahasā śiśirīkṛtāśe
10.65.14 prāpūrayo muralikāṃ yamunāvanānte ||
10.65.21 sambhūrchanābhiruditasvaramaṇḍalābhiḥ
10.65.22 sammūrchayantamakhilaṃ bhuvanāntarālam |
10.65.23 tvadveṇunādamupakarṇya vibho! taruṇyas
10.65.24 tattādṛśaṃ kamapi cittavimohamāpuḥ ||
10.65.31 tā gehakṛtyaniratāstanayaprasaktāḥ
10.65.32 kāntopasevanaparāśca saroruhākṣyaḥ |
10.65.33 sarvaṃ visṛjya muralīravamohitāste
10.65.34 kāntāradeśamayi kāntatano! sametāḥ ||
10.65.41 kāścinnijāṅgaparibhūṣaṇamādadhānā
10.65.42 veṇupraṇādamupakarṇya kṛtārdhabhūṣāḥ |
10.65.43 tvāmāgatā nanu tathaiva vibhūṣitābhyas
10.65.44 tā eva saṃrurucire tava locanāya ||
10.65.51 hāraṃ nitambabhūvi kācana dhārayantī
10.65.52 kāñcīṃ ca kaṇṭhabhuvi deva! samāgatā tvām |
10.65.53 hāritvamātmajaghanasya mukunda! tubhyaṃ
10.65.54 vyaktaṃ babhāṣa iva mugdhasukhī viśeṣāt ||
10.65.61 kācitkuce punarasajjitakañculīkā
10.65.62 vyāmohataḥ paravadhūbhiralakṣyamāṇā |
10.65.63 tvāmāyayau nirupamapraṇayātibhāra
10.65.64 rājyābhiṣekavidhaye kalaśīdhareva ||
10.65.71 kāścidgṛhātkila niretumapārayantyas
10.65.72 tvāmeva deva! hṛdaye sudṛḍhaṃ vibhāvya |
10.65.73 dehaṃ vidhūya paracitsukharūpamekaṃ
10.65.74 tvāmāviśan paramimā nanu dhanyadhanyāḥ ||
10.65.81 jārātmanā na paramātmatayā smarantyo
10.65.82 nāryo gatāḥ paramahaṃsagatiṃ kṣaṇena |
10.65.83 tattvāṃ prakāśaparamātmatanuṃ kathañci
10.65.84 ccitte vahannamṛtamaśramamaśnuvīya ||
10.65.91 abhyāgatābhirabhito vrajasundarībhir
10.65.92 mugdhāsmitārdravadanaḥ karuṇāvalokī |
10.65.93 nissīmakāntijaladhistvamavekṣyamāṇo
10.65.94 viśvaikahṛdya! hara me parameśa! rogān ||
10.66.11 upayātānāṃ sudṛśāṃ kusumāyudhabāṇapātavivaśānām |
10.66.12 abhivāñchitaṃ vidhātuṃ kṛtamatirapi tā jagātha vāmamiva ||
10.66.21 gaganagataṃ muninivahaṃ śrāvayituṃ jagitha kulavadhūdharmam |
10.66.22 dharmyaṃ khalu te vacanaṃ karma tu no nirmalasya viśvāsyam ||
10.66.31 ākarṇya te pratīpāṃ vāṇīmeṇīdṛśaḥ paraṃ dīnāḥ |
10.66.32 mā mā karuṇāsindho! parityajetyaticiraṃ vilepustāḥ ||
10.66.41 tāsāṃ ruditairlapitaiḥ karuṇākulamānaso murāre! tvam |
10.66.42 tābhiḥ samaṃ pravṛtto yamunāpulineṣu kāmamabhirantum ||
10.66.51 candrakarasyandalasatsundarayamunātaṭāntavīthīṣu |
10.66.52 gopījanottarīyairāpāditasaṃstaro nyaṣīdastvam ||
10.66.61 sumadhuranarmālapanaiḥ karasaṃgrahaṇaiśca cumbanollāsaiḥ |
10.66.62 gāḍhāliṅganasaṅgaistvamaṅganālokamākulīcakṛṣe ||
10.66.71 vāsoharaṇadine yadvāsoharaṇaṃ pratiśrutaṃ tāsām |
10.66.72 tadapi vibho! rasavivaśasvāntānāṃ kāntasubhruvāmadadhāḥ ||
10.66.81 kandalitadharmaleśaṃ kundamṛdusmeravaktrapāthojam |
10.66.82 nandasuta! tvāṃ trijagatsundaramupagūhya nanditā bālāḥ ||
10.66.91 viraheśvaṅgāramayaḥ śṛṅgāramayaśca saṅgame'pi tvam |
10.66.92 nitarāmaṅgāramayastatra punaḥ saṅgame'pi citramidam ||
10.66.101 rādhātuṅgapayodharasādhuparirambhalolupātmānam |
10.66.102 ārādhaye bhavantaṃ pavanapurādhīśa! śamaya sakalagadān ||
10.67.11 sphuratparānandarasātmakena tvayā samāsāditabhohalīlāḥ |
10.67.12 asīmamānandabharaṃ prapannā mahāntamāpurmadamambujākṣyaḥ ||
10.67.21 nilīyate'sau mayi mayyamāyaṃ ramāpatirviśvamanobhirāmaḥ |
10.67.22 itisma sarvāḥ kalitābhimānā nirīkṣya govinda! tirohito'bhūḥ ||
10.67.31 rādhābhidhāṃ tāvadajātagarvāmatipriyāṃ gopavadhūṃ murāre! |
10.67.32 bhavānupādāya gato vidūraṃ tayā saha svairavihārakārī ||
10.67.41 tirohite'tha tvayi jātatāpāḥ samaṃ sametāḥ kamalāyatākṣyaḥ |
10.67.42 vane vane tvāṃ parimārgayantyo viṣādamāpurbhagavannapāram ||
10.67.51 hā cūta! hā campaka! karṇikāra! hā mallike! mālati! bālavallyaḥ! |
10.67.52 kiṃ vīkṣito no hṛdayaikacora ityādi tāstvatpravaṇā vilepuḥ ||
10.67.61 nirīkṣito'yaṃ sakhi! paṅkajākṣaḥ puro mametyākulamālapantī |
10.67.62 tvāṃ bhāvanācakṣuṣi vīkṣya kācittāpaṃ sakhīnāṃ dviguṇīcakāra ||
10.67.71 tvadātmikāstā yamunātaṭānte tavānucakruḥ kila ceṣṭitāni |
10.67.72 vicitya bhūyo'pi tathaiva mānāttvayā viyuktāṃ dadṛśuśca rādhām ||
10.67.81 tataḥ samaṃ tā vipine samantāttamovatārāvadhi mārgayantyaḥ |
10.67.82 punarvimiśrā yamunātaṭānte bhṛśaṃ vilepuśca jagurguṇāṃste ||
10.67.91 tathāvyathāsaṃkulamānasānāṃ vrajāṅganānāṃ karuṇaikasindho! |
10.67.92 jagattrayīmohanamohanātmā tvaṃ prādurāsīrayi mandahāsī ||
10.67.101 sandigdhasandharśanamātmakāntaṃ tvāṃ vīkṣya tanvyaḥ sahasā tadānīm |
10.67.102 kiṃ kiṃ na cakruḥ pramadātibhārātsa tvaṃ gadātpālaya māruteśa! ||
10.68.11 tava vilokanādgoppikājanāḥ pramadasaṃkulāḥ paṅkajekṣaṇa! |
10.68.12 amṛtadhāraya saṃplutā iva stimitatāṃ dadhustvatpurogatāḥ ||
10.68.21 tadanu kācana tvatkarāmbujaṃ sapadi gṛhṇatī nirviśaṅkitam |
10.68.22 ghanapayodhare sannidhāya sā pulakasaṃvṛtā tasthuṣī ciram ||
10.68.31 tava vibho! purā komalaṃ bhujaṃ nijagalāntare paryaveṣṭayat |
10.68.32 galasamudgataṃ prāṇamārutaṃ pratinirundhatīvātiharṣulā ||
10.68.41 apagatatrapā kāpi kāminī tava mukhāmbujātpūgacarvitam |
10.68.42 pratigṛhayya tadvaktrapaṅkaje nidadhatī gatā pūrṇakāmatām ||
10.68.51 vikaruṇo vane saṃvihāya māmapagato'si kā tvāmi spṛśet |
10.68.52 iti saroṣayā tavadekayā sajalalocanaṃ vīkṣito bhavān ||
10.68.61 iti mudākulairvallavījanaiḥ samamupāgato yāmune taṭe |
10.68.62 mṛdukucāmbaraiḥ kalpitāsane ghusṛṇabhāsure paryaśobhathāḥ ||
10.68.71 katividhā kṛpā ke'pi sarvato dhṛtadayodayāḥ kecidāśrite |
10.68.72 katicidīdṛśā mādṛśeṣvatpītyabhihito bhavān vallavījanaiḥ ||
10.68.81 ayi kumārikā! naiva śaṅkyatāṃ kaṭhinatā mayi premakātare |
10.68.82 mayi yu cetaso vo'nuvṛttaye kṛtamidaṃ mayetyucivān bhavān ||
10.68.91 ayi niśamyatāṃ jīvavallabhāḥ! priyatamo jano nedṛśo mama |
10.68.92 tadiha ramyatāṃ ramyayāminīṣvanuparodhamityālapo vibho! ||
10.68.101 iti girādhikaṃ modamedurairvrajavadhūjanaiḥ sākamāraman |
10.68.102 kalitakautuko rāsakhelane gurupurīpate! pāhi māṃ gadāt ||
10.69.11 keśapāśadhṛtapicchikāvitati sañcalanmakarakuṇḍalaṃ
10.69.12 hārajālavanamālikālalitamaṅgarāgaghanasaurabham |
10.69.13 pītaceladhṛtakāñcikāñcitamudañcadaṃśumaṇinūpuraṃ
10.69.14 rāsakeliparibhūṣitaṃ tava hi rūpamīśa! kalayāmahe ||
10.69.21 tāvadeva kṛtamaṇḍane kalitakañculīkakucamaṇḍale
10.69.22 gaṇḍalolamaṇikuṇḍale yuvatimaṇḍale'tha parimaṇḍale |
10.69.23 antarā sakalasundarīyugalamindirāramaṇa! sañcaran
10.69.24 mañjulāṃ tadanu rāsakelimayi kañjanābha! samupādadhāḥ ||
10.69.31 vāsudeva! tava bhāsamānamiha rāsakelirasasaurabhaṃ
10.69.32 dūrato'pi khalu nāradāgaditamākalayya kutukākulā |
10.69.33 veṣabhūṣaṇavilāsapeśalavilāsinīśatasamāvṛtā
10.69.34 nākato yugapadāgatā viyati vegato'tha suramaṇḍalī ||
10.69.41 veṇunādakṛtatānadānakalagānarāgagatiyojanā
10.69.42 lobhanīyamṛdupādapātakṛtatālamelanamanoharam |
10.69.43 pāṇisaṃkvaṇitakaṅkaṇaṃ ca muhuraṃsalambitakarāmbujaṃṣ
10.69.44 śroṇibimbacaladambaraṃ bhajata rāsakelirasaḍambaram ||
10.69.51 śraddhayā viracitānugānakṛtatāratāramadhurasvare
10.69.52 nartane'tha lalitāṅgahāralulitāṅgahāramaṇibhūṣaṇe |
10.69.53 sammadena kṛtapuṣpavarṣamalamunmiṣaddiviṣadāṃ kulaṃ
10.69.54 cinmaye tvayi nilīyamānamiva saṃmumoha savadhūkulam ||
10.69.61 svinnasannatanuvallarī tadanu kāpi nāma paśupāṅganā
10.69.62 kāntamaṃsamavalambate sma tava tāntibhāramukulekṣaṇā |
10.69.63 kācidācalitakuntalā navapaṭīrasāranavasaurabhaṃ
10.69.64 vañcanena tava sañcucumba bhujamañcitorupulakāṅkuram ||
10.69.71 kāpi gaṇḍabhuvi sannidhāya nijagaṇḍamākulitakuṇḍalaṃ
10.69.72 puṇyapūranidhiranvavāpa tava pūgacarvitarasāmṛtam |
10.69.73 indirāvihṛtimandiraṃ bhuvanasundaraṃ hi naṭanāntare
10.69.74 tvāmavāpya dadhuraṅganāḥ kimu na sammadonmadadaśāntaram ||
10.69.81 gānamīśa! virataṃ krameṇa kila vādyamelanamupārataṃ
10.69.82 brahmasammadarasākulāḥ sadasi kevalaṃ nanṛturaṅganāḥ |
10.69.83 nāvidannapi ca nīvikāṃ kimapi kuntalīmapi ca kañculīṃ
10.69.84 jyotiṣāmapi kadambakaṃ divi vilambitaṃ kimaparaṃ bruve ||
10.69.91 modasīmni bhuvanaṃ vilāpya vihṛtiṃ samāpya ca tato vibho!
10.69.92 kelisamptṛditanirmalāṅganavagharmaleśasubhagātmanām  |
10.69.93 manmathāsahanacetasāṃ paśupayoṣitāṃ sukṛtacoditas
10.69.94 tāvadākalitamūrtirādadhitha māravīraparamotsavān ||
10.69.101 kelibhedaparilolitābhiratilālitābhirabalālibhiḥ
10.69.102 svairamīśa! nanu sūrajāpayasi cāru nāma vihṛtiṃ vyadhāḥ |
10.69.103 kānane'pi ca visāriśītalakiśoramārutamanohare
10.69.104 sūnasaurabhamaye vilesitha vilāsinīśatavimohanam ||
10.69.111 kāminīriti hi yāminīṣu khalu kāmanīyakanidhe! bhavān
10.69.112 pūrṇasammadarasārṇavaṃ kamapi yogigamyamanubhāvayan |
10.69.113 brahmaśaṅkaramukhānapīha paśupāṅganāsu bahumānayan
10.69.114 bhaktalokagamanīyarūpa! kamanīya! kṛṣṇa! paripāhi mām ||
10.70.11 iti tvayi rasākulaṃ ramitavallabhe vallavāḥ
10.70.12 kadāpi punarambikākamiturambikākānane |
10.70.13 sametya bhavatā samaṃ niśi niṣevya divyotsavaṃ
10.70.14 sukhaṃ suṣupuragrasīdvrajapamugranāgastadā ||
10.70.21 samunmukhamatholmukairabhihate'pi tasmin balā
10.70.22 damuñcati bhavatpade nyapati pāhi pāhīti taiḥ |
10.70.23 tadā khalu padā bhavān samupagamya pasparśa taṃ
10.70.24 babhau sa ca nijāṃ tanuṃ samupasādya vaidyādharīm ||
10.70.31 sudarśanadhara! prabho! nanu sudarśanākhyo'smyahaṃ
10.70.32 sunīn kvacidapāhasaṃ ta iha māṃ vyadhurvāhasam |
10.70.33 bhavatpadasamarpaṇādamalatāṃ gato'smītyasau
10.70.34 stuvannijapadaṃ yayau vrajapadaṃ ca gopā mudā ||
10.70.41 kadāpi khalu sīriṇā viharati tvayi strījanair
10.70.42 jahārdhanadānugaḥ sa kila śaṅkhacūḍo'balāḥ |
10.70.43 atidrutamanudrutastamatha muktanārījanaṃ
10.70.44 rurojitha śiromaṇiṃ halabhṛte ca tasyādadāḥ ||
10.70.51 dineṣu ca suhṛjjanaiḥ saha vaneṣu līlāparaṃ
10.70.52 manobhavamanoharaṃ rasitaveṇunādāmṛtam |
10.70.53 bhavantamamarīdṛśāmamṛtapāraṇādāyinaṃ
10.70.54 vicintya kimu nālapan virahatāpitā gopikāḥ ||
10.70.61 bhojarājabhṛtakastvatha kaścitkaṣṭaduṣṭapathadṛṣṭirariṣṭaḥ |
10.70.62 niṣṭhurākṛtirapaṣṭhuninādastiṣṭhate sma bhavate vṛṣarūpī ||
10.70.71 śākvaro'tha jagatīdhṛtihārī mūrtimeṣa bṛhatīṃ pradadhānaḥ |
10.70.72 paṅktimāśu parighūrṇya paśūnāṃ chandasāṃ nidhimavāpa bhavantam ||
10.70.81 tuṅgaśṛṅgamukhamāśvabhiyantaṃ sasaṅgṛhayya rabhasādabhiyaṃ tam |
10.70.82 bhadrarūpamapi daityamabhadraṃ mardayannamadayaḥ suralokam ||
10.70.91 citramadya bhagavan! vṛṣaghātātsusthirājani vṛṣasthitirurvyām |
10.70.92 vardhate ca vṛṣacetasi bhūyānmoda ityabhinuto'si surastvam ||
10.70.101 aukṣakāṇi! paridhāvata dūraṃ vīkṣyatāmayamihokṣavibhedī |
10.70.102 itthamāttahasitaiḥ saha gopairgehagastvamava vātapureśa! ||
10.71.11 yatneṣu sarveṣvapi nāvakeśī keśī sa bhojeśituriṣṭabandhuḥ |
10.71.12 tvaṃ sindhujāvāpya itīva matvā saṃprāptavān sindhujavājirūpaḥ ||
10.71.21 gandharvatāmeṣa gato'pi rūkṣairnādaiḥ samudvejitasarvalokaḥ |
10.71.22 bhavadvilokāvadhi gopavāṭīṃ pramardya pāpaḥ punarā patattvām ||
10.71.31 tārkṣyārpitāṅghrestava tārkṣya eṣa cikṣepa vakṣobhuvi nāma pādam |
10.71.32 bhṛgoḥ padāghātakathaṃ niśamya svenāpi śakyaṃ taditīva mohāt ||
10.71.41 pravañcayannasya khurāñcalaṃ drāgamuṃ ca vikṣepitha dūradūram |
10.71.42 saṃmūrchito'pi hatimūrchitena krodhoṣmaṇā khāditumādrutastvām ||
10.71.51 tvaṃ vāhadaṇḍe kṛtadhīśca bāhādaṇḍaṃ nyadhāstasya mukhe tadānīm |
10.71.52 tadvṛddhiruddhaśvasano gatāsuḥ saptībhavannapyayamaikyamāgāt ||
10.71.61 ālambhamātreṇa paśoḥ surāṇāṃ prasādake nūtna ivāśvamedhe |
10.71.62 kṛte tvayā harṣavaśātsurendrāstvāṃ tuṣṭuṣuḥ keśavanāmadheyam ||
10.71.71 kaṃsāya te śaurisutatvamuktvā taṃ tadvadhotkaṃ pratirudhya vācā |
10.71.72 prāptena keśikṣapaṇāvasāne śrīnāradena tvamabhiṣṭuto'bhūḥ ||
10.71.81 kadāpi gopaiḥ saha kānanānte nilāyanakrīḍanalolupaṃ tvām |
10.71.82 mayātmajaḥ prāpa durantamāyo vyomābhidho vyomacaroparodhī ||
10.71.91 sa corapālāyitavallaveṣu corāyito gopaśiśūn paśūṃśca |
10.71.92 guhāsu kṛtvā pidadhe śilābhistvayā ca buddhvā parimardito'bhūt ||
10.71.101 evaṃvidhaiścādbhutakelibhedairānandamūrchāmatulāṃ vrajasya |
10.71.102 pade pade nūtanayannasīmaṃ parātmarūpin! pavaneśa!pāyāḥ ||
10.72.11 kaṃso'tha nāradagirā vrajavāsinaṃ tvā
10.72.12 mākarṇya dīrṇahṛdayaḥ sa hi gāndineyam |
10.72.13 āhūya kārmukamakhacchalato bhavanta
10.72.14 mānetumenamahinodahināthaśāyin! ||
10.72.21 akrūra eṣa bhavadaṅghriparaścirāya
10.72.22 tvaddarśanākṣamamanāḥ kṣitipālabhītyā |
10.72.23 tasyājñayaiva punarīkṣitumudyatastvām
10.72.24 ānandabhāramatibhūritaraṃ babhāra ||
10.72.31 so'yaṃ rathena sukṛtī bhavato nivāsaṃ
10.72.32 gacchanmanorathagaṇāṃstvayi dhāryamāṇān |
10.72.33 āsvādayanmuhurapāyabhayena daivaṃ
10.72.34 saṃprārthayan pathi na kiñcidapi vyajānāt ||
10.72.41 drakṣyāmi devaśatagītagatiṃ mpumāṃsaṃ
10.72.42 sprakṣyāmi kiṃsvidapināma pariṣvajeya |
10.72.43 kiṃ vakṣyate sa khalu māṃ kva nu vīkṣitaḥ syā
10.72.44 ditthaṃ nināya sa bhavanmayameva mārgam ||
10.72.51 bhūyaḥ kramādabhiviśan bhavadaṅghripūtaṃ
10.72.52 bṛndāvanaṃ haraviriñcasurābhivandyam |
10.72.53 ānandamagna iva lagna iva pramohe
10.72.54 kiṃ kiṃ daśāntaramavāpa na paṅkajākṣa! ||
10.72.61 paśyannavandata bhavadvihṛtisthalāni
10.72.62 pāṃsuṣvaveṣṭata bhavaccaraṇāṅkiteṣu |
10.72.63 kiṃ brūmahe bahujanā hi tadāpi jātā
10.72.64 evaṃ tu bhaktiralā viralāḥ parātman! ||
10.72.71 sāyaṃ sa gopabhavanāni bhavaccaritra
10.72.72 gītāmṛtaprasṛtakarṇarasāyanāni |
10.72.73 paśyan pramodasariteva kilohyamāno
10.72.74 gacchan bhavadbhavana nnidhimanvayāsīt ||
10.72.81 tāvaddadarśa paśudohavilokalolaṃ
10.72.82 bhaktottamāgatimiva pratipālayantam |
10.72.83 bhūman! bhavantamayamagrajavantamantar
10.72.84 brahmānubhūtirasasindhumivodvamantam ||
10.72.91 sāyantanāplavaviśeṣaviviktagātrau
10.72.92 dvau pītanīlarucirāmbaralobhanīyau |
10.72.93 nātiprapañcadhṛtabhūṣaṇacāruveṣau
10.72.94 mandasmitārdravadanau sa yuvāṃ dadarśa ||
10.72.101 dūrādrathātsamavaruhya namantamena
10.72.102 mutthāpya bhaktakulamaulimathopagūhan |
10.72.103 harṣānmitākṣaragirā kuśalānuyogī
10.72.104 pāṇiṃ pragrhya sabalo'tha gṛhaṃ ninetha ||
10.72.111 nandena sākamamitādaramarcayitvā
10.72.112 taṃ yādavaṃ taduditāṃ niśamayya vārtām |
10.72.113 gopeṣu bhūpatinideśakathāṃ nivedya
10.72.114 nānākathābhiriha tena niśāmanaiṣīḥ ||
10.72.121 candrāgṛhe kimuta candrabhagāgṛhe nu
10.72.122 rādhāgṛhe nu bhavane kimu maitravinde |
10.72.123 dhūrtto vilambata iti pramadābhiruccai
10.72.124 rāśaṅkito niśi marutpuranātha! pāyāḥ ||
10.73.11 miśamayya tavātha yānavārtāṃ bhṛśamārtāḥ paśupālabālikāstāḥ |
10.73.12 kimidaṃ kimidaṃ kathaṃ nvitīmāḥ samavetāḥ paridevitānyakurvan ||
10.73.21 karuṇānidhireṣu nandasūnuḥ kathamasmān visṛjedananyanāthāḥ |
10.73.22 bata naḥ kimu daivamevamāsīditi tāstvadgatamānasā vilepuḥ ||
10.73.31 caramaprahare pratiṣṭhamānaḥ saha pitrā nijamitramaṇḍalaiśca |
10.73.32 paritāpabharaṃ nitambinīnāṃ śamayiṣyan vyamucaḥ sakhāyamekam ||
10.73.41 acirādupayāmi sannidhiṃ vo bhavitā sādhu mayaiva saṅgamaśrīḥ |
10.73.42 amṛtāmbunidhau nimajjayiṣye drutamityāśvasitā vadhūrakārṣīḥ ||
10.73.51 saviṣādabharaṃ sayāñcamuccairatidūraṃ vanitābhirīkṣyamāṇaḥ |
10.73.52 mṛdu taddiśi pātayannapāṅgān sabalo'krūrarathena nirgato'bhūḥ ||
10.73.61 anasā bahulena vallavānāṃ manasā canugato'tha vallabhānām |
10.73.62 vanamārtabhṛgaṃ viṣaṇṇavṛkṣaṃ samatīto yamunātaṭīmayāsīḥ ||
10.73.71 miyamāya nimajjya vāriṇi tvamabhivīkṣyātha rathe'pi gāndineyaḥ |
10.73.72 vivaśo'jani kinnvidaṃ vibhoste nanu citraṃ tvavalokanaṃ samantāt ||
10.73.81 punareṣa nimajjya puṇyaśālī puruṣaṃ tvāṃ paramaṃ bhumaṅgabhoge |
10.73.82 arikambugadāmbujaiḥ sphurantaṃ surasiddhoghaparītamāluloke ||
10.73.91 sa tadā paramātmasaukhyasindhau vinimagnaḥ praṇuvan prakārabhedaiḥ |
10.73.92 avilokya punaśca harṣasindhoranuvṛttyā pulakāvṛto yayau tvām ||
10.73.101 kimu śītalimā mahān jale yatpulako'sāviti coditena tena |
10.73.102 atiharṣaniruttareṇa sārdhaṃ rathavāsī pavaneśa! pāhi māṃ tvam ||
10.74.11 samprāpto mathurāṃ dinārdhavigame tatrāntarasmin vasa
10.74.12 nnārāme vihitāśanaḥ sakhijanairyātaḥ purīmīkṣitum |
10.74.13 prāpo rājapathaṃ ciraśrutidhṛtavyālokakautūhala
10.74.14 strīpuṃsodyadagaṇyapuṇyanigalairākṛṣyamāṇo nu kim ||
10.74.21 tvatpādaddutivatsarāgasubhagāstvanmūrtivadyoṣitaḥ
10.74.22 samprāptā vilasatpayodhararuco lolā bhavaddṛṣṭivat |
10.74.23 hāriṇyastvadurassthalīvadayi te mandasmitaprauḍhiva
10.74.24 nnairmalyollasitāḥ kacaugharucivadrājatkalāpāśritāḥ ||
10.74.31 tāsāmākalayannapāṅgavalanairmodaṃ praharṣādbhuta
10.74.32 vyāloleṣu janeṣu tatra rajakaṃ kañcitpaṭīṃ prārthayan |
10.74.33 kaste dāsyati rājakīyavasanaṃ yāhīti tenoditaḥ
10.74.34 sadyastasya kareṇa śīrṣamahṛthāḥ so'pyāpa puṇyāṃ gatim ||
10.74.41 bhūyo vāyakamekamāyatamatiṃ toṣeṇa veṣocitaṃ
10.74.42 dāśvāṃsaṃ svapadaṃ ninetha sukṛtaṃ ko veda jīvātmanām |
10.74.43 mālābhiḥ stabakaiḥ stavairapi punarmālākṛtā mānito
10.74.44 bhaktiṃ tena vṛtāṃ dideśitha parāṃ lakṣmīṃ ca lakṣmīpate! ||
10.74.51 kubjāmabjavilocanāṃ pathi punardṛṣṭvāṅgarāge tayā
10.74.52 datte sādhu kilāṅgarāgamadadāstasyā mahāntaṃ hṛdi |
10.74.53 cittasthāmṛjutāmatha prathayituṃ gātre'pi tasyāḥ sphuṭaṃ
10.74.54 gṛhṇanmañju kareṇa tāmudanayastāvajjagatsundarīm ||
10.74.61 tāvanniścitavaibhavāstava vibho! nātyantapāpā janā
10.74.62 yatkiñciddadate sma śaktyanuguṇaṃ tāmbulamālyādikam |
10.74.63 gṛhṇānaḥ kusumādi kiñcana tadā mārge nibaddhāñjalir
10.74.64 nātiṣṭhaṃ bata yato'dya vipulāmārtiṃ vrajāmi prabho! ||
10.74.71 eṣyāmīti vimuktayāpi bhagavannālepadātryā tayā
10.74.72 dūrātkātarayā nirīkṣitagatistvaṃ prāviśo gopuram |
10.74.73 āghoṣānumitatvadāgamamahāharṣollaladdevakī
10.74.74 vakṣojapragalatpayorasamiṣāttvatkīrtirantargatā ||
10.74.81 āviṣṭo nagarīṃ mahotsavavatīṃ kodaṇḍaśālāṃ vrajan
10.74.82 mādhuryeṇa nu tejasā nu puruṣairdūreṇa dattāntaraḥ |
10.74.83 sragbhirbhūṣitamarcitaṃ varadhanurmā meti vādātpuraḥ
10.74.84 prāgṛhṇāḥ samaropayaḥ kila samākrākṣīrabhāṅkṣīrapi ||
10.74.91 śvaḥ kaṃsakṣapaṇotsavasya purataḥ prārambhatūryopama
10.74.92 ścāpadhvaṃsamahādhvanistava vibho! devānaromāñcayat |
10.74.93 kaṃsasyāpi ca vepathustaduditaḥ kodaṇḍakhaṇḍadvayī
10.74.94 caṇḍābhyāhatarakṣipūruṣaravairutkūlito'bhūttvayā ||
10.74.101 śiṣṭairduṣṭajanaiśca dṛṣṭamihimā prītyā ca bhītyā tataḥ
10.74.102 saṃpaśyan purasampadaṃ pravivaran sāyaṃ gato vāṭikām |
10.74.103 śrīdāmnā saha rādhikāvirahajaṃ khedaṃ vadan prasvapa
10.74.104 nnānandannavatārakāryaghaṭanādvāteśa! saṃrakṣa mām ||
10.75.11 prātaḥ santrastabhojakṣitipativacasā prastute mallatūrye
10.75.12 saṅghe rājñāṃ ca mañcānabhiyayuṣi gate nandagope'pi harmyam |
10.75.13 kaṃse saudhādhirūḍhe tvamapi sahabalaḥ sānugaścāruveṣo
10.75.14 raṅgadvāraṃ gato'bhūḥ kupitakuvalayāpīḍanāgāvalīḍham ||
10.75.21 pāpiṣṭhāpehi mārgāddrutamiti vacasā niṣṭurakruddhabuddhe
10.75.22 ragbaṣṭhasya praṇodādadhikajavajuṣā hastinā gṛhyamāṇaḥ |
10.75.23 kelīmukto'tha gopīkucakalaśaciraspardhinaṃ kumbhamasya
10.75.24 vyāhatyālīyathāstvaṃ caraṇabhuvi punarnirgato valguhāsī ||
10.75.31 hastaprāpyo'pyagamyo jhaṭiti munijanasyeva dhāvan gajendraṃ
10.75.32 krīḍannāpatya bhūmau punarabhipatatastasya dantaṃ sajīvam |
10.75.33 mūlādunmūlya tanmūlagamahitamahāmauktikānyātmamitre
10.75.34 prādāstvaṃ hāramebhirlalitaviracitaṃ rādhikāyai diśeti ||
10.75.41 gṛhṇānaṃ dantamaṃse yutamatha halinā raṅgamaṅgāviśantaṃ
10.75.42 tvāṃ maṅgalyāṅgabhaṅgīrabhasahṛtamanolocanā vīkṣya lokāḥ |
10.75.43 haṃho dhanyo nu nando nahi nahi paśupālāṅganā no yaśodā
10.75.44 no no dhanyekṣaṇāḥ smastrijagati vayameveti sarve śaśaṃsuḥ ||
10.75.51 pūrṇaṃ brahmaiva sākṣānniravadhiparamānandasāndraprakāśaṃ
10.75.52 gopeṣu tvaṃ vyalāsīrna khalu bahujanaistāvadāvedito'bhūḥ |
10.75.53 dṛṣṭvātha tvāṃ tadedaṃprathamamupagate puṇyakāle janaughāḥ
10.75.54 pūrṇānandā vipāpāḥ sarasamabhijagustvatkṛtāni smṛtāni ||
10.75.61 cāṇūro mallavīrastadanu nṛpagirā muṣṭiko muṣṭiśālī
10.75.62 tvāṃ rāmaṃ cābhipede jhaṭajhaṭiti mitho muṣṭipātātirūkṣam |
10.75.63 utpātāpātanākarṣaṇavividharaṇānyāsatāṃ tatra citraṃ
10.75.64 mṛtyoḥ prāgeva mallaprabhuragamadayaṃ bhūriśo bandhamokṣān ||
10.75.71 hā dhikkaṣṭaṃ kumaārau sulalitavapuṣau mallavīrau kaṭhorau
10.75.72 na drakṣyāmo vrajāmastvaritamiti jane bhāṣamāṇe tadānīm |
10.75.73 cāṇūraṃ taṃ karādbhrāmaṇavigaladasuṃ pothayāmāsithorvyāṃ
10.75.74 piṣṭo'bhūnmuṣṭiko'pi drutamatha halinā naṣṭaśiṣṭairdadhāve ||
10.75.81 kaṃsaḥ saṃvāryaṃ tṅryaṃ khalamatiravidan kāryamāryān pitṃstā
10.75.82 nāhantuṃ vyāptamūrtestava ca samaśiṣadmañcamañcannudañcata
10.75.83 khaṅgavyāvalgadussaṃgrahamapi ca haṭhātprāgrahīraugrasenim ||
10.75.91 sadyo niṣpiṣṭasandhiṃ bhuvi narapatimāpātya tasyopariṣṭāt
10.75.92 tvayyāpātye tadaiva tvadupari patitā nākināṃ puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ |
10.75.93 kiṃ kiṃ brūmastadānīṃ satatamapi bhiyā tvadgatātmā sa bheje
10.75.94 sāyujyaṃ tvadvadhotthā parama! paramiyaṃ vāsanā kālanemeḥ ||
10.75.101 tadbhrātnaṣṭa piṣṭvā drutamatha pitarau sannamannugrasenaṃ
10.75.102 kṛtvā rājānamuccairyadukulamakhilaṃ modayan kāmadānaiḥ |
10.75.103 bhaktānāmuttamaṃ coddhavamamaragurorāptanītiṃ sakhāyaṃ
10.75.104 labdhvā tuṣṭo nagaryāṃ pavanapurapate! rundhi me sarvarogān ||
10.76.11 gatvā sāndīpanimatha catuṣṣaṣṭimātrairahobhiḥ
10.76.12 sarvajñastvaṃ saha musalinā sarvavidyāṃ gṛhītvā |
10.76.13 putraṃ naṣṭaṃ yamanilayanādāhṛtaṃ dakṣiṇārthaṃ
10.76.14 dattvā tasmai nijapuramagā nādayan pāñcajanyam ||
10.76.21 smṛtvā smṛtvā paśupasudṛśaḥ premabhārapraṇunnāḥ
10.76.22 kāruṇyena tvamapi vivaśaḥ prahiṇoruddhavaṃ tam |
10.76.23 kiñcāmuṣmai paramasuhṛde bhaktavaryāya tāsāṃ
10.76.24 bhaktyudrekaṃ sakalabhuvane durlabhaṃ darśayiṣyan ||
10.76.31 tvanmāhātmyaprathimapiśunaṃ gokulaṃ prāpya sāyaṃ
10.76.32 tvadvārtābhirbahu sa ramayāmāsa nandaṃ yaśodām |
10.76.33 prātardṛṣṭvā maṇimayarathaṃ śaṅkitāḥ paṅkajākṣyaḥ
10.76.34 śrutvau prāptaṃ bhavadanucaraṃ tyaktakāryāḥ samīyuḥ ||
10.76.41 dṛṣṭvā cainaṃ tvadupamalasadveṣabhūṣābhirāmaṃ
10.76.42 smṛtvā smṛtvā tava vilasitānyuccakaistāni tāni |
10.76.43 ruddhālāpāḥ kathamapi punargadgadāṃ vācamūcuḥ
10.76.44 saujanyādīnnijaparabhidāmapyalaṃ vismarantyaḥ ||
10.76.51 śrīman! kiṃ tvaṃ pitṛjanakṛte preṣito nirdayena
10.76.52 kvāsau kānto nagarasudṛśāṃ hā hare! nātha! pāyāḥ |
10.76.53 āśleṣāṇāmamṛtavapuṣo hanta te cumbanānā
10.76.54 munmādānāṃ kuhakavacasāṃ vismaretkānta! kā vā ||
10.76.61 rāsakrīḍālulitalalitaṃ viślathatkeśapāśaṃ
10.76.62 mandodbhinnaśramajalakaṇaṃ lobhanīyaṃ tvadaṅgam |
10.76.63 kāruṇyābdhe! sakṛdapi samāliṅgituṃ darśayeti
10.76.64 premonmādādbhuvanamadana! tvatpriyāstvāṃ vilepuḥ ||
10.76.71 evamprāyairvivaśavacanairākulā gopikāstās
10.76.72 tvatsandeśaiḥ prakṛtimanayatso'tha vijñānagarbhaiḥ |
10.76.73 bhūyastābhirmuditamatibhistvanmayībhirvadhūbhis
10.76.74 tattadvārtāsarasamanayatkānicidvāsarāṇi ||
10.76.81 tvatprodgāṇaiḥ sahitamaniśaṃ sarvato gehakṛtyaṃ
10.76.82 tvadvārtaiva prasarati mithaḥ saiva cotsvāpalāpāḥ |
10.76.83 ceṣṭāḥ prāyastvadanukṛtayastvanmayaṃ sarvamevaṃ
10.76.84 dṛṣṭvā tatra vyamuhadadhikaṃ vismayāduddhavo'yam ||
10.76.91 rādhāyā me priyatamamidaṃ matpriyaivaṃ bravīti
10.76.92 tvaṃ kiṃ maunaṃ kalayasi sakhe! māninī matpriyeva |
10.76.93 ityādyeva pravadati sakhi! tvatpriyo nirjane mā
10.76.94 mitthaṃvādairaramayadayaṃ tvatpriyāmutpalākṣīm ||
10.76.101 eṣyāmi drāganupagamanaṃ kevalaṃ kāryabhārād
10.76.102 viśleṣe'pi smaraṇadṛḍhatāsambhavānmāstu khedaḥ |
10.76.103 brahmānande milati nacirātsaṅgamo vā viyogas
10.76.104 tulyo vaḥ syāditi tava girā so'karonnirvyathāstāḥ ||
10.76.111 evaṃ bhaktiḥ sakalabhuvane neśitā na śrutā vā
10.76.112 kiṃ śāstraughaiḥ kimiha tapasā gopikābhyo namo'stu |
10.76.113 ityānandākulamupagataṃ gokulāduddhavaṃ taṃ
10.76.114 dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭo gurupurapate! pāhi māmāmayaughāt ||
10.77.11 sairandhryāstadanu ciraṃ smarāturāyā
10.77.12 yāto'bhūḥ salalitamuddhavena sārdham |
10.77.13 āvāsaṃ tvadupagamotsavaṃ sadaiva
10.77.14 dhyāyantyāḥ pratidinavāsasajjikāyāḥ ||
10.77.21 upagate tvayi pūrṇamanorathāṃ
10.77.22 pramadasambhramakamprapayodharām |
10.77.23 vividhamānanamādadhatīṃ mudā
10.77.24 rahasi tāṃ ramayañcakṛṣe sukham ||
10.77.31 pṛṣṭā varaṃ punarasāvavṛṇodvarākī
10.77.32 bhūyastvayā suratameva niśāntareṣu |
10.77.33 sāyujyamastviti vadedbudha eva kāmaṃ
10.77.34 sāmīpyamastvaniśāmityapi nābravītkim ||
10.77.41 tato bhavān deva! niśāsu kāsucin
10.77.42 mṛgīdṛśaṃ tāṃ nibhṛtaṃ vinodayan |
10.77.43 adādupaśloka iti śrutaṃ sutaṃ
10.77.44 sa nāradātsāttvatatantravidbabhau ||
10.77.51 akrūramandiramito'tha baloddhavābhyā
10.77.52 mabhyarcito bahu nuto muditena tena |
10.77.53 enaṃ visṛjya vipināgatapāṇḍaveya
10.77.54 vṛttaṃ viveditha tathā dhṛtarāṣṭraceṣṭām ||
10.77.61 vighātājjāmātuḥ paramasuhṛdo bhojanṛpater
10.77.62 jarāsandhe rundhatyanavadhiruṣāndhe'tha mathurām |
10.77.63 rathādyairdyolabdhaiḥ katipayabalastvaṃ balayuta
10.77.64 strayoviṃśatyakṣauhiṇi tadupanītaṃ samahṛthāḥ ||
10.77.71 baddhaṃ balādatha balena balottaraṃ tvaṃ
10.77.72 bhūyo balodyamarasena mumocithainam |
10.77.73 niśśeṣadigjayasamāhṛtaviśvasainyāt
10.77.74 ko'nyastato hi balapauruṣavāṃstadānīm ||
10.77.81 bhagnaḥ sa lagnahṛdayo'pi nṛpaiḥ praṇunno
10.77.82 yuddhaṃ tvayā vyadhita ṣoḍaśakṛtva evam |
10.77.83 akṣauhiṇīḥ śiva śivāsya jaghantha viṣṇo!
10.77.84 sambhūya saikanavatitriśataṃ tadānīm ||
10.77.91 aṣṭādaśe'sya samare samupeyuṣi tvaṃ
10.77.92 dṛṣṭvā puro'tha yavanaṃ yavanatrikoṭyā |
10.77.93 tvaṣṭrā vidhāpya puramāśu payodhimadhye
10.77.94 tatrātha yogabalataḥ svajanānanaiṣīḥ ||
10.77.101 padbhyāṃ tvaṃ padmamālī cakitiva purānnirgato dhāvamāno
10.77.102 mleccheśenānuyāto vadhasukṛtavihīnena śaile nyalaiṣīḥ |
10.77.103 suptenāṅghryāhatena drutamatha mucukundena bhasmīkṛte'smin
10.77.104 bhūpāyāsmai guhānte sulalitavapuṣā tasthiṣe bhaktibhāje ||
10.77.111 ekṣvāko'haṃ virakto'smyakhilanṛpasukhe tvatprasādaikakāṅkṣī
10.77.112 hā deveti stuvantaṃ varavitatiṣu taṃ nispṛhaṃ vīkṣya hṛṣyan |
10.77.113 muktestulyāṃ ca bhaktiṃ dhutasakalamalaṃ mokṣamapyāśu dattvā
10.77.114 kāryaṃ hiṃsāviśuddhyai tapa iti ca tadā prārtha lokapratītyai ||
10.77.121 tadanu mathurāṃ gatvā hatvā camūṃ yavanāhṛtāṃ
10.77.122 magadhapatinā mārge sainyaiḥ pureva nivāritaḥ |
10.77.123 caramavijayaṃ darpāyāsmai pradāya palāyito
10.77.124 jaladhinagarīṃ yāto vātālayeśvara! pāhi mām ||
10.78.11 tridivavardhakivardhitakauśalaṃ tridaśdattasamastavibhūtimat |
10.78.12 jaladhimadhyagataḥ tvamabhūṣayo navapuraṃ vapurañcitarociṣā ||
10.78.21 daduṣi revatabhubhṛti revatīṃ halabhṛte tanayāṃ vidhiśāsanāt |
10.78.22 mahitamutsavaghoṣamapūpuṣaḥ samuditairmuditaiḥ saha yādavaiḥ ||
10.78.31 atha vidarbhasutāṃ khalu rukmiṇīṃ praṇayiṇīṃ tvayi deva! sahodaraḥ |
10.78.32 svayamaditsata cedimahībhuje svatamasā tamasādhumupāśrayan ||
10.78.41 ciradhṛtapraṇayā tvayi bālikā sapadi kāṅkṣitabhaṅgasamākulā |
10.78.42 tava nivedayituṃ dvijamādiśatsvakadanaṃ kadanaṅgavinirmitam ||
10.78.51 dvijasuto'pi ca tūrṇamupāyayau tava puraṃ hi durāśadurāsadam |
10.78.52 mudamavāpa ca sādarapūjitaḥ sa bhavatā bhavatāpahṛtā svayam ||
10.78.61 sa ca bhavantamavocata kuṇḍine nṛpasutā khalu rājati rukmiṇī |
10.78.62 tvayi samutsukayā nijadhīratārahitayā hi tayā prahito'smyaham ||
10.78.71 tava hṛtāsmi puraiva guṇairahaṃ harati māṃ kila cedinṛpo'dhunā |
10.78.72 ayi kṛpālaya! pālaya māmiti prajagade jagadekapate! tayā ||
10.78.81 aśaraṇāṃ yadi māṃ tvamupekṣase sapadi jīvitameva jāhāmyaham |
10.78.82 iti girā sutanoratanodbhṛśaṃ suhṛdayaṃ hṛdayaṃ tava kātaram ||
10.78.91 akathayastvamathainamaye sakhe! tadadhikā mama manmathavedanā |
10.78.92 nṛpasamakṣamupetya harāmyahaṃ tadayi tāṃ dayitāmasitekṣaṇām ||
10.78.101 pramuditena ca tena samaṃ tadā rathagato laghu kuṇḍinameyivān |
10.78.102 gurumarutpuranāyaka! me bhavān vitanutāṃ tanutāṃ nikhilāpadām ||
10.79.11 balasametabalānugato bhavān puramagāhata bhīṣmakamānitaḥ |
10.79.12 dvijasutaṃ tvadupāgamavādinaṃ dhṛtarasā tarasā praṇanāma sā ||
10.79.21 bhuvanakāntamavekṣya bhavadvapurnṛpasutasya niśamya ca ceṣṭitam |
10.79.22 vipulakhedajuṣāṃ puravāsināṃ saruditairuditairagamanniśā ||
10.79.31 tadanu vanditumindumukhī śivāṃ vihitamaṅgalabhūṣaṇabhāsurā |
10.79.32 niragamadbhavadarpitajīvitā svapurataḥ purataḥ subhaṭāvṛtā ||
10.79.41 kulavadhūbhirupetya kumārikā girisutāṃ paripūjya ca sādaram |
10.79.42 muhurayācata tatpadapaṅkaje nipatitā patitāṃ tava kevalam ||
10.79.51 samavalokya kutuhalasaṅkule nṛpakule nibhṛtaṃ tvayi ca sthite |
10.79.52 nṛpasutā niragādgirijālayātsuruciraṃ rucirañjitadiṅmukhā ||
10.79.61 bhuvanamohanarūparucā tadā vivaśitākhilarājakadambayā |
10.79.62 tvamapi deva! kaṭākṣavimokṣaṇaiḥ pramadayā madayāñcakṛṣe manāk ||
10.79.71 kva tu gamiṣyasi candramukhīti tāṃ sarasametya kareṇa haran kṣaṇāt |
10.79.72 samadhiropya rathaṃ tvamapāhṛthā bhuvi tato vitato ninado dviṣām ||
10.79.81 kva nu gataḥ paśupāla iti krudhā kṛtaraṇā yadubhiśca jitā nṛpāḥ |
10.79.82 na tu bhavānudacālyata tairaho piśunakaiḥ śunakairiva kesarī ||
10.79.91 tadanu rukmiṇamāgatamāhave vadhamupekṣya nibadhya virūpayan |
10.79.92 hṛtamadaṃ parimucya baloktibhiḥ puramayā ramayā saha kāntayā ||
10.79.101 navasamāgamaljjitamānasāṃ praṇayakautukajṛmbhitamanmathām |
10.79.102 aramayaḥ khalu nātha! yathāsukhaṃ rahasi tāṃ hasitāṃśulasanmukhīm ||
10.79.111 vividhanarma bhirevamaharniśaṃ pramadamākalayan punarekadā |
10.79.112 ṛjumateḥ kila vakrāgirā bhavān varatanoratanodatilolatām ||
10.79.121 tadadhikairatha lālanakauśalaiḥ praṇayinīmadhikaṃ ramayannimām |
10.79.122 ayi mukunda! bhavaccaritāni naḥ pragadatāṃ gadatāntimapākuru ||
10.80.11 satrājitastvamatha lubdhavadarkalabdhaṃ
10.80.12 divyaṃ spamantakamaṇiṃ bhagavannayācīḥ |
10.80.13 tatkāraṇaṃ bahuvidhaṃ mama bhāti nūnaṃ
10.80.14 tasyātmajāṃ tvayi ratāṃ chalato vivoḍhum ||
10.80.21 adattaṃ taṃ tubhyaṃ maṇivaramanenālpamanasā
10.80.22 prasenastadbhrātā galabhuvi vahan prāpmṛgayām |
10.80.23 ahannenaṃ siṃho maṇimahasi māṃsabhramavaśāt
10.80.24 kapīndrastaṃ hatvā maṇimapi ca bālāya dadivān ||
10.80.31 śaśaṃsuḥ satrājidgiramanu janāstvāṃ maṇiharaṃ
10.80.32 janānāṃ pīyūṣaṃ bhavati guṇināṃ doṣakaṇikā |
10.80.33 tataḥ sarvajño'pi svajanasahito mārgaṇaparaḥ
10.80.34 prasenaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā harimapi gato'bhūḥ kapiguhām ||
10.80.41 bhavantamavitarkayannativayāḥ svayaṃ jāmbavān
10.80.42 mukundaśaranaṃ hi māṃ ka iha roddhumityālapan |
10.80.43 vibho! raghupate! hare! jaya jayetyalaṃ muṣṭibhi
10.80.44 ścaraṃstava samarcanaṃ vyadhita bhaktacūḍāmaṇiḥ ||
10.80.51 buddhvātha tena dattāṃ navaramaṇīṃ varamaṇīṃ ca parigṛhṇan |
10.80.52 anugṛhṇannamumāgāḥ sapadi ca satrājite maṇiṃ prādāḥ ||
10.80.61 tadanu sa khalu vrīḍālolo vilolavilocanāṃ
10.80.62 duhitaramaho dhīmān bhāmāṃ giraiva parārpitām |
10.80.63 adita maṇinā tubhyaṃ labhyaṃ sametya bhavānapi
10.80.64 pramuditamanāstasyaivādānmaṇīṃ gahanāśayaḥ ||
10.80.71 vrīḍākulāṃ ramayati tvayi satyabhāmāṃ
10.80.72 kaunteyadāhakathayātha kurūn prayāte |
10.80.73 hī gāndineyakṛtavarmagirā nipātya
10.80.74 satrājitaṃ śatadhanurmaṇimājahāra ||
10.80.81 śokātkurūnupagatāmavalokya kāntāṃ
10.80.82 hatvā drutaṃ śatadhunaṃ samaharṣayastām |
10.80.83 ratne saśaṅka iva maithilagehametya
10.80.84 rāmo gadāṃ samaśiśikṣata dhārtarāṣṭram ||
10.80.91 akrūra eṣa bhagavan! bhavadicchayaiva
10.80.92 satrājitaḥ kucaritasya yuyoja hiṃsām |
10.80.93 akrūrato maṇimanāhṛtavān punastvaṃ
10.80.94 tasyaiva bhūtimupadhātumiti bruvanti ||
10.80.101 bhaktastvayi sthirataraḥ sa hi gāndineyas
10.80.102 tasyaiva kāpathamatiḥ kathamīśa! jātā |
10.80.103 vijñānavān praśamavānahamityudīrṇaṃ
10.80.104 garvaṃ dhruvaṃ śamayituṃ bhavatā kṛtaiva ||
10.80.111 yātaṃ bhayena kṛtavarmayutaṃ punasta
10.80.112 māhūya tadvinihitaṃ ca maṇiṃ prakāśya |
10.80.113 tatriva suvaratadhare vinidhāya tuṣyan
10.80.114 bhāmākucāntaraśayaḥ pavaneśa! pāyāḥ ||
10.81.11 snigdhāṃ mugdhāṃ satatamapi tāṃ lālayan satyabhāmāṃ
10.81.12 yāto bhūyaḥ saha khalu tayā yājñasenīvivāham |
10.81.13 pārthaprītyai punarapi panāgāsthito hastipuryāṃ
10.81.14 śakraprasthaṃ puramapi vibho! saṃvidhāyāgato'bhūḥ ||
10.81.21 bhadrāṃ bhadrāṃ bhavadavarajāṃ kauraveṇārthyamānāṃ
10.81.22 tvadvācā tāmahṛta kuhanāmaskarī śakrasūnuḥ |
10.81.23 tatra kruddhaṃ balamanunayan pratyagāstena sārdhaṃ
10.81.24 śakraprasthaṃ priyasakhamude satyabhāmāsahāyaḥ ||
10.81.31 tatra krīḍannapi ca yamunākūladṛṣṭāṃ gṛhītvā
10.81.32 tāṃ kālindīṃ nagaramagamaḥ khāṇḍavaprīṇitāgniḥ |
10.81.33 bhrātṛtrastāṃ praṇayavivaśāṃ deva! paitṛṣvaseyīṃ
10.81.34 rājñāṃ madhye sapadi jahriṣe mitravindāmavantīm ||
10.81.41 satyāṃ gatvā punarudavaho nagnajinnandanāṃ tāṃ
10.81.42 baddhvā saptāpi ca vṛṣavarān saptamūrtirnimeṣāt |
10.81.43 bhadrāṃ nāma pradaduratha te deva! santardanādyās
10.81.44 tatsodaryāṃ varada! bhavataḥ sāpi paitṛṣvaseyī ||
10.81.51 pārthādyairapyakṛtalavanaṃ toyamātrābhilakṣyaṃ
10.81.52 lakṣaṃ chitvā śapharamavṛthā lakṣaṇāṃ madrakanyām |
10.81.53 aṣṭāvevaṃ tava samabhavan vallabhāstatra madhye
10.81.54 śuśrotha tvaṃ surapatigirā bhaumaduśceṣṭitāni ||
10.81.61 smṛtāyātaṃ pakṣipravaramadhirūḍhastvamagamo
10.81.62 vahannaṅke bhāmāmupavanamivārātinagaram |
10.81.63 vibhindan durgāṇi truṭitapṛtanāśonitarasaiḥ
10.81.64 puraṃ tāvatprāgjyotiṣamakuruthāḥ śoṇitapūram ||
10.81.71 murastvāṃ pañcāsyo jaladhivanamadhyādudapatat
10.81.72 sa cakre cakreṇa pradalitaśirā maṅkṣu bhavatā |
10.81.73 catudantairdantāvalapatibhirindhānasamaraṃ
10.81.74 rathāṅgenacchitvā narakamakarostīrṇarakam ||
10.81.81 stuto bhūmyā rājyaṃ sapadi bhagadatte'sya tanaye
10.81.82 gajaṃ caikaṃ dattvā prajighāyitha nāgānnijapurīm |
10.81.83 khalenābaddhānāṃ svagatamanasāṃ ṣoḍaśa punaḥ
10.81.84 sahasrāṇi strīṇāmapi ca dhanarāśiṃ ca vipulam ||
10.81.91 bhaumāpāhṛtakuṇḍalaṃ tadaditerdātuṃ prayāto divaṃ
10.81.92 śakrādyairmahitaḥ samaṃ dayitayā dyustrīṣu dattahviyā |
10.81.93 hṛtvā kalpataruṃ ruṣābhipatitaṃ jitvendramabhyāgamas
10.81.94 tattu śrīmadadoṣa īdṛśa iti vyākhyātumevākṛthāḥ ||
10.81.101 kalpadruṃ satyabhāmābhavanabhuvi sṛjan dvyaṣṭasāhasrayoṣāḥ
10.81.102 svīkṛtya pratyagāraṃ vihitabahuvapurlālayan kelibhedaiḥ |
10.81.103 āścaryānnāradālokitavividhagatistatra tatrāpi gehe
10.81.104 bhūyaḥ sarvāsu kurvan daśa daśa tanayān pāhi vātālayeśa! ||
10.82.11 pradyumno raukmiṇeyaḥ sa khalu tava kalā śambareṇāhṛtastaṃ
10.82.12 hatvā ratyā sahāpto nijapūramaharadrukmikanyāṃ ca dhanyām |
10.82.13 tatputro'thāniruddho guṇanidhiravahadrocanāṃ rukmipautrīṃ
10.82.14 tatrodvāhe gatastvaṃ nyavadhi musalinā rukmyapi dyūtavairāt ||
10.82.21 bāṇasya sā balisutasya sahasrabāhor
10.82.22 māheśvarasya mahitā duhitā kiloṣā |
10.82.23 tvatpautramenamaniruddhamadṛṣṭapūrvaṃ
10.82.24 svapne'nubhūya bhagavan! virahāturābhūt ||
10.82.31 yoginyatīva kuśalā khalu citralekhā
10.82.32 tasyāḥ sakhī vilikhatī taruṇānaśeṣān |
10.82.33 tatrāniruddhamuṣyā viditaṃ niśāyā
10.82.34 māneṣṭa yogabalato bhavato niketāt ||
10.82.41 kanyāpure dayitayā sukhamāramantaṃ
10.82.42 cainaṃ kathañcana babandhuṣi śarvabandhau |
10.82.43 śrīnāradoktatadudantadurantaroṣais
10.82.44 tvaṃ tasya śoṇitapuraṃ yadubhirnyarundhāḥ ||
10.82.51 purīpālaḥ śailapriyaduhitṛnātho'sya bhagavān
10.82.52 samaṃ bhūtavrātairyadubalamaśaṅkaṃ nirurudhe |
10.82.53 mahāprāṇo bāṇo jaṭiti yuyudhānena yuyudhe
10.82.54 guhaḥ pradyumnena tvamapi purahantrā jaghaṭiṣe ||
10.82.61 niruddhāśeṣāstre mumuhuṣi tavāstreṇa giriśe
10.82.62 drutā bhūtā bhītāḥ pramathakulavīrāḥ pramathitāḥ |
10.82.63 parāskandatskandaḥ kusumaśarabāṇaiśca sacivaḥ
10.82.64 sa kumbhāṇḍo bhāṇḍaṃ navamiva balenāśu bibhide ||
10.82.71 cāpānāṃ pañcaśatyā prasabhamupagate chinnacāpe'tha bāṇe
10.82.72 vyarthe yāte sameto jvarapatiraśanairajvari tvajjvareṇa |
10.82.73 jñānī stutvātha dattvā tava caritajuṣāṃ vijvaraṃ sa jvaro'gāt
10.82.74 prāyo'ntarjñānavanto'pi ca bahutamasā raudraceṣṭā hi raudrāḥ ||
10.82.81 bāṇaṃ nānāyudhograṃ punarabhipatitaṃ durpadoṣādvitanvan
10.82.82 nirlūnāśeṣadoṣaṃ sapadi bubudhuṣā śaṅkareṇopagītaḥ |
10.82.83 tadvācā śiṣṭabāhudvitayamubhayato nirbhayaṃ tatpriyaṃ taṃ
10.82.84 muktvā taddattamāno nijapuramagamaḥ sāniruddhaḥ sahoṣaḥ ||
10.82.91 muhustāvacchakraṃ varuṇamajayo nandaharaṇe
10.82.92 yamaṃ bālānītau davadahanapāne'nilasakham |
10.82.93 vidhiṃ vatsasteye giriśāmiha bāṇasya samare
10.82.94 vibho! viśvotkarṣī tadayamavatāro jayati te ||
10.82.101 dvijaruṣā kṛkalāsavapurdharaṃ nṛganṛpaṃ tridivālayamāpayan |
10.82.102 nijajane dvijabhaktimanuttamāmupadiśan pavaneśvara! pāhi mām ||
10.83.11 rāme'thagokulagate pramadāprasakte
10.83.12 hūtānupetayamunādamane madāndhe |
10.83.13 svairaṃ samāramati sevakavādamūḍho
10.83.14 dūtaṃ nyayuṅkta tava pauṇḍrakavāsudevaḥ ||
10.83.21 nārāyaṇo'hamavatīrṇa ihāsmi bhūmau
10.83.22 dhatse kila tvamapi māmakalakṣaṇāni |
10.83.23 utsṛjya tāni śaraṇaṃ vraja māmiti tvāṃ
10.83.24 dūto jagāda sakalairhasitaḥ sabhāyām ||
10.83.31 dūte'tha yātavati yādavasainikastvaṃ
10.83.32 yāto dadarśitha vapuḥ kila pauṇḍrakīyam |
10.83.33 tāpena vakṣasi kṛtāṅkamanalpamūlya
10.83.34 śrīkaustubhaṃ makarakuṇḍalapītacelam ||
10.83.41 kālāyasaṃ nijasudarśanamasyato'sya
10.83.42 kālānalotkarakireṇa sudarśanena |
10.83.43 śīrṣaṃ cakartitha mamarditha cāsya senāṃ
10.83.44 tanmitrakāśipaśiro'pi cakartha kāśyām ||
10.83.51 jāḍyena bālakagirāpi kilāhameva
10.83.52 śrīvāsudeva iti rūḍhamatiściraṃ saḥ |
10.83.53 sāyujyameva bhavadaikyadhiyā gato'bhūt
10.83.54 ko nāma kasya sukṛtaṃ kathamityaveyāt ||
10.83.61 kāśīśvarasya tanayo'tha sudakṣiṇākhyaḥ
10.83.62 śarvaṃ prapūjya bhavate vihitābhicāraḥ |
10.83.63 kṛtyānalaṃ kamapi bāṇaraṇātibhītair
10.83.64 bhūtaiḥ kathañcana vṛtaiḥ samamabhyamuñcat ||
10.83.71 tālapramāṇacaraṇāmakhilaṃ dahantīṃ
10.83.72 kṛtyāṃ vilokya cakitaiḥ kathito'pi pauraiḥ |
10.83.73 dyūtotsave kamapi no calito vibho! tvaṃ
10.83.74 pārśvasthamāśu visasarjitha kālacakram ||
10.83.81 abhyāpatatyamitadhāmni bhavanmahāstre
10.83.82 hā heti vidrutavatī khalu ghorakṛtyā |
10.83.83 roṣātsudakṣiṇamadakṣiṇaceṣṭitaṃ taṃ
10.83.84 puploṣa cakramapi kāśipurāmadhākṣīt ||
10.83.91 sa khalu vivido rakṣoghāte kṛtopakṛtiḥ purā
10.83.92 tava tu kalayā mṛtyuṃ prāptuṃ tadā khalatāṃ gataḥ |
10.83.93 narakasacivo halinā yudhyannaddhā papāta talāhataḥ ||
10.83.101 sāmbaṃ kauravyaputrīharaṇaniyamitaṃ sāntvanārthī kurūṇāṃ
10.83.102 yātastadvākyaroṣoddhṛtakarinagaro mocayāmāsa rāmaḥ |
10.83.103 te ghātyāḥ pāṇḍaveyairiti yadupṛtanāṃ nāmucastvaṃ tadānīṃ
10.83.104 taṃ tvāṃ durbodhalīlaṃ pavanapurapate! tāpaśāntyai niṣeve ||
10.84.11 kvacidatha tapanoparāgakāle puri nidadhatkṛtavarmakāmasūnū |
10.84.12 yadukulamahilāvṛtaḥ sutīrthaṃ samupagato'si samantapañcakākhyam ||
10.84.21 bahutarajanatāhitāya tatra tvamapi punan vinimajjya tīrthatoye |
10.84.22 dvijagaṇaparimuktavittarāśiḥ samamilathāḥ kurupāṇḍavādimitraiḥ ||
10.84.31 tava khalu dayitājanaiḥ sametā drupadasutā tvayi gāḍhabhaktibhārā |
10.84.32 taduditabhavadāhṛtiprakārairatimumude samamanyabhāminībhiḥ ||
10.84.41 tadanu ca bhagavan! nirīkṣya gopānatikutukādupagamya mānayitvā |
10.84.42 cirataravirahāturāṅgarekhāḥ paśupavadhūḥ sarasaṃ tvamanvayāsīḥ ||
10.84.51 sapadi ca bhavadīkṣaṇotsavena pramuṣitamānahṛdāṃ nitambinīnām |
10.84.52 atirasaparimuktakañculīke paricayahṛdyatare kuce nyalaiṣīḥ ||
10.84.61 ripujanakalahaiḥ punaḥ punarme samupagatairiyatī vilambanābhūt |
10.84.62 iti kṛtaparirambhaṇe tvayi drāgativivaśā khalu rādhikā nililye ||
10.84.71 apagatavirahavyathāstadā tā rahasi vidhāya dadātha tattvabodham |
10.84.72 paramasukhacidātmako'hamātmetyudayatu vaḥ sphuṭameva cetasīti ||
10.84.81 sukharasaparimiśrito viyogaḥ kimapi purābhavaduddhavopadeśaiḥ |
10.84.82 samabhavadamutaḥ paraṃ tu tāsāṃ paramasukaikyamayī bhavadvicintā ||
10.84.91 munivaranivahaistavātha pitrā duritaśamāya śubhāni pṛcchyamānaiḥ |
10.84.92 tvayi sati kimidaṃ śubhāntarairityuruhasitairapi yājitastadāsau ||
10.84.101 sumahati yajane vitāyamāne pramuditamitrajane sahaiva gopāḥ |
10.84.102 yadujanamahitāstrimāsamātraṃ bhavadanuṣaṅgarasaṃ pureva bhejuḥ ||
10.84.111 vyapagamasamaye sametya rādhāṃ dṛḍhamupagūhya nirīkṣya vītakhedām |
10.84.112 pramuditahṛdayaḥ puraṃ prayātaḥ pavanapureśvara! pāhi māṃ gadebhyaḥ ||
10.85.11 tato magadhabhūmṛtā ciranirodhasaṃkleśitaṃ
10.85.12 śatāṣṭakayutāyutadvitayamīśa! bhūmībhṛtām |
10.85.13 anāthaśaraṇāya te kamapi pūruṣaṃ prāhiṇo
10.85.14 dayācata sa māgadhakṣapaṇameva kiṃ bhūyasā ||
10.85.21 yiyāsurabhimāgadhaṃ tadanu nāradodīritād
10.85.22 yudhiṣṭhiramakhodyamādubhayakāryaparyākulaḥ |
10.85.23 viruddhajayino'dhvarādubhayasiddhirityuddhave
10.85.24 śaśaṃsuṣi nijaiḥ samaṃ puramiyetha yaudhiṣṭhirīm ||
10.85.31 aśeṣadayitāyute tvayi samāgate dharmajo
10.85.32 vijitya sahajairmahīṃ bhavadapāṅgasaṃvardhitaiḥ |
10.85.33 śriyaṃ nirupamāṃ vahannahaha bhaktadāsāyitaṃ
10.85.34 bhavantamayi! māgadhe prahitavān sabhīmārjunam ||
10.85.41 girivrajapuraṃ gatāstadanu deva! yūyaṃ trayo
10.85.42 yayāca samarotsavaṃ dvijamiṣeṇa taṃ māgadham |
10.85.43 apūrṇasukṛtaṃ tvamuṃ pavanajena saṃgrāmayan
10.85.44 nirīkṣya saha jiṣṇunā tvamapi rājayudhvā sthitaḥ ||
10.85.51 aśāntasamaroddhataṃ viṭapapāṭanāsaṃjñayā
10.85.52 nipātya jarasaḥ sutaṃ pavanajena niṣpāṭitam |
10.85.53 vimucya nṛpatīnmudā samanugṛhya bhaktiṃ parāṃ
10.85.54 dideśitha gataspṛhānapi ca dharmaguptyai bhuvaḥ ||
10.85.61 pracakruṣi yudhiṣṭhire tadanu rājasūyādhvaraṃ
10.85.62 prasannabhṛtakībhavatsakalarājakavyākulam |
10.85.63 tvamapyayi jagatpate! dvijapadāvanejādikaṃ
10.85.64 cakartha kimu kathyate nṛpavarasya bhāgyonnatiḥ ||
10.85.71 tataḥ savanakarmaṇi pravaramagryapūjāvidhiṃ
10.85.72 vicārya sahadevavāganugataḥ sa dharmātmajaḥ |
10.85.73 vyadhatta bhavate mudā sadasi viśvabhūtātmane
10.85.74 tadā sasuramānuṣaṃ bhavanameva tṛptiḥ dadhau ||
10.85.81 tataḥ sapadi cedipo muninṛpeṣu tiṣṭhatsvaho
10.85.82 sabhājayati ko jaḍaḥ paśupadurdurūṭaṃ vaṭum |
10.85.83 iti tvayi sa durvacovitatimudvamannāsanā
10.85.84 dudāpatadudāyudhaḥ samapatannamuṃ pāṇḍavāḥ ||
10.85.91 nivārya nijapakṣagānabhimukhasya vidveṣiṇas
10.85.92 tvameva jahiṣe śiro danujadāriṇā svāriṇā |
10.85.93 janustritayalabdhayā satatacintayā śuddhadhīs
10.85.94 tvayā sa paramekatāmadhṛta yogināṃ durlabhām ||
10.85.101 tataḥ sumāhito tvayā kratuvare nirūḍhe jano
10.85.102 yayau jayati dharmajo jayati kṛṣṇa ityālapan |
10.85.103 khalaḥ sa tu suyodhano dhutamanāḥ sapatnaśriyā
10.85.104 mayārpitasabhāmukhe sthalajalabhramādabhramīt ||
10.85.111 tadā hasitamutthitaṃ drupadandanābhīmayo
10.85.112 rapāṅgakalayā vibho! kimapi tāvadujjṛmbhayan |
10.85.113 dharābharanirākṛtau sapadi nāma bījaṃ vapan
10.85.114 janārdana! marutpurīnilaya! pāhi māmāmayāt ||
