10.86.11 sālvo bhaiṣmīvivāhe yadubalavijitaścandracūḍādvimānaṃ
10.86.12 vindan saubhaṃ sa māyī tvayi vasati kurūṃstvatpurīmabhyabhāṅkṣīt |
10.86.13 pradyumnastaṃ nirundhannakhilayadubhaṭairnyagrahīdugravīryaṃ
10.86.14 tasyāmātyaṃ dyumantaṃ vyajani ca samaraḥ saptaviṃśatyahāntam ||
10.86.21 tāvattvaṃ rāmaśālī tvaritamupagataḥ khaṇḍitaprāyasainyaṃ
10.86.22 saubheśaṃ taṃ nyarundhāḥ sa ca kila gadayā śārṅgamabhraṃśayatte |
10.86.23 māyātātaṃ vyahiṃsīdapi tava puratastattvayāpi kṣaṇārdhaṃ
10.86.24 nājñāyītyāhureke tadidamavamataṃ vyāsa eva nyaṣedhīt ||
10.86.31 kṣiptvā saubhaṃ gadācūrṇitamudakanidhau maṅkṣu sālve'pi cakre
10.86.32 ṇotkṛtte dantavaktraḥ prasabhamabhipatannabhyamuñcadgadāṃ te |
10.86.33 kaumodakyā hato'sāvapi sukṛtanidhiścaidyavatprāpadaikyaṃ
10.86.34 sarveṣāmeṣa pūrvaṃ tvayi dhṛtamanasāṃ mokṣaṇārtho'vatāraḥ ||
10.86.41 tvayyāyāte'tha jāte kila kurusadasi dyūtake saṃyatāyāḥ
10.86.42 krandantyā yājñasenyāḥ sakaruṇamakṛthāścelamālāmanantām |
10.86.43 annāntaprāptaśarvāṃśajamunicakitadraupadīcintito'tha
10.86.44 prāptaḥ śākānnamaśnanmunigaṇamakṛthāstṛptimantaṃ vanānte ||
10.86.51 yuddhodyoge'tha mantre milati sati vṛtaḥ phalgunena tvamekaḥ
10.86.52 kauravye dattasainyaḥ karipuramagamo dūtyakṛtpāṇḍavārtham |
10.86.53 mīṣmadroṇādimānye tava khalu vacane dhikkṛte kauraveṇa
10.86.54 vyāvṛṇvan viśvarūpaṃ munisadasi purīṃ kṣobhayitvāgato'bhūḥ ||
10.86.61 jiṣṇostvaṃ kṛṣṇa! sūtaḥ khalu samaramukhe bandhughāte dayāluṃ
10.86.62 khinnaṃ taṃ vīkṣya vīraṃ kimidamayi sakhe! nitya eko'yamātmā |
10.86.63 ko vadhyaḥ ko'tra hantā tadiha vadhabhiyaṃ projjhya mayyarpitātmā
10.86.64 dharmyaṃ yuddhaṃ careti prakṛtimanayathā darśayan viśvarūpam ||
10.86.71 bhaktottaṃse'tha bhīṣme tava dharaṇibharakṣepakṛtyaikasakte
10.86.72 nityaṃ nityaṃ vibhindatyavanibhṛdayutaṃ prāptasāde ca pārthe |
10.86.73 niśśastratvapratijñāṃ vijahadarivaraṃ dhārayan krodhaśālī
10.86.74 vādhāvan prāñjaliṃ taṃ nataśirasamatho vīkṣya modādapāgāḥ ||
10.86.81 yuddhe droṇasya hastisthiraraṇabhagadatteritaṃ vaiṣṇavāstraṃ
10.86.82 vakṣasyādhatta cakrasthagitaravimahāḥ prārdayan sindhurājam |
10.86.83 nāgāstre karṇamukte kṣitimavanamayan kevalaṃ kṛttamauliṃ
10.86.84 tatre tatrāpi pārthaṃ kimiva na hi bhavān pāṇḍavānāmakārṣīt ||
10.86.91 yuddhādau tīrthagāmi sa khalu haladharo naimiśakṣetramṛccha
10.86.92 nnapratyutthāyisūtakṣayakṛdatha sutaṃ tatpade kalpayitvā |
10.86.93 yajñaghnaṃ balvalaṃ parvaṇi paridalayaṃ snātatīrtho raṇānte
10.86.94 samprāpto bhīmaduryodhanaraṇamaśamaṃ vīkṣya yātaḥ purīṃ te ||
10.86.101 saṃsuptadraupadeyakṣapaṇahatadhiyaṃ drauṇimetya tvaduktyā
10.86.102 tanmuktaṃ brāhmamastraṃ samahṛta vijayo mauliratnaṃ ca jahe |
10.86.103 ucchittyai pāṇḍavānāṃ punarapi ca viśatyuttarāgarbhamastre
10.86.104 rakṣannaṅguṣṭhamātraḥ kila jaṭharamagāścakrapāṇirvibho! tvam ||
10.86.111 dharmaughaṃ dharmasūnorabhidadhadakhilaṃ chandamṛtyuḥ sa bhīṣmas
10.86.112 tvāṃ paśyan bhaktibhūmnaiva hi sapadi yayau niṣkalabrahmabhūyam |
10.86.113 saṃyājyāthāśvamedhaistribhiratimahitairdharmajaṃ pūrṇakāmaṃ
10.86.114 samprāpto dvārakāṃ tvaṃ pavanapurapate! pāhi māṃ sarvarogāt ||
10.87.11 kucelanāmā bhavataḥ satīrthyatāṃ gataḥ sa sāndīpanimandire dvijaḥ |
10.87.12 tvadekarāgeṇa dhanādiniḥspṛho dināni ninye praśamī gṛhāśramī ||
10.87.21 samānaśīlāpi tadāyavallabhā tathaiva no cittahayaṃ sameyusī |
10.87.22 kadācidūce bata vṛttilabdhaye ramāpatiḥ kiṃ na sakhā niṣevyate ||
10.87.31 itīrito'yaṃ priyayā kṣudhārtayā jugupsamāno'pi dhane madāvahe |
10.87.32 tadā tvadālokanakautukādyayau vahan paṭānte pṛthukānupāyanam ||
10.87.41 gato'yamāścaryamayīṃ bhavatpūrīṃ gṛheṣu śaibyābhavanaṃ sameyivān |
10.87.42 praviśya vaikuṇṭhamivāpa nirvṛtiṃ tavātisambhāvanayā tu kiṃ punaḥ ||
10.87.51 prapūjitaṃ taṃ priyayā ca vījitaṃ kare gṛhītvākathayaḥ purā kṛtam |
10.87.52 yadindhanārthaṃ gurudāracoditairapartuvarṣaṃ tadamarṣi kānane ||
10.87.61 trapājuṣo'smātpṛthukaṃ balādatha pragṛhya muṣṭau sakṛdāśite tvayā |
10.87.62 kṛtaṃ kṛtaṃ nanviyateti sambhramādramā kilopetya karaṃ rurodha te ||
10.87.71 bhakteṣu bhaktena sa mānitastvayā purīṃ vasannekaniśāṃ mahāsukham |
10.87.72 batāparedyurdraviṇaṃ vinā yayau vicitrarūpastava khalvanugrahaḥ ||
10.87.81 yadi hyayāciṣyamadāsyadacyuto vadāmi bhāryāṃ kimiti vrajannasau |
10.87.82 tvaduktilīlāsmitamagnadhīḥ punaḥ kramādapaśyanmaṇidīpramālayam ||
10.87.91 kiṃ mārgavibhraṃśa iti bhraman kṣaṇaṃ gṛhaṃ praviṣṭaḥ sa dadarśa vallabhām |
10.87.92 sakhīparītāṃ maṇihemabhūṣitāṃ bubodha ca tvatkaruṇāṃ mahādbhutām ||
10.87.101 sa ratnaśālāsu vasannapi svayaṃ samunnamadbhaktibharo'mṛtaṃ yayau |
10.87.102 tvamevamāpūritabhaktavāñchito marutpurādhīśa! harasva me gadān ||
10.88.11 prāgevācāryaputrāhṛtiniśamanayā svīyaṣaṭsūnuvīkṣāṃ
10.88.12 kāṅkṣantyā māturukatyā sutalabhuvi baliṃ prāpya tenārcitastvam |
10.88.13 dhātuḥ śāpāddhiraṇyānvitakaśipubhavān śaurijān kaṃsabhagnā
10.88.14 nānīyainān pradarśya svapadamanayathāḥ pūrvaputrānmarīceḥ ||
10.88.21 śrutadeva iti śrutaṃ dvijendraṃ bahulāśvaṃ nṛpatiṃ ca bhaktipūrṇam |
10.88.22 yugapattvamanugrahītukāmo mithilāṃ prāpitha tāpasaiḥ sametaḥ ||
10.88.31 gacchan dvimūrtirubhayoryugapanniketa
10.88.32 mekena bhūrivibhavairvihitopacāraḥ |
10.88.33 anyena taddinabhṛtaiśca phalaudanādyais
10.88.34 tulyaṃ praseditha dadātha ca muktimābhyām ||
10.88.41 bhūyo'tha dvāravatyāṃ dvijatanayamṛtiṃ tatpralāpānapi tvaṃ
10.88.42 ko vā daivaṃ nirundhyāditi kila kathayan viśvavoḍhāpyasoḍhāḥ |
10.88.43 jiṣṇorgarvaṃ vinetuṃ tvayi manujadhiyā kuṇṭhitāṃ cāsya buddhiṃ
10.88.44 tattvārūḍhāṃ vidhātuṃ paramatamapadaprekṣaṇeneti manye ||
10.88.51 naṣṭā aṣṭāsya putrāḥ punarapi tava tūpekṣayā kaṣṭavādaḥ
10.88.52 spaṣṭo jāto janānāmatha tadavasare dvārakāmāra pārthaḥ |
10.88.53 maitryā tatroṣito'sau navamasutabhṛtau vipravaryaprarodaṃ
10.88.54 śrutvā cakre pratijñāmanupahṛtasutaḥ sannivekṣye kṛśānum ||
10.88.61 mānī sa tvāmapṛṣṭvā dvijanilayagato bāṇajālairmahāstrai
10.88.62 rundhānaḥ sūtigehaṃ punarapi sahasā dṛṣṭanaṣṭe kumāre |
10.88.63 yāmyāmaindrīṃtathāyāḥ suravaranagarīrvidyayāsādya sadyo
10.88.64 moghodyogaḥ patiṣyan hutabhuji bhavatā sasmitaṃ vārito'bhūt ||
10.88.71 sārdhaṃ tena pratīcīṃ diśamatijavinā syandanenābhiyāto
10.88.72 lokālokaṃ vyatītastimirabharamatho cakradhāmnā nirundhan |
10.88.73 cakrāṃśukliṣṭadṛṣṭiṃ sthitamatha vijayaṃ paśya paśyeti vārāṃ
10.88.74 pāre tvaṃ prādadaśaḥ kimapi hi tamasāṃ dūradūraṃ padaṃ te ||
10.88.81 tatrāsīnaṃ bhujaṅgādhipaśayanatale divyabhūṣāyudhādyai
10.88.82 rāvītaṃ pītacelaṃ pratinavajaladaśyāmalaṃ śrīmadaṅgam |
10.88.83 mūrtīnāmīśitāraṃ paramiha tisṛṇāmekamarthaṃ śrutīnāṃ
10.88.84 tvāmeva tvaṃ parātman! priyasakhasahito nemitha kṣemarūpam ||
10.88.91 yuvāṃ māmevadvāvadhikavivṛtāntarhitatayā
10.88.92 vibhinnau sundraṣṭuṃ svayamahamahārṣaṃ dvijasutān |
10.88.93 nayetaṃ drāgenāniti khalu vitīrṇān punaramūn
10.88.94 dvijāyādāyādāḥ praṇutamahimā pāṇḍujanuṣā ||
10.88.101 evaṃ nānāvihārairjagadabhiramayan vṛṣṇivaṃśaṃ prapuṣṇa
10.88.102 nnījāno yajñabhaidairatulavihṛtibhiḥ prīṇayanneṇanetrāḥ |
10.88.103 bhūbhārakṣepadambhātpadakamalajuṣāṃ mokṣaṇāyāvatīrṇaḥ
10.88.104 pūrṇaṃ brahmaiva sākṣādyaduṣu manujatārūṣitastvaṃ vyalāsīḥ ||
10.88.111 prāyeṇa dvāravatyāmavṛtadayi tadī nāradastvadrasārdras
10.88.112 tasmāllebhe kadācitkhalu sukṛtanidhistvatpitā tattvabodham |
10.88.113 bhaktānāmagrayāyī sa ca khalu matimānuddhavastvatta eva
10.88.114 prāpto vijñānasāraṃ sa kila janahitāyādhunāste vadaryām ||
10.88.121 so'yaṃ kṛṣṇāvatāro jayati tava vibho! yatra sauhārdabhīti
10.88.122 snehadveṣānurāgaprabhṛtibhiratulairaśramairyogabhedaiḥ |
10.88.123 ārtiṃ tīrvā samastāmamṛtapadamaguḥ sarvataḥ sarvalokāḥ
10.88.124 sa tvaṃ viśvārtiśāntyai pavanapurapate! bhaktipūrtyai ca bhūyāḥ ||
10.89.11 ramājāne! jāne yadiha tava bhakteṣu vibhavo
10.89.12 na sampadyaḥ sadyastadiha madakṛttvādaśaminām |
10.89.13 praśāntiṃ kṛtvaiva pradiśasi tataḥ kāmamakhilaṃ
10.89.14 praśānteṣu kṣipraṃ na khalu bhavadīye cyutikathā ||
10.89.21 sadyaḥprasādaruṣitān vidhiśaṅkarādīn
10.89.22 kacidvibho! nijaguṇānuguṇaṃ bhajantaḥ |
10.89.23 bhraṣṭā bhavanti bata kaṣṭamadīrghadṛṣṭyā
10.89.24 spaṣṭaṃ vṛkāsara udāharaṇaṃ kilāsmin ||
10.89.31 śakunijaḥ sa hi nāradamekadā tvaritatoṣṣamapṛcchadadhīśvaram |
10.89.32 sa ca dideśa girīśamupāsituṃ na tu bhavantamabandhumasādhuṣu ||
10.89.41 tapastaptvghoraṃ sa khalu kupitaḥ saptamadine
10.89.42 śiraśchittvā sadyaḥ puraharamupasthāpya purataḥ |
10.89.43 atikṣudraṃ raudraṃ śirasi karadānena nidhanaṃ
10.89.44 jagannāthādvavre bhavati vimukhānāṃ kva śubhadhūḥ ||
10.89.51 moktāraṃ bandhamukto hariṇapatiriva prādravatso'tha rudraṃ
10.89.52 daityādbhītyā sma devo diśi diśi valate pṛṣṭhato dattadṛṣṭiḥ |
10.89.53 tūṣṇīke sarvaloke tava padamadhirokṣyantamudvīkṣya śarvaṃ
10.89.54 dūrādevāgratastvaṃ paṭuvaṭuvapuṣā tasthiṣe dānavāya ||
10.89.61 bhadraṃ te śākuneya! bhramasi kimadhunā tvaṃ piśācasya vācā
10.89.62 sandehaścenmaduktau tava kimu na karoṣyaṅgulīmaṅga! maulau |
10.89.63 itthaṃ tvadvākyamūḍhaḥ śirasi kṛtakaraḥ so'patacchinnapātaṃ
10.89.64 bhraṃśo hyevaṃ paropāsiturapi ca gatiḥ śūlino'pi tvameva ||
10.89.71 bhṛguṃ kila sarasvatīnikaṭavāsinastāpasā
10.89.72 strimurtiṣu samādiśannadhikasattvatāṃ veditum |
10.89.73 ayaṃ punaranādarāduditaruddharoṣe vidhau
10.89.74 hare'pi ca jihiṃsiṣau girijayā dhṛte tvāmagāt ||
10.89.81 suptaṃ ramāṅkabhuvi paṅkajalocanaṃ tvāṃ
10.89.82 vipre vinighnati padena mudotthitastvam |
10.89.83 sarvaṃ kṣamasva munivarya! bhavetsadā me
10.89.84 tvatpādacihnamiha bhūṣaṇamityavādīḥ ||
10.89.91 niścitya te ca sudṛḍhaṃ tvayi baddhabhāvāḥ
10.89.92 sārasvatā munivarā dadhire vimokṣam |
10.89.93 tvāmevamacyuta! punaścyutidoṣahīnaṃ
10.89.94 sattvoccayaikatanumeva vayaṃ bhajāmaḥ ||
10.89.101 jagatsṛṣṭyādau tvāṃ nigamanivahairvandibhiriva
10.89.102 stutaṃ viṣṇo! saccitparamarasanirdvaitavapuṣam |
10.89.103 parātmānaṃ bhūman! paśupavinatābhāgyanivahaṃ
10.89.104 parītapaśrāntyai pavanapuravāsin! paribhaje ||
10.90.11 vṛkabhṛgusunimohinyambarīṣādivṛtte
10.90.12 ṣvayi tava hi mahattvaṃ sarvaśarvādijaitram |
10.90.13 sthitamiha paramātman! niṣkalārvāgabhinnaṃ
10.90.14 kimapi yadavabhātaṃ taddhi rūpaṃ tavaiva ||
10.90.21 mūrtitrayeśvarasadāśivapañcakaṃ yat
10.90.22 prāhuḥ parātmavapureva sadāśivo'smin |
10.90.23 tatreśvarastu sa vikuṇṭhapadastvameva
10.90.24 tritvaṃ punarbhajasi satyapade tribhāge ||
10.90.31 tatrāpi sāttvikatanuṃ tava viṣṇumāhur
10.90.32 dhātā tu sattvaviralo rajasaiva pūrṇaḥ |
10.90.33 satttvotkaṭatvamapi cāsti tamovikāra
10.90.34 ceṣṭādikaṃ ca tava śaṅkaranāmni mūrtau ||
10.90.41 taṃ ca trimūrtyatigataṃ purapūruṣaṃ tvāṃ
10.90.42 śarvātmanāpi khalu sarvamayatvahetoḥ |
10.90.43 śaṃsantyupāsanaavidhau tadapi svatastu
10.90.44 tvadrūpamityatidṛḍhaṃ bahu naḥ pramāṇam ||
10.90.51 śrīśaṅkaro'pi bhagavān sakaleṣu tāvat
10.90.52 tvāmeva mānayati yo na hi pakṣapātī |
10.90.53 tvanniṣṭhameva sa hi nāmasahasrakādi
10.90.54 vyākhyadbhavatstutiparaśca gatiṃ gato'nte ||
10.90.61 mūrtitrayātigamuvāca ca mantraśāstras
10.90.62 yādau kalāyasuṣamaṃ sakaleśvaraṃ tvām |
10.90.63 dhyānaṃ ca niṣkalamasau praṇave khalūktvā
10.90.64 tvāmeva tatra sakalaṃ nijagāda nānyam ||
10.90.71 samastasāre ca purāṇasaṃgrahe visaṃśayaṃ tvanmahimaiva varṇyate |
10.90.72 trimūrtiyuksatyapadatribhāgataḥ paraṃ padaṃ te kathitaṃ na śūlinaḥ ||
10.90.81 yadbrāhmakalpa iha bhāgavatadvitīya
10.90.82 skandhoditaṃ vapuranāvṛtamīśa! dhātre |
10.90.83 tasyaiva nāma hariśarvamukhaṃ jagāda
10.90.84 śrīmādhavaṃ śivaparo'pi purāṇasāre ||
10.90.91 ye svaprakṛtyanuguṇā giriśaṃ bhajante
10.90.92 teṣāṃ phalaṃ hi dṛḍhayaiva tadīyabhaktyā |
10.90.93 vyāso hi tena kṛtavānadhikārihetoḥ
10.90.94 skāndādikeṣu tava hānivaco'rthavādaiḥ ||
10.90.101 bhūtārthakīrtiranuvādaviruddhavādau
10.90.102 tredhārthavādagatayaḥ khalu rocanārthāḥ |
10.90.103 skāndādikeṣu bahavo'tra viruddhavādās
10.90.104 tvattāmasatvaparibhūtyupaśikṣaṇādyāḥ ||
10.90.111 yatkiñcidapyaviduṣāpi vibho! mayoktaṃ
10.90.112 tanmantraśāstravacanādyabhidṛṣṭameva |
10.90.113 vyāsoktisāramayabhāgavatopagīta!
10.90.114 kleśān vidhūya kuru bhaktibharaṃ parātman! |||

11.91.11 śrīkṛṣṇa! tvatpadopāsanamabhayatamaṃ baddhamithyārthadṛṣṭer
11.91.12 martyasyārtasya manye vyapasarati bhayaṃ yena sarvātmaiva |
11.91.13 yattāvattvatpraṇītāniha bhajanavidhīnāsthito mohamārge
11.91.14 dhāvannapyāvṛtākṣaḥ skhalati na kuhaciddevadevākhilātman! ||
11.91.21 bhūman! kāyena vācā muhurapi manasā tvadbalapreritātmā
11.91.22 yadyatkurve samastaṃ tadiha paratare tvayyasāvarpayāmi |
11.91.23 jātyāpīha śvapākastvayi nihitamanaḥ karmavāgindriyārtha
11.91.24 prāṇo viśvaṃ punīte na tu vimukhamanāstvatpadādvipravaryaḥ ||
11.91.31 bhītirnāma dvitīyādbhavati nanu manaḥkalpitaṃ ca dvitīyaṃ
11.91.32 tenaikyābhyāsaśīlo hṛdayamiha yathāśakti buddhyā nirundhyām |
11.91.33 māyāviddhe tu tasmin punarapi na tathā bhāti māyādhināthaṃ
11.91.34 tattvāṃ bhaktyā mahatyā satatamanubhajannīśa! bhītiṃ vijahyām ||
11.91.41 bhakterutpattivṛddhī tava caraṇajuṣaṃ saṅgamenaiva puṃsā
11.91.42 māsādye puṇyabhājāṃ śriya iva jagati śrīmatāṃ saṅgamena |
11.91.43 tatsaṅgo deva! bhūyānmama khalu satataṃ tanmukhādunmiṣaddhis
11.91.44 tvanmāhātmyaprakārairbhavati ca sudṛḍhā bhaktiruddhūtapāpā ||
11.91.51 śreyomārgeṣu bhaktāvadhikabahumatirjanmakarmāṇi bhūyo
11.91.52 gāyan kṣemāṇi nāmānyapi tadubhayataḥ pradrutaṃ pradrutātmā |
11.91.53 udyaddhāsaḥ kadācitkuhācidapi rudan kvāpi garjan pragāya
11.91.54 nnunmādīva pranṛtyannayi kuru karuṇāṃ lokabāhyaścareyam ||
11.91.61 bhūtānyetāni bhūtātmakamapi sakalaṃ pakṣimatsyānmṛgādīn
11.91.62 martyānmitrāṇi śatrūnapi yamitamatistvanmayānyānamāni |
11.91.63 tvatsevāyāṃ hi sidhyenmama tava kṛpayā bhaktidārḍhyaṃ virāgas
11.91.64 tvattattvasyāvabodho'pi ca bhuvanapate! yatnabhedaṃ vinaiva ||
11.91.71 no muhyan kṣuttṛḍādyairbhavasaraṇibhavaistvannilīnāśayatvā
11.91.72 ccintāsātatyaśālī nimiṣalavamapi tvatpadādaprakampaḥ |
11.91.73 iṣṭāniṣṭeṣu tuṣṭivyasanavirahito māyikatvāvabodhā
11.91.74 jjyotsnābhistvannakhendoradhikaśiśiritenātmanā sañcareyam ||
11.91.81 bhūteṣveṣu tvadaikyasmṛtisamadhigatau nādhikāro'dhunā cet
11.91.82 tvatprema tvatkamaitrī jaḍamatiṣu kṛpā dviṭsu bhūyādupekṣā |
11.91.83 ārcāyāṃ vā samarcākutukamurutaraśraddhayā vardhatāṃ me
11.91.84 tvatsaṃsevī tathāpi drutamupalabhate bhaktalokottamatvam ||
11.91.91 āvṛtya tvatsvarūpaṃ kṣitijalamarudādyātmanā vikṣipantī
11.91.92 jīvīn bhūyiṣṭhakarmāvalivivaśagatīn duḥkhajāle kṣipantī |
11.91.93 tvanmāyā mābhibhūnmāmayi bhuvanapate! kalpate tatpraśāntyai
11.91.94 tvatpāde bhaktirevetyavadadayi vibho! siddhayogī prabuddhaḥ ||
11.91.101 duḥkhānyālokya jantuṣvalamuditaviveko'hamācāryavaryā
11.91.102 llabdhvā tvadrūpatattvaṃ guṇacaritakathādyudbhaktibhūmā |
11.91.103 māyāmenāṃ taritvā paramasukhamaye tvatpade moditāhe
11.91.104 tasyāyaṃ pūrvaraṅgaḥ pavanapurapate! nāśayāśeṣarogān ||
11.92.11 vaidaiḥ sarvāṇi karmāṇyaphalaparatayā varṇitānīti buddhvā
11.92.12 tāni tvayyarpitānyeva hi samanucaran yāni naiṣkarmyamīśa! |
11.92.13 mā bhūdvedairniṣiddhe kuhacidapi manaḥkarmavācāḥ pravṛttir
11.92.14 durvarjaṃ cedavāptaṃ tadapi khalu bhavatyarpaye citprakāśe ||
11.92.21 yastvanyaḥ karmayomastava bhajanamayastatra cābhīṣṭamūrtiṃ
11.92.22 hṛdyāṃ sattvaikarūpāṃ dṛṣadi hṛdi mṛdi kvāpi vā bhāvayitvā |
11.92.23 puṣpairmandhairnivedyairapi ca viracitaiḥ śaktito bhaktipūtair
11.92.24 nityaṃ varyāṃ saparyāṃ vidadhadayi vibho! tvatprasādaṃ bhajeyam ||
11.92.31 strīśūdrāstvatkathādiśravaṇavirahitā āsatāṃ te dayārhās
11.92.32 tvatpādāsannayātān dvijakulajanuṣo hanta śocāmyaśāntān |
11.92.33 vṛttyarthaṃ te yajanto bahukathitamapi tvāmanākarṇayanto
11.92.34 dṛptā vidyābhijātyaiḥ kimu na vidadhate tādṛśaḥ mā kṛthā mām ||
11.92.41 papo'yaṃ kṛṣṇa! rāmetyabhilapati nijaṃ gūhituṃ diścāritraṃ
11.92.42 nirlajjasyāsya vācā bahutarakathanīyāni me vighnitāni |
11.92.43 bhrātā me vandhyaśīlo bhajati kila sadā viṣṇumitthaṃ budhāṃste
11.92.44 nindantyuccairhasanti tvayi nihitaratīṃstādṛśaṃ mā kṛthā mām ||
11.92.51 śvetacchāyaṃ kṛte tvāṃ munivaravapuṣaṃ prīṇayante tapobhi
11.92.52 stretāyāṃ sruksruvādyaṅkitamaruṇatanuṃ yajñarūpaṃ yajante |
11.92.53 sevante tantramārgairvilasadarigadaṃ dvāpare śyāmalāṅgaṃ
11.92.54 nīlaṃ saṅkīrtanādyairiha kalisamaye mānuṣāstvāṃ bhajante ||
11.92.61 so'yaṃ kāleyakālo jayati muraripo! yatra saṅkīrtanādyair
11.92.62 niryatnaireva mārgairakhilada! nacirāttvatprasādaṃ bhajante |
11.92.63 jātāstretākṛtādāvapi hi kila kalau sambhavaṃ kāmayante
11.92.64 daivāttatraiva jātān viṣayaviṣarasairmā vibho! vañcayāstmān ||
11.92.71 bhaktāstāvatkalau spurdramilabhuvi tato bhūriśastatra coccaiḥ
11.92.72 kāverīṃ tāmraparṇīman kila kṛtamālāṃ ca puṇyāṃ pratīcīm |
11.92.73 hā māmapyetadantarbhavamapi ca vibho! kiñcidañcidrasaṃ tva
11.92.74 yyāśāpāśairnibadhya bhramaya na magavan! pūraya tvanniṣevām ||
11.92.81 dṛṣṭvā dharmadruhaṃ taṃ kalimapakaruṇaṃ prāṅmahīkṣitparīkṣi
11.92.82 ddhantuṃ vyākṛṣṭakhaḍgo'pi na vinihatavān sāravedī muṇāṃśāt |
11.92.83 tvatsevādyāśu sidhyedasadiha na tathā tvatpare caiṣa bhīrur
11.92.84 yattu prāgeva rogādibhirapaharate tatra hā śikṣayainam ||
11.92.91 gaṅgā gītā ca gāyatryapi ca tulasikā gopikācandanaṃ tat
11.92.92 sālagrāmābhipūjā parapuruṣa! tathaikādaśī nāmavarṇāḥ |
11.92.93 etānyaṣṭāpyayatnānyayi kalisamaye tvatprasādapravṛddhyā
11.92.94 kṣipraṃ muktipradānītyabhidadhurṛṣayasteṣu māṃ sajjayethāḥ ||
11.92.101 devarṣīṇāṃ pitṇāmapi na punarṛṇī kiṅgaro vā sa bhūman!
11.92.102 yo'sau sarvātmanā tvāṃ śaraṇamupagataḥ sarvakṛtyāni hitvā |
11.92.103 tasyotpannaṃ vikarmāpyakhilamapanudasyeva cittasthitastvaṃ
11.92.104 tanme papotthatāpān pavanapurapate! rundi bhaktiṃ praṇīyāḥ ||
11.93.11 bandhusnehaṃ vijahyāṃ tava hi karuṇayā tvayyupāveśitātmā
11.93.12 sarvaṃ tvaktvā careyaṃ sakalamapi jagadvīkṣya māyāvilāsam |
11.93.13 nānātvādbhṛāntijanyātsati khalu guṇadoṣāvabodhe vidhirvā
11.93.14 vyāsedho vā kathaṃ tau tvayi nihitamatervītavaiṣamyabuddheḥ ||
11.93.21 kṣuttṛṣṇālopamātre satatakṛtadhiyo jantaghaḥ santyanantā
11.93.22 stebhyo vijñānavattvātpuruṣa iha varastajjanirdurlabhaiva |
11.93.23 tatrāpyātmātmanaḥ syātsuhṛdapi ca ripuryastvayi nyastacetā
11.93.24 stāpocchitterupāthaṃ smarati sa hi suhṛtsvātmavairī tato'nyaḥ ||
11.93.31 tvatkāruṇye pravṛtte ka iva na hi gururlokavṛtte'pi bhūman!
11.93.32 sarvākrāntāpi bhūmirna hi calati tataḥ satkṣamāṃ śikṣayeyam |
11.93.33 gṛhṇīyāmīśa! tattadviṣayaparicate'pyaprasaktiṃ samīrād
11.93.34 vyāptatvaṃ cātmano me gaganaguruvaśādbhātu nirlepatā ca ||
11.93.41 svacchaḥ syāṃ pāvano'haṃ madhura udakavadvahnivanmā sma gṛhṇāṃ
11.93.42 sarvānnīno'pi doṣaṃ taruṣu tamiva māṃ sarvabhūteṣvaveyām |
11.93.43 puṣṭirnaṣṭiḥ kalānāṃṃ śaśina iva tanornātmano'stīti vidyāṃ
11.93.44 toyādivyastamārtaṇḍavadapi ca tanuṣvekatāṃ tvatprasādāt ||
11.93.51 snehādvyādhāstaputrapraṇayamṛtakapotīyito mā sma bhūvaṃ
11.93.52 prāptaṃ prāśnan saheya kṣudhamapi śayuvatsindhuvatsyāmagādhaḥ |
11.93.53 mā paptaṃ yoṣidādau śikhini śalabhavadbhṛṅgavatsārabhāgī
11.93.54 bhūyāsaṃ kintu tadvaddhanacayanavaśānmāhamīśa! praneśam ||
11.93.61 mā badhyāsaṃ taruṇyā gaja iva vaśayā nārjayeyaṃ dhanaughaṃ
11.93.62 hartānyastaṃ hi mādhvīhara iva mṛgavanmā guhaṃ grāmyagītaiḥ |
11.93.63 nātyāsajjeya bhojye jhaṣa iva baḍiśe piṅgalāvannirāśaḥ
11.93.64 supyāṃ bhartavyayogātkurara iva vibho! sāmiṣo'nyairna hanyai ||
11.93.71 varteya tyaktamānaḥ sukhamatiśiśuvannissahāyaścareyaṃ
11.93.72 kanyāyā ekaśeṣo valaya iva vibho! varjitānyonyaghoṣaḥ |
11.93.73 tvaccitto nāvabudhyai paramiṣukṛdiva kṣmābhṛdāyānaghoṣaṃ
11.93.74 geheṣvanyapraṇīteṣvahiriva nivasānyundurormandireṣu ||
11.93.81 tvayyeva tvatkṛtaṃ tvaṃ kṣapayasi jagadityūrṇanābhātpratīyāṃ
11.93.82 tvaccintā tvatsvarūpaṃ kuruta iti dṛḍhaṃ śikṣeye peśakārāt |
11.93.83 viḍbhasmātmā ca dehi bhavati guruvaro yo vivekaṃ viraktiṃ
11.93.84 dhatte sañcintyamāno mama tu bahurujāpīḍito'yaṃ viśeṣāt ||
11.93.91 hī hī me dehamohaṃ tyaja pavanapurādhīśa! yatpremahetor
11.93.92 gehe citte kalatrādiṣu ca vivāśitāstvatpadaṃ vismaranti |
11.93.93 so'yaṃ vahneḥ śuno vā paramiha parataḥ sāmprataḥ cākṣikarṇa
11.93.94 tvagjihvādyā vikarṣantyavaśamata itaḥ ko'pi na tvatpadābje ||
11.93.101 durvāro dehamoho yadi punaradhunā tarhi niśśeṣarogān
11.93.102 hṛtvā bhaktiṃ dradhiṣṭhāṃ kuru tava padapaṅkeruhe paṅkajākṣa! |
11.93.103 nūnaḥ nānābhavānte samadhigatamimaṃ muktidaṃ vipradehaṃ
11.93.104 kṣudre hā hanta mā mā kṣipa viṣayarase pāhi māṃ māruteśa! ||
11.94.11 nānātvasthaulyakārśyādi tu guṇajavapussaṅgato'dhyāsitaṃ te
11.94.12 vahnerdāruprabhedeṣviva mahadaṇutādīptatāśāntatādi ||
11.94.21 ācāryākhyādharasthāraṇisamanumilacchiṣyarūpottarāre
11.94.22 ṇyāvedhodbhāsitena sphuṭataraparibodhāgninā dahyamāne |
11.94.23 karmālīvāsanātatkṛtatanubhuvanabhrāntikāntārapūre
11.94.24 dāhyābhāvane vidyāśikhini ca virate tvanmayī khalvavasthā ||
11.94.31 evaṃ tvatprāptito'nyo nahi khalu nikhilakleśahānerupāyo
11.94.32 naikāntātyantikāste kṛṣivadagadaṣāḍguṇyaṣaṭkarmayogāḥ |
11.94.33 durvaikalyairakalyā api nigamapathāstatphalānyapyavāptā
11.94.34 mattāstvāṃ vismarantaḥ prasajati patane yāntyanantān viṣādān ||
11.94.41 tvallokādanyalokaḥ kva nu bhayarahito yatparārdhadvayānte
11.94.42 tvadbhītaḥ sapyaloke'pi na sukhavasatiḥ padmabhūḥ padmanābhaḥ! |
11.94.43 evambhāve tvadharmārjitabahutamasāṃ kā kathā nārakāṇāṃ
11.94.44 tanme tvaṃ chindhi bandhaṃ varada! kṛpaṇabandho! kṛpāpūrasindho! ||
11.94.51 yāthārthyāttvanmasyaiva hi mama na vibho! vastuto bandhamokṣau
11.94.52 māyāvidyātanubhyāṃ tava tu viracitau svapnabodhopamau tau |
11.94.53 baddhe jīvadvimuktiṃ gatavati ca bhidā tāvatī tāvedeko
11.94.54 bhuṅkte dehadrumastho viṣayaphalarasānnāparo nirvyathātmā ||
11.94.61 jīvanmuktatvamevaṃvidhamiti vacasā kiṃ phalaṃ dūradūre
11.94.62 tannāmāśuddhabuddherna ca laghu manasaḥ śodhanaṃ bhaktito'nyat |
11.94.63 tanme viṣṇo! kṛṣīṣṭhāstvayi kṛtasakalaprārpaṇaṃ bhaktibhāraṃ
11.94.64 yena syāṃ maṅkṣu kiñcidguruvacanamilattvatprabodhastvadātmā ||
11.94.71 śabdabrahmaṇyapīha prayatitamanasastvāṃ na jānanti kecit
11.94.72 kaṣṭaṃ vandhyaśramāste cirataramiha gāṃ bibhrate niṣprasūtim |
11.94.73 yasyāḥ viśvābhirāmāḥ sakalamalaaharā divyalīlāvatārāḥ
11.94.74 saccitsāndraṃ ca rūpaṃ tava na nigaditaṃ tāṃ na vācaṃ bhriyāsam ||
11.94.81 yo yāvān yādṛśo vā tvamiti kimapi naivāvagacchāmi bhūma
11.94.82 nnevañcānanyabhāvastvadanubhajanamevādriye caidyavairin! |
11.94.83 tvalliṅgānāṃ tvadaṅghripriyajanasadasāṃ darśanasparśanādir
11.94.84 bhayānme tvatprapūjānatinutiguṇakarmānukīrtyādaro'pi ||
11.94.91 yadyallabhyeta tattattava samupahṛtaṃ deva! dāso'smi te'haṃ
11.94.92 tvadgehonmārjanādyaṃ bhavatu mama muhuḥ karma nirmāyameva |
11.94.93 sūryāgnibrāhmaṇātmādisu lasitacaturbāhumārādhaye tvāṃ
11.94.94 tvatpremārdratvarūpo mama satatamabhiṣyandatāṃ bhaktiyogaḥ ||
11.94.101 ekyaṃ te dānohimavrataniyamatapassāṅkhyayogairdurāpaṃ
11.94.102 tvatsaṅgenaiva gopyaḥ kila sukṛtitamāḥ prāpurānandasāndram |
11.94.103 bhakteṣvanyeṣu bhūyassvapi bahumanuṣe bhaktimeva tvamāsāṃ
11.94.104 tanme tvadbhaktimeva draḍhaya hara gadān kṛṣṇa! vātālayeśa! ||
11.95.11 ādau hairaṇyagabhīṃ tanumavikalajīvātmikāmāsthitastvaṃ
11.95.12 jīvatvaṃ prāpya māyāguṇagaṇakhacito vartase viśvayone! |
11.95.13 tatrodvṛddhena sattvena tu gaṇayugalaṃ bhaktibhāvaṃ gatena
11.95.14 cchitvā sattvaṃ ca hitvā punaranupahito vartitāhe tvameva ||
11.95.21 sattvonmeṣātkadācitkhalu viṣayarase doṣabodhe'pi bhūman!
11.95.22 bhūyo'pyeṣu pravṛttiḥ satamasi rajasi proddhate durnivārā |
11.95.23 cittaṃ tāvadguṇāśca grathitamiha mithastāni sarvāṇi roddhuṃ
11.95.24 turye tvayyekabhaktiḥ śaraṇamiti bhavān haṃsārūpī nyagādīt ||
11.95.31 santi śreyāṃsi bhūyāṃsyapi rucibhidayā karmiṇāṃ nirmitāni
11.95.32 kṣudrānandāśca sāntā bahuvidhagatayaḥ kṛṣṇa! tebhyo bhaveyuḥ |
11.95.33 tvaṃ cācakhyātha sakhye nanu mahitatamāṃ śreyasāṃ bhaktimekāṃ
11.95.34 tvadbhaktyānandatulyaḥ khalu viṣayajuṣāṃ sammadaḥ kena vā syāt ||
11.95.41 tvadbhaktyā tuṣṭabuddheḥ sukhamiha carato vicyutāśasya cāśāḥ
11.95.42 sarvāḥ syuḥ saukhyamayyaḥ salilakuharagasyeva toyaikamayyaḥ |
11.95.43 so'yaṃ khalvindralokaṃ kamalajabhavanaṃ yogasiddhīśca hṛdyā
11.95.44 nākāṅkṣatyetadāstāṃ svayamanupatite mokṣasaukhye'pyanīhaḥ ||
11.95.51 tvadbhakto bādhyamāno'pi ca viṣayarasairindriyāśāntihetor
11.95.52 bhaktyaivākramyamāṇaiḥ punarapi khalu tairdurbalairnābhijayyaḥ |
11.95.53 saptārcirdīpitārcirdahati kila yathā bhūridāruprapañcaṃ
11.95.54 tvadbhaktyoghe tathaiva pradahati duritaṃ durmadaḥ kvendriyāṇām ||
11.95.61 cittārdrībhāvavamuccairvapuṣi ca pulakaṃ harṣabāṣyaṃ ca hitvā
11.95.62 cittaṃ śudhyetkathaṃ vā kimu bahutapasā vidyayā vītabhakteḥ |
11.95.63 tvadgāthāsvādasiddhāñjanasatatamarīmṛjyamāno'yamātmā
11.95.64 cakṣurvattattvasūkṣmaṃ bhajati na tu tathābhyastayā tarkakoṭyā ||
11.95.71 dhyānaṃ te śīlayeyaṃ samatanusukhabaddhāsano nāsikāgra
11.95.72 nyastākṣaḥ pūrakādyairjitapavanapathaścittapadmaṃ tvavāñcam |
11.95.73 ūrdhvāgraṃ bhavayitvā ravividhuśikhinaḥ saṃvicintyopariṣṭāt
11.95.74 tatrasthaṃ bhāvaye tvāṃ sajalajaladharaśyāmalaṃ komalāṅgam ||
11.95.81 ānīlaślakṣṇakeśaṃ jvalitamakarasatkuṇḍalaṃ mandahāsa
11.95.82 syandārdraṃ kaustubhaśrīparigatavanamāloruhārābhirāmam |
11.95.83 śrīvatsāṅkaṃ subāhuṃ mṛdulasadudaraṃ kāñcanacchāyacelaṃ
11.95.84 cārusnigdhorumambhoruhalalitapadaṃ bhāvayeyaṃ bhavantam ||
11.95.91 sarvāṅgeṣvaṅga! raṅgatkutukamatimuhurdhārayannīśa! cittaṃ
11.95.92 tatrāpyekatra yuñje vadanasarasije sundare mandahāse |
11.95.93 tatrālīnaṃ tu cetaḥ paramasukhacidadvaitarūpe vitanva
11.95.94 nnanyanno cintayeyaṃ muhuriti samupārūḍhayogo bhaveyam ||
11.95.101 itthaṃ tvaddhyānayoge sati punaraṇimādyaṣṭasaṃsiddhayastā
11.95.102 dūraśrutyādayo'pi hyahamahamikayā sampateyurmurāre! |
11.95.103 tvatsamprāptau vilambāvahamakhimidaṃ nādriye kāmaye'haṃ
11.95.104 tvāmevānandapūrṇaṃ pavanapurapate! pāhi māṃ sarvatāpāt ||
11.96.11 tvaṃ hi brahmaiva sākṣātparamurumahimannakṣarāṇāmakāra
11.96.12 stāro mantreṣu rājñāṃ manurasi muniṣu tvaṃ bhṛgurnārado'pi |
11.96.13 prahlādo dānavānāṃ paśuṣu ca surabhiḥ pakṣiṇāṃ vainateyo
11.96.14 nāgānāmasyanantaḥ surasaridapi ca srotasāṃ viśvamūrte! ||
11.96.21 brahmaṇyānāṃ balistvaṃ kratuṣu ca japayajño'so vīreṣu pārtho
11.96.22 bhaktānāmuddhavastvaṃ balamasi balināṃ dhāma tejasvināṃ tvam |
11.96.23 nāstyantastvadvibhūtervikasadatiśayaṃ vastu sarvaṃ tvameva
11.96.24 tvaṃ jīvastvaṃ pradhānaṃ yadiha bhavadṛte tanna kiñcitprapañce ||
11.96.31 dharmaṃ varṇāśramāṇāṃ śrutipathavihitaṃ tvatparatvena bhaktyā
11.96.32 kurvanto'ntarvirāge vikasati śanakaiḥ santyajanto labhante |
11.96.33 sattāsphūrtipriyatvātmakamakhilapadārtheṣu bhinneṣvabhinnaṃ
11.96.34 nirmūlaṃ viśvamūlaṃ paramamahamiti tvadvibodhaṃ viśuddham ||
11.96.41 jñānaṃ karmāpi bhaktistritayamiha bhavatprāpakaṃ tatra tāvad
11.96.42 nirviṇṇānāmaśeṣe viṣaya iha bhavedjñānayoge'dhikāraḥ |
11.96.43 saktānāṃ karmayogastvayi hi vinihito ye tu nātyantasaktā
11.96.44 nāpyatyantaṃ viraktāstvayi ca dhṛtarasā bhaktiyogo hyamīṣām ||
11.96.51 jñānaṃ tvadbhaktatāṃ vā laghu sukṛtavaśānmartyaloke labhante
11.96.52 tasmāttatraiva janma spṛhayati bhagavan! nākago nārako vā |
11.96.53 āviṣṭaṃ māṃ tu daivādbhavajalanidhipotāyite martyadehe
11.96.54 tvaṃ kṛtvā karṇadhāraṃ gurumanuguṇavātāyitastārayethāḥ ||
11.96.61 avyaktaṃ mārgayantaḥ śrutibhirapi nayaiḥ kevalajñānalubdhāḥ
11.96.62 kliśyante'tīva siddhiṃ bahutarajanuṣāmanta evāpnuvanti |
11.96.63 durasthaḥ karmayogo'pi ca paramaphale nanvayaṃ bhaktiyoga
11.96.64 stvāmūlādeva hṛdyastvaritamayi! bhavatprāpako vardhatāṃ me ||
11.96.71 jñānāyaivātiyatnaṃ munirapavadate brahmatattvaṃ tu śṛṇvan
11.96.72 gāḍhaṃ tvatpādabhaktiṃ śaraṇamayati yastasya muktiḥ karāgre |
11.96.73 tvaddhyāne'pīha tulyā punarasukaratā cittacāñcalyaheto
11.96.74 rabhyāsādāśu śakyaṃ vaśayituṃ tvatkṛpācārutābhyām ||
11.96.81 nirviṇṇaḥ karmamārge khalu viṣamatame tvatkathādau ca gāḍhaṃ
11.96.82 jātaśraddho'pi kāmānayi bhuvanapate! naiva śaknomi hātum |
11.96.83 tadbhūyo niścayena tvayi nihitamanā doṣabuddhyā bhajaṃstān
11.96.84 puṣṇīyāṃ bhaktimeva tvayi hṛdayagate maṅkṣu naṅkṣyanti saṅgāḥ ||
11.96.91 kaścitkleśārjitārthakṣayavimalamatirnudyamāno janaudhaiḥ
11.96.92 prāgevaṃ prāhi vipro na khalu mama janaḥ kālakarmagrahā vā |
11.96.93 ceto me duḥkhahetustadiha guṇagaṇaṃ bhāvayatsarvakārī
11.96.94 tyuktvā śānto gatastvāṃ mama ca kuru vibho! tādṛśīṃ cittaśāntim ||
11.96.101 elaḥ prāgurvaśīṃ pratyativivaśamanāḥ sevamānaściraṃ tāṃ
11.96.102 gāḍhaṃ nirvidya bhūyo yuvatisukhamidaṃ kṣudrameveti gāyan |
11.96.103 tvadbhaktiṃ prāpya pūrṇaḥ sukhataramacarattadvaduddhūya saṅgaṃ
11.96.104 bhaktottaṃsaṃ kriyā māṃ pavanapurapate! hanta me rundhirogān ||
11.97.11 traiguṇyādbhinnarūpaṃ bhavati hi bhuvane hīnamadhyottamaṃ ya
11.97.12 jñānaṃ śraddhā ca kartā vasatirapi sukhaṃ karma cāhārabhedāḥ |
11.97.13 tvatkṣetratvanniṣevādi tu yadiha punastvatparaṃ tattu sarvaṃ
11.97.14 prāhurnairguṇyaniṣṭhaṃ tadanubhajanato maṅkṣu siddho bhaveyam ||
11.97.21 tvayyeva nyastacittaḥ sukhamayi vicaran sarvaceṣṭāstvadarthaṃ
11.97.22 tvadbhaktaiḥ sevyamānānapi caritacarānāśrayan puṃṇyadeśān |
11.97.23 dasyau vipre gṛhādiṣvapi ca samamatirmucyamānāvamāna
11.97.24 spardhāsūyādidoṣaḥ satatamakhilabhūteṣu sampūjaye tvām ||
11.97.31 tvadbhāvo yāvadeṣu sphurati na viśadaṃ tāvadevaṃ hyupāstiṃ
11.97.32 kurvannaikātmyabodhe jhaṭiti vikasati tvanmayo'haṃ careyam |
11.97.33 tvaddharmasyāsya tāvatkimapi na bhagavan! prastutasya praṇāśa
11.97.34 stasmātsarvātmanaiva pradiśa mama vibho! bhaktimārgaṃ manojham ||
11.97.41 taṃ cainaṃ bhaktiyogaṃ draḍhayitumayi! me sādhyamārogyamāyur
11.97.42 diṣṭyā tatrāpi sevyaṃ tava caraṇamaho bheṣajāyeva dugdham |
11.97.43 mārkaṇḍeyo hi pūrvaṃ gaṇakanigaditadvādaśābdāyuruccaiḥ
11.97.44 sevitvā vatsaraṃ tvāṃ tava bhaṭanivahairdrāvayāmāsa mṛtyum ||

12.97.51 mārkaṇḍeyaścirāyuḥ sa khalu punarapi tvatparaḥ puṣpabhadrā
12.97.52 tīre ninye tapasyannatulasukharatiḥ ṣaṭtu manvantarāṇi |
12.97.53 devendraḥ saptamastaṃ surayuvatimarunmanmathairmohayiṣyan
12.97.54 yogoṣmapluṣyamāṇairna tu punaraśakattvajjanaṃ nirjayetkaḥ ||
12.97.61 prītyā nārāyaṇākhyastvamatha narasakhaḥ prāptavānasya pārśvaṃ
12.97.62 tuṣṭyā toṣṭūyamānaḥ sa tu vividhavarairlobhito nānumene |
12.97.63 draṣṭuṃ māyāṃ tvadīyāṃ kila punaravṛṇodbhaktitṛptāntarātmā
12.97.64 māyāduḥkhānabhijñastadapi mṛgayate nūnamāścaryahetoḥ ||
12.97.71 yāte tvayyāśu vātākulajaladagalattotapūrṇātighūrṇa
12.97.72 tsaptārṇorāśimagne jagati sa tu jale sambhraman varṣakoṭīḥ |
12.97.73 dīnaḥ praikṣiṣṭa dūre vaṭadalaśayanaṃ kañcidāścaryabālaṃ
12.97.74 tvāmeva śyāmalāṅgaṃ vadanasarasijanyastapādāṅgulīkam ||
12.97.81 dṛṣṭvā tvāṃ hṛṣṭaromā tvaritamabhigataḥ spraṣṭukāmo munīndraḥ
12.97.82 śvāsenāntarniviṣṭaḥ punariha sakalaṃ dṛṣṭavān viṣṭapaugham |
12.97.83 bhūyo'pi śvāsavātairbahiranupatito vīkṣitastvatkaṭākṣair
12.97.84 modādāśleṣṭukāmastvayi pihitatanau svāśrame prāgvadāsīt ||
12.97.91 gauryā sārdhaṃ tadagre purabhidatha gatastvatpriyaprekṣaṇārthī
12.97.92 siddhānevāsya dattvā svayamayamajarāmṛtyutādīn gato'bhūt |
12.97.93 evaṃ tvatsevayaiva smararipurapi sa prīyate yena tasmā
12.97.94 nmūrtitrayyātmakastvaṃ nanu sakalaniyanteti suvyaktamāsīt ||
12.97.101 tryaṃśe'smin satyaloke vidhiharipurabhinmandirāṇyūrdhvamūrdhvaṃ
12.97.102 tebhyo'pyūrdhvaṃ tu māyāvikṛtivirahito bhāti vaikuṇṭhalokaḥ |
12.97.103 tatra tvaṃ kāraṇāmbhasyapi paśupakule śuddhasattvaikarūpī
12.97.104 saccidbrahmādvayātmā pavanapurapate! pāhi māṃ sarvarogāt ||
12.98.11 yasminnetadvibhātaṃ yata idamabhavadyena cedaṃ ya etad
12.98.12 yo'smāduttīrṇarūpaḥ khalu sakalamidaṃ bhāsitaṃ yasya bhāsā |
12.98.13 yo vācāṃ dūradūre punarapi manasāṃ yasya devā nunīndrā
12.98.14 no vidyustattvarūpaṃ kimu punarapare kṛṣṇa! tasmai namaste ||
12.98.21 janmātho karma nāma sphuṭamiha guṇadoṣādikaṃ vā na yasmin
12.98.22 lokānāmūteya yaḥ svayamanubhajate tāni māyānusārī |
12.98.23 bibracchaktīrarūpo'pi ca bahutararūpo'vabhātyaddhutātmā
12.98.24 tasmai kaivalyadhāmne pararasaparipūrṇāya viṣṇo! namaste ||
12.98.31 no tiryañcaṃ na martyaṃ na ca suramasuraṃ na striyaṃ no pumāṃsaṃ
12.98.32 na dravyaṃ karma jātiṃ guṇamapi sadasadvāpi te rūpamāhuḥ |
12.98.33 śiṣṭaṃ yatsyānniṣedhe sati nigamaśatairlakṣaṇāvṛttitastat
12.98.34 kṛccheṇāvedyamānaṃ paramasukhamayaṃ bhāti tasmai namaste ||
12.98.41 māyāyāṃ bimbitastvaṃ sṛjasi mahadahaṅkāratanmātrabhedair
12.98.42 bhūtagrāmendriyādyairapi sakalajagatsvapnasaṅkalpakalpam |
12.98.43 bhūyaḥ saṃhṛtya sarvaṃ kamaṭha iva padānyātmanā kālaśaktyā
12.98.44 gambhīre jāyamāne tamasi vitimiro bhāsi tasmai namaste ||
12.98.51 śabdabrahmeti karmetyaṇuriti bhagavan! kāla ityālapanti
12.98.52 tvāmekaṃ viśvahetuṃ sakalamayatayā sarvathā kalpyamānam |
12.98.53 vedāntairyattu gītaṃ puruṣaparacidātmābhidhaṃ tattu tattvaṃ
12.98.54 prekṣāmātreṇa mūlaprakṛtivikṛtikṛtkṛṣṇa! tasmai namaste ||
12.98.61 sattvenāsattayā vā na ca khalu sadasattvena nirvācyarūpā
12.98.62 dhatte yāsāvavidyā guṇaphaṇimativadviśvadṛśyāvabhāsam |
12.98.63 vidyātvaṃ saiva yātā śrutivacanalavairyatkṛpāsyandalābhe
12.98.64 saṃsārāraṇyasadyastruṭanaparaśutāmeti tasmai namaste ||
12.98.71 bhūṣāsu svarṇavadvā jagati ghaṭaśarāvādike mṛttikāvat
12.98.72 tattve sañcintyamāne sphurati tadadhunāpyadvitīyaṃ vapuste |
12.98.73 svapnadraṣṭuḥ prabodhe timiralayavidhau jīrṇarajjośca yadvad
12.98.74 vidyālābhe tathaiva sphuṭamapi vikasetkṛṣṇa! tasmai namaste ||
12.98.81 yadbhītyodeti sūryo dahati ca dahano vāti vāyustathānye
12.98.82 yadbhītāḥ padmajādyāḥ punarucitabalīnāharante'nukālam |
12.98.83 yenaivāropitāḥ prāṅnijapadamapi te cyāvitāraśca paścāt
12.98.84 tasmai viśvaṃ niyantre vayamapi bhavate kṛṣṇa! kurmaḥ praṇāmam ||
12.98.91 trailokyaṃ bhāvayantaṃ triguṇamayamidaṃ tryakṣarasyaikavācyaṃ
12.98.92 trīśānāmaikyarūpaṃ tribhirapi nigamairgīyamānasvarūpam |
12.98.93 tisro'vasthā vidantaṃ triyugajanijuṣaṃ trikramakrāntaviśvaṃ
12.98.94 traikālye bhedahīnaṃ tribhirahamaniśaṃ yogabhedairbhaje tvām ||
12.98.101 satyaṃ śuddhaṃ vibuddhaṃ jayati tava vapurnityamuktaṃ nirīhaṃ
12.98.102 nirdvandvaṃ nirvikāraṃ nikhilaguṇagaṇavyañjanādhārabhūtam |
12.98.103 nirmūlaṃ nirmalaṃ tanniravadhimahimollāsi nirlīnamantar
12.98.104 nissaṅgānāṃ munīnāṃ nirupamaparamānandasāndraprakāśam ||
12.98.111 durvāraṃ dvādaśāraṃ triśataparimilatṣaṣṭiparvābhivītaṃ
12.98.112 saṃbhṛāmyatkrūravegaṃ kṣaṇamanu jagadācchidya sandhāvamānam |
12.98.113 cakraṃ te kālarūpaṃ vyathayatu na tu māṃ tvatpadaikāvalambaṃ
12.98.114 viṣṇo! kāruṇyasindho! pavanapurapate! pāhi sarvāmayaughāt ||
12.99.11 viṣṇorvīryāṇi ko vā kathayatu dharaṇeḥ kaśca reṇūnmimīte
12.99.12 yasyaivāṅghritrayeṇa trijagadabhimitaṃ modate pūrṇasampat |
12.99.13 yo'sau viśvāni dhatte priyamiha paramaṃ dhāma tasyābhiyāyāṃ
12.99.14 tadbhaktā yatra mādyantyamṛtarasamarandasya yatra pravāhaḥ ||
12.99.21 ādyāyāśeṣakartre pratinimiṣanavīnāya bhartre vibhūter
12.99.22 bhaktātmā viṣṇave yaḥ prādiśati havirādīni yajñārcanādau |
12.99.23 kṛṣṇādyaṃ janma vā mahadiha mahato varṇayetso'yameva
12.99.24 prītaḥ pūrṇo yaśobhistvaritamabhisaretprāpyamante padaṃ tat ||
12.99.31 he stotāraḥ! kavīndrāstamiha khalu yathā cetayadhve tathaiva
12.99.32 vyaktaṃ vedasya sāraṃ praṇuvata jananopāttalīlākathābhiḥ |
12.99.33 jānantaścāsya nāmānyakhilasukhakarāṇīti saṅkīrtayadhvaṃ
12.99.34 he viṣṇo! kīrtanādyaistava khalu mahatastattvabodhaṃ bhajeyam ||
12.99.41 viṣṇoḥ karmāṇi sampaśyata manasi sadā yaiḥ sa dharmānabadhnād
12.99.42 yānīndrasyaiṣa bhṛtyaḥ priyasakha iva ca vyātanotkṣemakārī |
12.99.43 vīkṣante yogasiddhāḥ parapadamaniśaṃ pasya samyakprakāśaṃ
12.99.44 viprendrā jāgarūkāḥ kṛtabahunutayo yacca nirbhāsayante ||
12.99.51 no jāto jāyamāno'pi ca samadhigatastvanmahimno'vasānaṃ
12.99.52 deva! śreyāṃsi vidvān pratimuhurapi te nāma śaṃsāmi viṣṇo! |
12.99.53 taṃ tvāṃ saṃstaumi nānāvidhanutivacanairasya lokatrayasyā
12.99.54 pyūrdhvaṃ vibhrājamāne viracitavasatiṃ tatra vaikuṇṭhaloke ||
12.99.61 āpaḥ sṛṣṭyādijanyāḥ prathamamayi vibho! garbhadeśe dadhustvāṃ
12.99.62 yatra tvayyeva jīvā jalaśayana! hare! saṅgatā ekyamāpan |
12.99.63 tasyājasya prabho! te vinihitamabhavatpadmamekaṃ hi nābhau
12.99.64 dikpatraṃ yatkilāhuḥ kanakadharaṇibhṛtkarṇikaṃ lokarūpam ||
12.99.71 he lokā viṣṇuretadbhavanamajanayattanna jānītha yūyaṃ
12.99.72 yuṣmākaṃ hyantarasthaṃ kimapi tadaparaṃ vidyate viṣṇurūpam |
12.99.73 nīhāraprakhyamāyāparivṛtamanaso mohitā nāmarūpaiḥ
12.99.74 prāṇaprītyaikatṛptāścaratha makhaparā hanta necchā mukunde ||
12.99.81 mūrdhnāmakṣaṇāṃ padānāṃ vahasi khalu sahasrāṇi sampūrya viśvaṃ
12.99.82 tatprotkramyāpi tiṣṭhan parimitavivare bhāsi cittāntare'pi |
12.99.83 bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ ca sarvaṃ parapuruṣa! bhavān kiñca dehendriyādi
12.99.84 ṣvāviṣṭo hyudgatatvādamṛtasukharasaṃ cānubhuṅkṣe tvameva ||
12.99.91 yattu trailokyarūpaṃ dadhadapi ca tatonirgatānantaśuddha
12.99.92 jñānātmā vartase tvaṃ tava khalu mahimā so'pi tāvān kimanyat |
12.99.93 stokaste bhāga evākhilabhuvanatayā dṛśyate tryaṃśakalpaṃ
12.99.94 bhūyiṣṭhaṃ sāndramodātmakamupari tato bhāti tasmai namaste ||
12.99.101 avyaktaṃ te svarūpaṃ duradhigamatamaṃ tattu śuddhaikasattvaṃ
12.99.102 vyaktaṃ cāpyetadeva sphuṭamamṛtarasāmbhodhikallolatulyam |
12.99.103 sarvotkṛṣṭāmabhīṣṭāṃ tadiha guṇaramenaiva cittaṃ harantīṃ
12.99.104 mūrtiṃ te saṃśraye'haṃ pavanapurapate! pāhi māṃ kṛṣṇa! rogāt ||
12.100.11 agre paśyāmi tejo nibiḍatarakalāyāvalīlobhanīyaṃ
12.100.12 pīyūṣāplāvito'haṃ tadanu tadudare divyakaiśoraveṣam |
12.100.13 tāruṇyārambharamyaṃ paramasukharasāsvādaromāñcitāṅgai
12.100.14 rāvītaṃ nāradādyaivilasadupaniṣatsundarīmaṇḍalaiśca ||
12.100.21 nīlābhaṃ kuñcitāgraṃ ghanamamalataraṃ saṃyataṃ cārubhaṅgyā
12.100.22 ratnottaṃsābhirāmaṃ valayitamudayaccandrakaiḥ piñchajālaiḥ |
12.100.23 mandārasraṅnivītaṃ tava pṛthukabarībhāramālokaye'haṃ
12.100.24 snigdhaścetordhvapuṇḍrāmapi ca sulalitāṃ phālabālenduvīthīm ||
12.100.31 hṛdyaṃ pūrṇānukampārṇavamṛdulaharīcañcalabhrūvilāsai
12.100.32 rānīlasnigdhapakṣmāvaliparilasitaṃ netrayugmaṃ vibho! te |
12.100.33 sāndracchāyaṃ viśālāruṇakamaladalākāramāmugdhatāraṃ
12.100.34 kāruṇyālokalīlāśiśiritabhuvanaṃ kṣipyatāṃ mayyanāthe ||
12.100.41 uttuṅgollāsināsaṃ harimaṇimukuraprollasadgaṇḍapālī
12.100.42 vyālolatykarṇapāśāñcitamakaramaṇīkuṇḍaladvandvadīpram |
12.100.43 unmīladdantapaṅktisphuradaruṇataracchāyabimbādharāntaḥ
12.100.44 prītiprasyandimandasmitaśiśirataraṃ vaktramudbhāsatāṃ me ||
12.100.51 bāhudvandvena ratnojjvalavalayabhṛtā śoṇapāṇipravāle
12.100.52 nopāttāṃ veṇunālīṃ prasṛtanakhamayūkhāṅgulīsaṅgaśārām |
12.100.53 kṛtvā vaktrāravindre sumadhuravikasadrāgamudbhāvyamānaiḥ
12.100.54 śabdabrahmāmṛtaistvaṃ śiśiritabhuvanaiḥ siñca me karṇavīthīm ||
12.100.61 utsarpatkaustubhaśrūtatibhiraruṇitaṃ komalaṃ kaṇṭhadeśaṃ
12.100.62 vakṣaḥ śrīvatsaramyaṃ taralatarasamuddīprahārapratānam |
12.100.63 nānāvarṇaprasūnāvalikisalayinīṃ vanyamālāṃ vilola
12.100.64 llolambāṃ lambamānāmurasi tava tathā bhāvaye ratnamālām ||
12.100.71 aṅge pañcāṅkarāgairatiśayavikasatsaurabhākṛṣṭalokaṃ
12.100.72 līnānekatrilokīvitatimapi kṛśāṃ bibhrataṃ madhyavallīm |
12.100.73 śakrāśmanyastataptojjvalakanakanibhaṃ pītacelaṃ dadhānaṃ
12.100.74 dhyāyāmi dīptaraśmisphuṭamaṇiraśanākiṅgiṇīmaṇḍitaṃ tvām ||
12.100.81 ūrū cārū tavorū ghanamasṛṇarucau cittacorau ramāyā
12.100.82 viśvakṣobhaṃ viśaṅkya dhruvamaniśamubhau pītacelāvṛtāṅgau |
12.100.83 ānamrāṇāṃ purastānnyasanadhṛtasamastārthapālīsamudga
12.100.84 cchāyāṃ jānudviyaṃ ca kramapṛthulamanojñe ca jaṅghe niṣeve ||
12.100.91 mañjīraṃ mañjunādairiva padabhajanaṃ śreya ityālapantaṃ
12.100.92 pādāgraṃ bhrāntimajjatpraṇatajanamanomandaroddhārakūrmam |
12.100.93 uttuṅgātāmrarājannakharahimakarajyotsnayā cāśritānāṃ
12.100.94 santāpadhvāntahattrīṃ tatimanukalaye maṅgalāmaṅgulīnām ||
12.100.101 yogīndrāṇāṃ tvadaṅgeṣvadhikasumadhuraṃ muktibhājāṃ nivāso
12.100.102 bhaktānāṃ kāmavarṣadyutarukisalayaṃ nātha! te pādamūlam |
12.100.103 nityaṃ cittasthitaṃ me pavanapurapate! kṛṣṇa! kāruṇyasindho!
12.100.104 hṛtvā niḥśeṣatāpān pradiśatu paramānandasandohalakṣmīm ||
12.100.111 ajñātvā te mahattvaṃ yadiha nigaditaṃ viśvanātha! kṣamethāḥ
12.100.112 stotraṃ caitatsahasrottaramadhikataraṃ tvatprsādāya bhūyāt |
12.100.113 dvedhā nārāyaṇīyaṃ śrutiṣu ca januṣā stutyatāvarṇanena
12.100.114 sphītaṃ līlāvatārairidamiha kurutāmāyurārogyasaukhyam
